<ṭitle page>


|| śrīḥ ||


<prāṇatoṣiṇī>

<śrīrāmatoṣaṇa bhaṭṭācāryeṇa>

<tantraśāstrāt saṃkalayya viracitā>

<Vi, e upādhidhāriṇā paṇḍitakulapatinā>

<śrījīvānandavidyāsāgarabhaṭṭācaryyeṇa prakāśitā |>

<Kalikātānagare>

<Kalikātāyantre mudritā>

<1898 āḍ>

[2 dharmakāṇḍa]


acintyāpi dhyeyā svakhilahṛdaye dhvāntarucirā manodhvānta gāḍhaṃ harasi 
karuṇāmbhonidhirapi | 

mahākālī svairiṇyasi sakalamūrttiḥ kadahamānadṛśyā lokānāṃ tvamasi 
jayatādadbhutatamaḥ || 

āsīdrāmaharīti pūruṣaharī rākendubhājitvarīrāśāpūrttikarīḥ suśītalaharīḥ kīrttiḥ 
samāsādayan | 

viśvāsānvayapāvano gurukṛpāpātraṃ kuloddipakam yo daivairjanayāmbabhūvakṛtinaṃ 
śrīprāṇakṛṣṇābhidham || 

kṛṣṇamaṅgalavidyāvāgīśasūnuḥ satāṃ mude | 

gaṅgādevīsuto'kārṣīddharmakāṇḍaṃ dvitīyakam || 

dvitīyasyocyate dharmakāṇḍasya saṃskriyātmanaḥ | 

nirghaṇṭaḥ prathamaṃ tatra saṃskārasya praśaṃsanam || 

dvijānāṃ daśa saṃskārāḥ karmamātrādhikārakāḥ | 

śūdraśūdraprakārāṇāṃ vijñātavyā navaiva te | 

māyādikatvaṃ mantrāṇāṃ vijñeyaṃ kalisammatam | 

ṛtukarma tato garbhādhānaṃ puṃsavanaṃ tataḥ | 

pañcāmṛtapradānañca sīmantonnayanaṃ tathā | 

jātakarma tato medhājananaṃ kavitārakam | 

māyādidānaṃ viprebhyo bālasya puṣṭikāraṇam | 

vijñeyaṃ nāmakaraṇaṃ tato niṣkramaṇaṃ smṛtam | 

annāśanaṃ tataścūḍākarṇavedhastataḥ param | 

kumārīśūdrajātīnāṃ sarvametadamantrakam | 

upanāyo vedabhede sūtramāṇaṃ prakīrttitam | 

sūtranirmāṇasaṃskārau sūtrādhiṣṭhātṛdevatā | 

sāvitrīgrahaṇaṃ karma samāvarttanamityapi | 

adārajananindā ca dharmapatnīnirūpaṇam | 

vivāho daśasaṃskāraprayogastadanantaram | 

<p. 142> iti dvitīyakāṇḍādyaparicchedasamāpanam || 





ādau gurorlakṣaṇañca tataḥ sadgurulakṣaṇam | 

kaulagurupraśaṃsā ca kūṭārthakṛtidūṣaṇam | 

guruviśeṣakathanaṃ śāstre tvāvaśyakaṃ guroḥ | 

gurumantradevatānāmekatvabhāvanākramaḥ | 

gurusantuṣṭyādiphalaṃ gurūccāraṇajaṃ phalam | 

gurutṛpte jagattṛptirādhikyaṃ sarvato guroḥ | 

jagatpūjāphalāvāptirgurupādāmbuje'rccite | 

dīkṣāśikṣādibhedena gurubhedanirūpaṇam | 

eka eva mahākālogururna mānuṣaḥ kvacit | 

tadadhiṣṭhānavaśato māhātmyaṃ mānuṣasya ca | 

guruvaṃśasya maryyādā paśumantrādibhedataḥ | 

parāparādibhedaśca guruṇāṃ tadanantaram | 

strīgurorlakṣaṇaṃ varjyaṃ gurutvakalpane tathā | 

tyājyo guruśca śiṣyasya lakṣaṇaṃ varjanaṃ tathā | 

varjyā śiṣyakartṛnindā gurvājñālaṅghane tathā | 

gurudravyādiharaṇadoṣāniṣṭakṛtestathā | 

guruśāpajanyadoṣastannindā śravaṇasya ca | 

tacchravaṇapratīkāro gurunindākarasya ca | 

doṣo gurau manuṣyatvajñānanindā tathaiva ca | 

gurudrohādidoṣaśca tyāge gurukulasya ca | 

niṣedho gurugotrasya mānanāpūjane tathā | 

gurudarśanakālasya pūjyatvaṃ gurupūjanam | 

vineṣṭapūjādoṣaśca gurupūjādijaṃ phalam | 

gurugehasthitakṛtyapuṇyasyākṣayatā tathā | 

gurupādapadmareṇūdakamāhātmyavarṇanam | 

gurvannabhakṣaṇaphalaṃ gurūcchiṣṭaphalaṃ tathā | 

taducchiṣṭābhojanajadoṣaśca tadanantaram | 

gurupādukādipraṇatirniṣiddhaṃ gurusannidhau | 

gurvājñayā niṣiddhasyācaraṇānnāsti pātakam | 

jātividyādigarvasya tyāgaḥ śrīgurusannidhau | 

sadā śrīgurupādābjadhyānasya phalanirṇayaḥ | 

guruṇaikāsanatyāgo gurubhaktiphalaṃ tathā | 

paitragurukulatyāgadoṣaḥ śrīpūrvakīrttanam | 

gurvāderarccanākāla evaṃ tannāmabhāṣaṇam | 

caturvidhā tu śuśrūṣā gurumitrādimānanā | 

gurvālaye praveśasya prakārastadanantaram | 

iti dvitīyakāṇḍasya paricchedo dvitīyakaḥ || 





<p. 143> nāḍīcakravicāraśca bhūparīkṣā tataḥ param | 

digviśeṣaplavā bhadrāphalapradāyinī | 

brahmaṇyādimahījñānaṃ śubhabhūminirūpaṇam | 

tyājyā bhūmyo hi cakrañca tasya vyākhyānameva ca | 

śalyoddhāraḥ sa dvividhaḥ śaṅkunā diṅnirūpaṇam | 

maṇḍapakaraṇaṃ tasmāttoraṇañca tataḥ param | 

aṅkurāropaṇaṃ tasya parīkṣā tadanantaram | 

aśubhāṅkuraśāntiśca paricchedatṛtīyake || 

dīkṣāśabdārthanirṇītirdīkṣāmāhātmyakīrttanam | 

adīkṣitasya nindā ca dīkṣitasya praśaṃsanam | 

dīkṣayā caiva jyeṣṭhatvaṃ dīkṣayā mokṣasādhanam | 

ekamantrasādhanena sarvamantrasya sādhanam | 

mantragrahaṇanindā ca pustakālokanāditaḥ | 

dīkṣāṃ vinā nādhikāraḥ pūjādau nindyadīkṣitaḥ | 

tataśca trividhā dīkṣā caturdhāpi prakīrttitā | 

adhivāsanidhiścāṅkurārpaṇādyadhivāsayoḥ | 

prayoga iti turyyākhya paricchedasamāpanam || 

gurornimantraṇaṃ pūrvaṃ snānāditantrakarmaṇi | 

sauramāsasamullekho mātṛkādiprapūjanam | 

vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ sarvakāryye pañcadevaprapūjanam | 

lakṣmyādipūjanañcaiva grahamantrastataḥ param | 

grahapūjādānavidhirgrahāṇāṃ kavacaṃ tataḥ | 

tithidhyānamantrapūjākavacaṃ tadanantaram | 

varṇadhyānādyāsanasya śuddhiścaiva tataḥ param | 

arccanāhomakarmāṇi samāsena tataḥ param | 

iti pañcamavicchedasamāptirabhidhīyate || 

dīkṣā nānāvidhā ṣaṣṭhe kīrtitānyad yathāsthalam | 

jñātavyamuddeśamātraṃ nirghaṇṭena kṛtaṃ mayā | 

vyākhyayā granthabāhulyadarśanādadhunā mayā | 

pramāṇamātraṃ likhitaṃ mīmāṃsāpi kvacit kvacit || 

idānīṃ prāptāvasareṇa garbhādhānādisaṃskārā ucyante | 

saṃskārāṇāṃ prayogasyāvaśyambhāvādagre prayogo lekhanīyo nāto vyākhyājātā 
pramāṇamātraṃ likhyate || 

mahānirvāṇatantra navamollāse || 

sadāśiva uvāca || 

saṃskāreṇa vinā devi (!) dehaśuddhirna jāyate | 

nāmaṃskṛto'dhikārī syāddaive paitrye ca karmaṇi | 

<p. 144> ato viprādibhirvarṇaiḥ svasvavarṇoktasaṃskriyā || 

karttavyā sarvathā yatnairihāmutra hitepsubhiḥ | 

vījasekaḥ puṃsavanaṃ sīmantonnayanaṃ tathā | 

jātanāmno niṣkramaṇamannāśanamataḥ param | 

cūḍopanayanodvāhāḥ saṃskārāḥ kathitā daśa | 

śūdrāṇāṃ śūdrabhinnānāmupavītaṃ na vidyate | 

śūdrabhinnānāṃ śūdraprakārāṇāmadhamaśūdrakaivarttādīnāmityarthaḥ | 

teṣāṃ navaiva saṃskārā dvijātīnāṃ daśa smṛtāḥ | 

nityāni sarvakārmāṇi tathā naimittikāni ca | 

kāmyānyapi varārohe (!) kuryyācchāmbhavavartmanā | 

saṃskāreṣu ca sarveṣu tathaivānyeṣu karmasu | 

viprādivarṇabhedena kramānmantrāśca darśitāḥ | 

satyatretādvāpareṣu tattatkarmasukālike (!) | 

praṇavādyāṃstu tān mantrān prayogeṣu niyojayet | 

kalau tu parameśāni (!) taireva manubhirnarāḥ |

māyādyaiḥ sarvakarmāṇi kuryyuḥ śaṅkaraśāsanāt | 

nigamāgamatantreṣu vedeṣu saṃhitāsu ca | 

sarve mantrā mayaivoktāḥ prayogā yugabhedataḥ | 

athocyate mahāmāye (!) garbhādhānādikā kriyā | 

tatrādāvṛtusaṃskāraḥ kathyate kramataḥ śṛṇu | 

kṛtanityakriyaḥ śuddhaḥ pañca devān samarcayet | 

brahmā durgā gaṇeśaśca grahā dikpatayastathā | 

sthaṇḍilendrādidigbhāge ghaṭeṣvetān prapūjayet | 

tatasu mātṛkāḥ pūjyā gauryyādyāṃ ṣoḍaśa kramāt | 

gaurī padmā śacī medhā sāvitrī vijayā jayā | 

devasenā svadhā svāhā śāntiḥ puṣṭirdhṛtiḥ kṣamā | 

ātmano devatā caiva tathaiva kuladevatā | 

āyāntu mātaraḥ sarvāstridaśānandakārikāḥ | 

vivāhavratayajñānāṃ sarvābhīṣṭaṃ prakalpyatām | 

yānaśaktisamārūḍhāḥ saumyamūrtidharāḥ sadā | 

āyāntu mātaraḥ sarvā yajñotsavasamṛddhaye | 

ityāvāhya mātṛgaṇān svaśaktyā paripūjya ca | 

dehalyāṃ nābhimātrāyāṃ prādeśaparimāṇataḥ | 

saptadhā pañca vā bindūn dadyāt sindūracandanaiḥ | 

pratyekavindau matimān kāmaṃ māyāṃ ramāṃ smaran | 

<p. 145> ghṛtadhārāmavicchinnāṃ dattvā tatra vasuṃ yajet | 

vasudhārāṃ prakalpyaivaṃ mayoktenaiva vartmanā | 

viracya sthaṇḍilaṃ dhīro vahnisthāpanapūrvakam | 

homadravyāṇi saṃskṛtya paceccarumanuttamam | 

prājāpatyaścaruścātra vāyunāmā hutāśanaḥ | 

samāpya dhārāhomāntaṃ kṛtyamārttavamārabhet | 

hrīṃ prajāpataye svāhā caruṇaivāhutitrayam | 

pradāyaivāhutiṃ dadyādimaṃ mantramudīrayan | 

viṣṇuryoniṃ kalpayatu tvaṣṭā rūpāṇi yacchatu || 

āsiñcatu prajāpatirdhātā garbhaṃ dadhātu te | 

svāhā ājyena caruṇāpi vā | 

sūryyaṃ prajāpatiṃ viṣṇuṃ dhyāyannāhutimutsṛjet | 

garbhaṃ dhehi sinīvālī garbhaṃ dhehi sarasvatī | 

garbhaṃ te aśvinau devāvādhattāṃ puṣkarasrajau | 

dhyātvā devīṃ sinīvālīṃ sarasvatyaśvinau tathā | 

svāhāntamanunānena dadyādāhutimuttamām | 

ataḥ kāmaṃ badhūṃ māyāṃ ramāṃ kūrccaṃ samuccaran | 

amuṣyai putrakāmāyai garbhamādhehi sadviṭham | 

uktvā dhyātvā raviṃ viṣṇuṃ juhuyāt saṃskṛte'nale | 

yatheyaṃ pṛthivī devī hyuttānā garbhamādadhe | 

tathā tvaṃ garbhamādhehi daśame māsi sūtaye | 

svāhāntenāmunā viṣṇuṃ dhyāyannāhutimācaret | 

punarājyaṃ samādāya dhyātvā viṣṇuṃ parātparam | 

viṣṇo (!) jyeṣṭhena rūpeṇa nāryyāmasyāṃ varīyasam | 

sutamādhehi ṭhadvandvamuktvā vahnau havistyajet | 

kāmena puṭitāṃ māyāṃ māyayā puṭitāṃ badhūm | 

punaḥ kāmañca māyāñca paṭhitvāsyāḥ śiraḥ spṛśet | 

patiputravatībhiśca nārībhiḥ pariveṣṭitaḥ | 

śira ālabhya hastābhyāṃ badhvāḥ krīḍāñcale patiḥ | 

viṣṇuṃ durgāṃ vidhiṃ sūryyaṃ dhyātvā dadyāt phalatrayam | 

tataḥ sviṣṭikṛtaṃ hutvā prāyaścittaṃ samāpayet | 

yadvā pradoṣasamaye gaurīśaṅkarapūjanāt | 

bhāskarārghyapradānācca dampatyoḥ śodhanaṃ bhavet |

ārttavaṃ kathitaṃ karmma garbhādhānamatho śṛṇu || 

tadrātrāvanyarātrau vā yugmāyāṃ niśi bhāryyayā | 

<p. 146> sadanābhyantaraṃ gatvā dhyātvā devaṃ prajāpatim | 

spṛśet patnīṃ paṭhedbharttā māyāvījapuraḥsaram | 

āvayoḥ suprajāyai tvaṃ śayyāśubhaṅkarī bhava | 

āruhya bhāryyayā śayyāṃ prāṅmukho vāpyudaṅmukhaḥ | 

upaviśya striyaṃ spṛśyan hastamādāya mastake | 

vāmena pāṇināliṅgya sthāne sthāne manuṃ japet | 

śīrṣe kāmaṃ śataṃ japtvā civuke vāgbhavaṃ śatam | 

kaṇṭhe ramāṃ viṃśatidhā stanadvandve śataṃ śatam | 

hṛdaye daśadhā māyāṃ nābhau tāṃ pañcaviṃśatim | 

japtvā yonau karaṃ dattvā kāmena saha vāgbhavam | 

śatamaṣṭottaraṃ japtvā liṅge'pyevaṃ samācaran | 

vikāmya māyayā yoniṃ striyaṃ gacchet sutāptaye | 

retaḥsampātasamaye dhyātvā viśvakṛtaṃ patiḥ | 

nābheradhastāt citkuṇḍe vastikāyāṃ prapātayet | 

śukrasekāntare vidvān imaṃ mantramudīrayet | 

yathāgninā sagarbhā bhūrdyauryathā vajradhāriṇā | 

vāyunā dig garbhavatī tathā garbhavatī bhava || 

iti garbhādhānam ||

jāte garbhe ṛtau dvām(?)sannayasmin vā maheśvari (!) | 

tṛtīye garbhamāse tu caret puṃsavanaṃ gṛhī | 

kṛtanityakriyo bharttā pañca devān samarccayet | 

gauryyādimātṛkāścaiva vasordhārāṃ prapātayet | 

vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā pūrvoktavidhinā sudhīḥ | 

dhārāhomāntamāpādya kuryyāt puṃsavanakriyām | 

prājāpatyaścarustatra candranāmā hutāśanaḥ | 

gavye dadhni yavañcaikaṃ dvau māṣāvapi nikṣipet | 

patiḥ pṛcchet striyaṃ bhadre (!) kiṃ tvaṃ pibasi triḥ kṛtam | 

tataḥ sīmantitī brūyānmayā puṃsavanaṃ tridhā | 

prasṛtīṃstrīn pibennārī yavamāṣayutaṃ dadhi | 

jīvatsutābhirvanitāṃ yāgasthānaṃ samānayet | 

saṃsthāpya vāmabhāge tāṃ caruhomaṃ samācaret | 

pūrvavaccarumādāya māyāṃ kūrccaṃ samuccaran | 

ye garbhavighnakarttāro ye ca garbhavināśakāḥ | 

bhūtāḥ pretāḥ piśācāśca vetālā bālaghātakāḥ | 

<p. 147> tān sarvān nāśaya dvandvaṃ garbharakṣāṃ kuru dviṭhaḥ | 

mantreṇānena rakṣoghnaṃ cintayitvā hutāśanam | 

rudraṃ prajāpatiṃ dhyāyan pradadyādvādaśāhutīḥ | 

tato māyācandramase svāhetyāhutipañcakam | 

dattvā bhāryyāṃ hṛdi spṛṣṭvā māyāṃ lakṣmīṃ śataṃ japet | 

tataḥ sviṣṭikṛtaṃ hutvā prāyaścittaṃ samāpayet || 

iti puṃsavanam ||

tatastu pañcame māsi dadyāt pañcāmṛtaṃ striyai | 

śarkarā madhu dugdhañca ghṛtaṃ dadhi samāṃśakam | 

pañcāmṛtamidaṃ proktaṃ dehaśuddhyai vidhīyate | 

vāgbhavaṃ madanaṃ lakṣmīṃ māyāṃ kūrccaṃ purandaram | 

pañcagavyopari śive (!) prajapya pañca pañcadhā | 

ekīkṛtyāmṛtānyatra prāśayeddayitāṃ patiḥ || 

iti pañcāmṛtadānam ||

sīmantonnayanaṃ kuryyānmāsi ṣaṣṭhe'ṣṭame'pi vā | 

yāvanna jāyate'patyaṃ tāvat sīmantanakriyā | 

pūrvoktadhārāhomāntaṃ karmma kṛtvā striyā saha | 

upaviśyāsane prājñaḥ pradadyādāhutitrayam | 

viṣṇave bhāskare dhātre vahnijāyāṃ samuccaran | 

tataścandramasaṃ dhyātvā śivanāmni hutāśane | 

saptadhā havanaṃ kuryyāt somamuddiśya mānavaḥ | 

aśvinau  vāsavaṃ viṣṇuṃ śivaṃ durgāṃ prajāpatim | 

dhyātvā pratyekato dadyādāhutīḥ pañcadhā śive (!) | 

svarṇakaṅkatikāṃ bharttā gṛhītvā dakṣiṇe kare | 

sīmantādbaddhakeśāntaḥkeśapāśe niveśayet | 

śivaṃ viṣṇuṃ vidhiṃ dhyāyan māyābījaṃ samuccaran | 

bhāryye (!) kalyāṇi (!) subhage (!) daśame māsi suvrate (!) | 

suprasūtā bhava prītā prasādādviśvakarmmaṇaḥ | 

āyuṣmati (!) kaṅkatikā varccasvī te śubhaṃ kuru | 

tataḥ samāpayet karmma sviṣṭikṛddhavanādibhiḥ || 

iti sīmantonnayanam ||

jātamātraṃ sutaṃ dṛṣṭvā dattvā svarṇaṃ gṛhāntare | 

pūrvoktavidhinā dhīro dhārāhomaṃ samāpayet | 

tataḥ pañcāhutīrdadyādagnimindraṃ prajāpatim | 

<p. 148> viśvān devāṃśca brahmāṇamuddiśya tadanantaram | 

madhusarpiḥ kāṃsyapātre samānīya samāṃśakam | 

vāgbhavaṃ saptadhā japtvā prāśayettanayaṃ pitā | 

dakṣahastānāmikayā mantramevaṃ samuccaran | 

āyurvarcco balaṃ medhā varddhatāṃ te sadā śiśo (!) | 

ityāyurjananaṃ kṛtvā guptaṃ nāma prakalpayet | 

gandharvatantre dvitīyapaṭale || 

bālakasya tu jihvāyāṃ tridinābhyantare nyaset | 

madhunā śvetadūrvābhiḥ suvarṇasya śalākayā | 

idaṃ vāgbhavakūṭantu likhedvai jananāntare | 

sa eva paṇḍito bhūyānna tu mūrkho bhaveddhruvam || 

vāgbhavakūṭamapi tatraiva yathā | 

kāmadevastato yonisturyyasvarapurandarī | 

bhuvaneśī tataḥ paścāt pañcavaktravibhūṣitaḥ | 

ayaṃ sa vāgbhavo devīvāgīśatvapradāyakaḥ | 

idaṃ vāgbhavakūṭantu bāliśasyāpi mūrddhani | 

hastaṃ dattvā paṭhet siddhamaṣṭottaraśataṃ priye (!) | 

so'pi ślokaṃ maheśāni (!) karotyeva na saṃśayaḥ || 

bāliśasya mūrkhasya | 

sattarkapadavākyārthaśabdālaṅkārasāravit | 

jihvāyāṃ nyāsanāddevi (!) mūko'pi sukavirbhavet | 

vṛhannīlatantre saptamapaṭale dvitīyavidyātryakṣarīmantramadhikṛtya | 

idānīṃ śṛṇu cārvaṅgi (!) kavitākārakaṃ param | 

prayogaṃ durlabhaṃ gopyaṃ tava snehāt prakāśitam | 

caṇḍīṃ cāṇḍālinīñcaiva tyāginaṃ sarvamohanam | 

vījatrayaṃ japedrātrau madhye caiva raverdine | 

aṣṭādhikasahasrasya pramāṇena japaṃ caret | 

śatābhimantritaṃ kṛtvā pibecca jalamuttamam | 

saptadinaprayogeṇa kavitā cittamodinī | 

jāyate nātra sandehaḥ satyaṃ suragaṇārcite (!) | 

pāṇinā dakṣiṇenaiva madhulājān samānayet | 

nāḍīcchedācca prāgeva bālaṃ saṃskuryyācca sādhakaḥ | 

kavitvaṃ jāyate tena pāṇḍityaṃ suravandite (!) | 

jihvāṃ sammārjya deveśi (!) likheddhemaśalākayā | 

dūrvayā vā mahādevi (!) jihvoṣṭhayoḥ samālikhet | 

paṃktidvayena saṃlekhya kuryācca bālasaṃskriyām | 

ekādaśāhe deveśi (!) dvādaśāhe'tha vā punaḥ | 

<p. 149> varṇajātyādibhedena māsāntaḥ sambhaviṣyati | 

yathāśaktyupacāreṇa devatāṃ pūjayet punaḥ | 

sampūjya devatāṃ bhaktyā likhenmantraṃ maheśvari (!) | 

yadā pitā na deśasthaḥ pitṛvyo mātulo'pi vā | 

likhitvā parameśāni (!) kuryyācca bālasaṃskriyām | 

mūlamantraṃ likhenmantrī yasyauṣṭhe śvetadūrvayā | 

vākyoccāraṇato bālo vāgmī drutakavirbhavet | 

janmasaṃskārakaṃ nāma putre jāte praśasyate | 

jihvāyāñca likhedyantraṃ yatne dārukuśena vā | 

vāratrayantu sammārjya dakṣiṇenaiva pāṇinā | 

mantramuccāryya pratyekaṃ paṃktiṃ kuryyāt suśobhanam | 

ādau saṃskāraḥ karttavyastadante vilikhenmanum | 

gandhacandanapuṣpaiśca pūjayet tāriṇīṃ śive (!) | 

uttarābhimukho bhūtvā sthāpayet pīṭhamuttamam | 

pūjayet tāriṇīṃ devīṃ nānābhakṣyaiḥ suśobhanaiḥ | 

ṣoḍaśairupacāraiśca pūjayed bhaktibhāvataḥ | 

dhūpaṃ dadyādguggulunā sarvakarmmaphalapradam | 

nārikelaṃ tathā rambhāṃ vadaraṃ vakulaṃ tathā | 

vījapūraṃ karṇikāraṃ śarkarāṃ gandhasaṃyutām | 

madhūdakaṃ kalāyañca siddhānnaṃ pāyasāplutam | 

māṣaṃ matsyaṃ piṣṭakañca dadyādatipriyaṃ mahat | 

kavirvāgmī bhavet putraḥ sarvakāmaprakārakaḥ | 

jitendriyaḥ satyavādī dhārmiko jāyate mahān || 

dadyādityanena sampradānopasthitervacane brāhmaṇapadasyābhāve'pi brāhmaṇebhya iti 
labhyate | 

ato brāhmaṇasampradānakamāṣādidānavyavahāra iti || 

mahānirvāṇatantre || 

kṛtopanayane putre tena nāmnā samāhvayet | 

prāyaścittādikaṃ kṛtvā jātakarmma samāpayet | 

nālacchedaṃ tato dhātrī kuryyādutsāhapūrvakam | 

yāvanna cchidyate nālaṃ tāvat śaucaṃ na bādhate | 

prāgeva nāḍīkācchedāt daivīṃ paitrīṃ kriyāñcaret | 

kumāryāścāpi karttavyamevamevamamantrakam || 

iti jātakarmma ||

ṣaṣṭhe vā cāṣṭame māsi nāma kuryyāt prakāśataḥ | 

snāpayitvā śiśuṃ mātā paridhāpyāmbare śubhe | 

<p. 150> bharttuḥ pārśvaṃ samāgatya prāṅmukhaṃ sthāpayet sutam | 

abhiṣiñcet śiśormūrdhni sahiraṇyakuśodakaiḥ | 

jāhnavī yamunā revā sapavitrā sarasvatī | 

narmmadā varadā kumbhī sāgarāśca sarāṃsi ca | 

ete tvāmabhiṣiñcantu dharmmakāmārthasiddhaye | 

hrīṃ | 

āpo hiṣṭhā mayo bhuvastā na ūrjje dadhātana | 

maheraṇāya cakṣaṣe yo vaḥ śivatamo rasastasya bhājayateha naḥ | 

uśatīriva mātaraḥ tasmā araṅgamāma vo kṣayāya jinvatha āpo janayathā ca naḥ | 

abhiṣicya tribhirmantraiḥ pūrvavadvahnisaṃskriyām | 

katvā sampādya dhārāntaṃ dadyāt pañcāhutīḥ sudhīḥ | 

agnaye prathamaṃ dattvā vāsavāya tataḥ param | 

tataḥ prajānāṃ pataye viśvedevebhya eva | 

brahmaṇe cāhutīṃ dadyādvahnau pārthivasaṃjñake | 

tato'ṅke putramādāya śrāvayeddakṣiṇaśrutau | 

svalpākṣaraṃ sukhoccāryaṃ śubhaṃ nāma vicakṣaṇaḥ | 

śrāvayitvā tridhā nāma brāhmaṇebhyo nivedya ca | 

tataḥ samāpayet karmma kṛtvā sviṣṭikṛtādikam | 

kanyāyā niṣkramo nāsti vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ na vidyate | 

nāmānnaprāśanaṃ cūḍāṃ kuryyāddhīmānamantrakam | 

caturthe māsi ṣaṣṭhe vā kuryyānniṣkramaṇaṃ śiśoḥ | 

kṛtanityakriyastātaḥ sampūjya gaṇanāyakam | 

snāpayitvā tu tanayaṃ vastrālaṅkārabhūṣitam | 

saṃsthāpya purato vidvānimaṃ mantramudīrayet | 

brahmā viṣṇuḥ śivo durgā gaṇeśo bhāskarastathā | 

indro rāyuḥ kuveraśca varuṇo'gnirvṛhaspatiḥ | 

śiśoḥ śubhaṃ prakurvantu rakṣantu pathi sarvadā | 

ityuktvāṅke samādāya gītavādyapuraḥsaram | 

vahirniṣkrāmayedbālaṃ sānandaiḥ svajanaiḥ saha | 

gatvādhvani kiyaddūraṃ śiśuṃ sūryyaṃ nirīkṣayet | hrīṃ | 

taccakṣurdevahitaṃ purastācchukramuccarat | 

paśyemaśaradaḥ śataṃ jīvema śaradaḥ śatam | 

śṛṇuyāma śaradaḥ śatam | 

ityādityaṃ darśayitvā samāgatya nijālayam | 

arghyaṃ dattvā dineśāya svajanān bhojayet pitā || 

<p. 151> iti niṣkramaṇam ||

ṣaṣṭhe māsi kumārasya māse vāpyaṣṭame śive (!) | 

pitṛbhrātā pitā vāpi kuryyādannāśanakriyām | 

pūrvavaddevapūjādi vahnisaṃskaraṇaṃ tathā | 

evaṃ dhārāntakarmāṇi sampādya vidhivat pitā | 

dadyāt pañcāhuhīstatra śucināmni hutāśane | 

agnimuddiśya prathamāṃ dvitīyāṃ vāsavaṃ smaran | 

tataḥ prajāpatiṃ devaṃ viśvān devān tataḥ param | 

brahmāṇañca samuddiśya pañcamīmāhutiṃ tyajet | 

tato'gnāvannadāṃ dhyātvā dattapañcāhutiḥ pitā | 

tatrāthavā gṛhe'nyasmin vastrālaṅkāraśobhitam | 

kroḍe nidhāya tanayaṃ prāśayet pāyasāmṛtam | 

pañcaprāṇāhutermantrairbhojayitvā tu pañcadhā | 

tato'nnavyañjanādīnāṃ dattvā kiñcit śiśormukhe | 

śaṅkhatūryyādighoṣeṇa prāyaścittaṃ samāpayet |

ityannaprāśanaṃ proktaṃ cūḍāvidhimataḥ śṛṇu | 

tṛtīye pañcame varṣe kulācārānusārataḥ | 

cūḍākarma śiśoḥ kuryyādbālasaṃskārasiddhaye | 

devapūjādidhārāntakarma niṣpādya sādhakaḥ | 

satyāgneruttare deśe vṛṣagomayapūritam | 

tilagodhūmasaṃyuktaṃ śarāvaṃ sthāpayed budhaḥ | 

kavoṣṇaṃ salilañcāpi kṣuramekaṃ suśāṇitam | 

āsādya tanayaṃ tatra janakaḥ svīyavāmataḥ | 

saṃsthāpya jananīkroḍe kavoṣṇasalilaiśca taiḥ | 

vāruṇaṃ daśadhā japtvā sammārjya śuśumūrddhajān | 

māyayā kuśapatrābhyāṃ juṣṭimekāṃ prakalpayet | 

māyāṃ lakṣmīṃ tridhā japtvā gṛhītvā lauhajaṃ kṣuram | 

chittvā tu juṣṭikāmūlaṃ mātṛhaste niveśayet | 

kumāramātā hastābhyāmādāya gomayānvite | 

śarāve sthāpayejjuṣṭiṃ nāpitāya pitā vadet | 

kṣuramuṇḍe śiśoḥ kṣauraṃ sukhaṃ sādhaya ṭhadvayam | 

paṭhitvā nāpitaṃ paśyan satyanāmani pāvake | 

prajāpatiṃ samuddiśya pradadyādāhutitrayam | 

nāpitena kṛtakṣauraṃ snāpayitvā śuśuṃ tataḥ | 

vastrālaṅkāramālyena bhūṣayitvāgnisannidhau | 

<p. 152> svavāmabhāge saṃsthāpya sviṣṭikṛddhomamācaret | 

prāyaścittaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā dadyāt pūrṇāhutiṃ pitā | 

māyā śiśo (!) te kuśalaṃ  kurutāṃ viśvakṛdvibhuḥ | 

paṭhitvainaṃ śiśoḥ karṇe svarṇamayyā śalākayā | 

rājatyā lauhamayyā vā tatra vedhaṃ prakalpayet | 

āpohiṣṭheti mantreṇa abhiṣicya sutaṃ tataḥ | 

śāntyādidakṣiṇāṃ kṛtvā cūḍākarma samāpayet | 

garbhādhānādicūḍāntaṃ samānaṃ sarvajātiṣu | 

śūdrasāmānyajātīnāṃ sarvametadamantrakam | 

jātakarmādicūḍāntaṃ kumāryyāścāpyamantrakam | 

karttavyaṃ pañcabhirvarṇairekaṃ niṣkramaṇaṃ vinā ||

athocyate dvijātīnāmupavītikriyāvidhiḥ | 

yasmin kṛte dvijanmāno daivapaitryādhikāriṇaḥ | 

garbhāṣṭame'ṣṭame vābde kuryyādupanayaṃ śiśoḥ | 

ṣoḍaśābdādhiko nopanetavyo niṣkriyo hi saḥ | 

kṛtanityakriyo vidvān pañca devān samarccayet | 

gauryyādimātṛkāścaiva vasudhārāṃ prakalpayet | 

vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ tataḥ kuryyāt devatāpitṛtṛptaye | 

kuśaṇḍikoktavidhinā dhārāhomāntamācaret | 

prātaḥ katvāśanaṃ bālaṃ susnātañca svalaṅkṛtam | 

śikhāṃ vinā kṛtakṣauraṃ kṣaumāmbaravibhūṣitam | 

chāyāmaṇḍapamānīya samudbhavahutāśituḥ | 

samīpe cātmano vāme saṃsthāpya vimalāsane | 

śiṣyaṃ vadedbrahmacaryyaṃ kuru vatsa (!) tataḥ śiśuḥ | 

brahmacaryyaṃ karomīti gurave vinivedayet | 

tato guruḥ prasannātmā śiśave śāntacetase | 

kāṣāyavāsasī dadyāddīrghāṣuṣṭvāya varccase | 

mauñjīṃ kuśamayīṃ vāpi trivṛttāṃ granthisaṃyutām | 

tūṣṇīñca mekhalāṃ dadyāt kāṣāyavastradhāriṇe | 

māyāmuccārya subhagā mekhalā syācchubhapadā | 

ityuktvā mekhalāṃ baddhvā maunī tiṣṭhed guroḥ puraḥ | 

yajñopavītaṃ paramaṃ pavitraṃ vṛhaspateryat sahajaṃ purastāt | 

āyuṣyamagryaṃ pratimuñca śubhraṃ yajñopavītaṃ balamastu tejaḥ || 

mantreṇānena śiśave dadyāt kṛṣṇājinānvitam | 

<p. 153> yajñasūtraparimāṇantu mātṛkābhedatantre ekādaśapaṭale || 

yajñasūtrasya yanmānaṃ tat śṛṇuṣva varānane (!) | 

ṛgvedī dhārayet sūtraṃ nābherūrddhvaṃ stanādadhaḥ | 

yajuṣāṃ sūtramānaṃ hi āścaryaṃ śṛṇu śailaje (!) | 

bāhumūlapramāṇena yajñasūtraṃ dvijātibhiḥ | 

dhāraṇīyaṃ prayatnena nānyadaghnaṃ kadācana | 

nānyadaghnaṃ nānyapramāṇamityarthaḥ | 

sāmagasya dīrghasūtraṃ trividhaṃ varavarṇini (!) | 

brahmarandhrānnābhideśaparyyantaṃ yajñasūtrakam | 

athavāpi ca grīvāyāmāropya nābhimā(-)spṛśet | 

tasmāt pṛṣṭhe merudaṇḍaparyyantaṃ yajñasūtrakam | 

athavā parameśāni (!) prakārāntaramucyate | 

grīvāyā dakṣiṇāṅguṣṭhaparyyantaṃ yajñasūtrakam | 

atharvo dhārayet sūtraṃ yatnena yajuṣāṃ matam | 

athavā dhāvayet sūtraṃ sāmagasya pramāṇataḥ | 

athavā dhārayet yajñasūtraṃ paramamohanam | 

ājñācakrānnābhideśaparyyantaṃ yajñasūtrakam | 

etat saṅketamajñātvā yaḥ kuryyāt sūtradhāraṇam | 

sa caṇḍālasamo devi (!) yadi vyāsasamo bhavet || 

prasaṅgād yajñasūtrasya karttanaṃ jīvanyāsaśca likhyate || 

gāyatrīṃtantre caturthabrāhmaṇapaṭale || 

kanyā ca karttayet sūtraṃ patiputtravatī tathā | 

vidhavā'vidhavā vāpi putrahīnāpi brāhmaṇī | 

kṣatrāṇī vaiśyadārā ca karttayenna tu śūdriṇī | 

dīkṣitā yadi sā śūdrā karttanena ca ninditā | 

sacchūdrā yadi sā bhadre (!) śuddhā sā sūtrakarttane | 

asa cchūdrā maheśāni (!) ninditā sūtrakarttane | 

kārpāsasambhavaṃ sūtraṃ yajñasūtraṃ vinirmitam | 

sūkṣmātisūkṣmaṃ paramaṃ sarvadevamayaṃ tathā || 

iti nirmāṇam || 

aṇugranthiḥ | 

granthikāle smaredviprān sūtraṃ bhavati mūrtimat | 

brahmā ca kāśyapo vipraḥ sanakaśca sanandanaḥ | 

sanatsanātano vipro nāradaḥ kapilastathā | 

marīciratriḥ pulahaḥ pulastyo gautamaḥ kratuḥ | 

bhṛgurdakṣaḥ pracetāśca vaśiṣṭho vālmikistathā | 

dvaipāyano bharadvājaḥ śukro jaiminireva ca | 

<p. 154> vidūrathaḥ śunaḥśepho jātukarṇaśca rauravaḥ | 

aurvaḥ saṃvarttakaḥ śukraḥ surācāryo vṛhaspatiḥ | 

candraḥ sūryyo budhaḥ śrīmān yajñasūtrasya granthiṣu | 

tiṣṭhantu mama vāmāṃśe vāmaskandhe tvaharniśam | 

brahmādyā devatāḥ sarvā yajñasūtrasya devatāḥ || tathā | 

jīvanyāsaṃ pravakṣyāmi yajñasūtrasya saṃśṛṇu | 

yajñasūtraṃ kare kṛtvā dakṣiṇe dvijasattamaḥ | 

gāyatrīṃ prathamaṃ japtvā praṇavaṃ prajapet śatam | 

gāyatrīṃ punaruktvā vai vāmaskandhe niveśayet | 

jīvanyāsaṃ vinā devi (!) yajñasūtrañca sūtravat || 

mahānirvāṇe || 

yajñopavītaṃ daṇḍañca vaiṇavaṃ khādirañca vā | 

pālāśamathavā dadyāt kṣīravṛkṣasamudbhavam | 

āpohiṣṭheti mantreṇa māyayā puṭitena ca | 

trirāvṛttyā kuśāmbhobhirdhṛtadaṇḍopavītakam | 

abhiṣicya tatastoyaiḥ pūrayedbālakāñjalim | 

tadañjaliṃ dineśāya dātāraṃ brahmacāriṇam | 

taccakṣuriti mantreṇa darśayedbhāskaraṃ guruḥ | 

dṛṣṭvā bhāskaramācāryo vadenmāṇavakaṃ tataḥ | 

mama vrate mano dhehi mama cittaṃ dadāmi te | 

juṣasvaikamanā vatsa (!) mama vāco'stu te śivam | 

hṛdi spṛṣṭvā paṭhitvainaṃ kiṃnāmāsīti taṃ vadet | 

śiṣyastvamukaśarmāhaṃ bhavantamabhivādaye | 

kasya tvaṃ brahmacārīti gurau pṛcchati pārvati (!) | 

śiṣyaḥ sāvahito brūyādbhavato brahmacāryyaham | 

indrasya brahmacāro tvamācāryyaste hutāśanaḥ | 

ityuktvā sa guruḥ paścāddevebhyastaṃ samarpayet | 

tvāṃ prajāpataye vatsa (!) savitre varuṇāya ca | 

pṛthivyai sarvadevebhyaḥ sarvavedebhya eva ca | 

samarpayāmi te sarva rakṣantu tvāṃ nirantaram | 

tato māṇavako vahniṃ dakṣiṇāvartmayogataḥ | 

guruṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya āsane punarāviśet | 

guruḥ śiṣyeṇa sampṛṣṭaḥ samudbhavahutāśane | 

pañca devān samuddiśya dadyāt pañcāhutīḥ priye (!) | 

prajāpatistataḥ śakro viṣṇubrahmā śivastathā | 

māyādivahnijāyāntairjuhuyāt svasvanāmabhiḥ | 

anuktamantre sarvatra vidhireṣa prakīrttitaḥ | 

<p. 155> tato durgā mahālakṣmīḥ sundarī bhuvaneśvarī | 

indrādidaśadikpālā bhāskarādinavagrahāḥ | 

pratyekanāmnā hutvaitān vāsasācchādya bālakam | 

pṛcchenmāṇavakaṃ prājño brahmacaryyābhimāninam | 

ko vāśrayaste tanaya (!) brūhi kinte manogatam | 

tataḥ śiṣyaḥ sāvahito dhṛtvā gurupadadvayam | 

karotu māmāśramiṇaṃ brahmavidyopadeśataḥ | 

evaṃ prārthayamānasya dakṣakarṇe śiśostadā | 

śrāvayitvā tridhā tāraṃ sarvamantramayaṃ śive (!) | 

vyāhṛtitrayamuccāryya sāvitrīṃ śrāvayed guruḥ | 

ṛṣiḥ sadāśivaḥ proktaśchando'nuṣṭubudāhṛtam | 

adhiṣṭhātrī ca sāvitrī mokṣārthe viniyogitā | 

antargataṃ mahadvarcco varaṇīyaṃ yathātmabhiḥ | 

dhyāyema tat padaṃ satyaṃ sarvavyāpi sanātanam | 

yo bhargaḥ sarvasākṣī no manobuddhīndriyāṇi ca | 

dharmārthakāmamokṣeṣu prerayedviniyojayet | 

itthamarthayutāṃ brahmavidyāmāsādya sadguruḥ | 

śiṣyaṃ niyojaeddevi (!) gṛhasthāśramakarmasu | 

brahmacaryyocitaṃ veśaṃ vatsedānīṃ parityaja | 

śāmbhavoditamārgeṇa devān pitṝn samarccaya | 

brahmavidyopadeśena pavitraṃ te kalevaram | 

prāptā gṛhasthāśramitā taduktaṃ karma kalpaya | 

upavītadvayaṃ divyavastrālaṅkaraṇāni ca | 

gṛhāṇa pādukācchatraṃ gandhamālyānulepanaiḥ | 

tataḥ kāṣāyavasanaṃ kṛṣṇājinasamanvitam | 

yajñasūtraṃ mekhalāñca daṇḍaṃ bhikṣākaraṇḍakam | 

ācārādarjitāṃ  bhikṣāṃ samarpya gurave śive (!) | 

śuddhopavītayugalaṃ paridhāyāmbare śubhe | 

gandhamālyadharastūṣṇīṃ tiṣṭhedācāryasannidhau |

tato gṛhasthāśramiṇaṃ śiṣyametadvaded guruḥ | 

jitendriyaḥ rātyavādī brahmavidyārato bhava | 

svādhyāyāśramakarmāṇi yathādharmeṇa sādhaya | 

ityādiśya tvidaṃ paścāt samudbhavahutāśane | 

māyādipraṇavānte na bhūrbhuvaḥsvastrayeṇa ca | 

hāvayitvā tridhācāryyaḥ sviṣṭikṛddhomamācaret | 

dattvā pūrṇāhutiṃ bhadre (!) vratakarma samāpayet | 

bījasekādisaṃskārā vratāntāḥ pitṛto nava | 

<p. 156> udvāhaḥ pitṛto vāpi svato'pi sidhyati priye (!) || 

matsyamūkte ekatriṃśatpaṭale || 

adārasya gatirnāsti sarvāstasyāphalāḥ kriyāḥ | 

surārccanaṃ mahāyajñaṃ hīnabhāryyo vivarjayet | 

ekacakraratho yadvadekapakṣo yathā khagaḥ | 

abhāryyo'pi narastadvadayogyaḥ sarvakarmasu | 

bhāryāhīne kriyā nāsti bhāryyāhīne kutaḥ sukham | 

bhāryāhīne gṛhaṃ kasya tasmādbhāryyāṃ samāśrayet | 

sarvasvenāpi deveśi (!) karttavyo dārasaṃgrahaḥ | 

iti dāraparigrahasyāvaśyakatvam tatraiva || 

satryāsamathavā kāryyaṃ tṛtīyaṃ nopapadyate | 

savarṇā brāhmaṇī yā tu dharmapatnī ca sā smṛtā | 

asavarṇā ca yā bhāryyā kāmapatnī tu sā smṛtā || 

iti dharmapatnīnirṇayaḥ | 

mahānirvāṇatantre || 

vivāhe hi kṛtasnānaḥ kṛtanityakriyaḥ kṛtī | 

pañca devān samabhyarcya gauryyādimātṛkāstathā | 

vasordhārāṃ kalpayitvā vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ samācaret | 

rātrau pratiśrutaṃ pātraṃ gītavādyapuraḥsaram | 

chāyāmaṇḍapamānīya upaviśya varāsane | 

vāsavābhimukhaṃ dātā paścimābhimukho viśet | 

ācāryyaḥ svastimṛddhiñca kathayedbrāhmaṇaiḥ saha | 

sādhupraśnaṃ varaṃ pṛcchedarccanāpraśnameva ca | 

varātpraśnottaraṃ nītvā pādādyairvaramarcayet | 

samarpayāmi vākyena deśadravyaṃ samarpayet | 

pādayorarpayetpādyaṃ śirasyarghyaṃ nivedayet | 

ācamyaṃ vadane dadyādgandhaṃ mālyaṃ suvāsasī | 

divyābharaṇaratnāni yajñasūtraṃ samarpayet | 

tatastu bhājane kāṃsye kṛtvā dadhi ghṛtaṃ madhu | 

samarpayāmi vākyena madhuparkaṃ kare'rpayet | 

varo'pi pātramādāya vāme pāṇau nidhāya ca | 

dakṣāṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ prāṇāhutyuktamantrakaiḥ | 

pañcadhāghrāya tatpātramudīcyāṃ diśi dhārayet | 

madhuparkaṃ samāpyaivaṃ punarācāmayed dvayam | 

dūrvākṣatābhyāṃ jāmāturvidhṛtya jānudakṣiṇam | 

smṛtvā viṣṇuṃ tatsaditi māsapakṣau tithiṃ tataḥ | 

samullikhya nimittāni vṛṇuyādvaramuttamam | 

gotrapravaranāmāni pratyekaṃ prapitāmahāt | 

<p. 157> ṣaṣṭhyantāni samuccārya varasya janakāvadhi | 

dvitīyāntaṃ varaṃ brūyādgotrapravaranāmabhiḥ | 

tathaiva kanyāmuddiśya brāhmodvāhena paṇḍitaḥ | 

dātuṃ bhavantamityuktvā vṛṇe'hamiti kīrttayet | 

vṛto'smīti varo brūyāt tato dātā vadedvaram | 

yathāvihitamityuktvā vivāhakarmma kurviti | 

varo brūyād yathājñānaṃ karavāṇi taduttaram | 

tataḥ kanyāṃ samānīya vastrālaṅkārabhūṣitām | 

vastrāntareṇa sañchādya sthāpayedvarasammukhe | 

punarvaraṃ samabhyarcya vāso'laṅkaraṇādibhiḥ | 

varasya dakṣiṇe pāṇau kanyāpāṇiṃ niyojayet | 

tanmadhye pañcaratnāni phalatāmbūlameva vā | 

dattvārccayitvā tanayāṃ varāya viduṣe'rpayet | 

prāgvat traipuruṣākhyānaṃ nimittākhyānameva ca | 

ātmanaḥ kāmamuddiśya caturthyantaṃ varaṃ vadet | 

kanyābhidhāṃ dvitīyāntāmarccitāṃ samalaṅkṛtām | 

sācchādanāṃ prajāpatidevatākāmudīrayan | 

tubhyamahamiti procya dadyāt sampradade vadan | 

varaḥ svastīti svīkuryyāt sampradātā varaṃ vadet | 

dharme cārthe ca kāme ca bhavatā bhāryyayā saha | 

varttitavyaṃ varo vācamuktvā kāmastutiṃ paṭhet | 

dātā kāmo grahītāpi kāmāyādācca kāminīm | 

kāmena tvāṃ pratigṛhṇāpi kāmaḥ pūrṇo'stu cāvayoḥ | 

tato vadet sampradātā kanyāṃ jāmātaraṃ prati | 

prajāpatiprasādena yuvayorabhivāñchitam | 

pūrṇamastu śivañcāstu dharmaṃ pālayataṃ yuvām | 

tata ācchādya vastreṇa sampradātā sumaṅgalaiḥ | 

parasparamukhālokaṃ kārayedvarakanyayoḥ | 

tato hiraṇyaratnāni yathāśaktyanusārataḥ | 

jāmātre dakṣiṇāṃ dadyādacchidramavadhārayet | 

varastu bhāryyayā sārddhaṃ tadrādau divase'pi vā | 

kuśaṇḍikoktavidhinā vahnisthāpanamācaret | 

yojakākhyaḥ pāvako'tra prājāpatyaścaruḥ smṛtaḥ | 

dhārāntaṃ karmma sampādya dadyāt pañcāhutīrvaraḥ | 

śivaṃ durgāṃ tathā viṣṇuḥ brahmāṇaṃ vajradhāriṇam | 

<p. 158> dhyātvaikekaikaṃ samuddiśya juhuyāt saṃskṛte'nale | 

bhāryyāyāḥ pāṇiyugalaṃ gṛhṇīyāttānyudīrayan | 

pāṇiṃ gṛhṇāmi subhage (!) gurudevaratā bhava | 

gārhasthyaṃ dharmakarmeṇa yathāvadanuśīlaya | 

ghṛtena svāmidattena lājairbhrātrāhṛtaiḥ śive (!) | 

prajāpatiṃ samuddiśya dadyāddve cāhutī varaḥ | 

pradakṣiṇīkṛtya vahnimutthāya bhāryyayā saha | 

durgāṃ śivaṃ ramāṃ vuṣṇuṃ brāhmīṃ brahmāṇameva ca | 

yugmaṃ yugmaṃ samuddiśya tristridhā havanaṃ caret | 

aśmamaṇḍalikāsaptārohau kuryyādamantrakam | 

niśāyāñcettadā strībhiḥ paśyeddhūmrāmarundhatīm | 

pratyāvṛtyāsane samyagupaviśya varastadā | 

sviṣṭikṛddhomapūrṇāhutyantaṃ karmma samāpayet | 

brāhmo vivāho vihitī doṣahīnaḥ savarṇayā | 

kuladharmānusāreṇa gotrabhinnā sapiṇḍajā | 

brāhmodvāhena yā grāhyā saiva patnī gṛheśvarī | 

tadanujñāṃ vinā brāhmavivāhaṃ nācaret punaḥ | 

asyā apatye tadvaṃśe vidyamāne kuleśvari (!) ||
	
atha mahānirvāṇatantroktadaśasaṃskāraprayogaḥ | 

saṃskārairvinā daivapaitrakarmmānadhikāritayā sarvairviprādibhirvarṇaiḥ svasvavarṇoktāḥ 
saṃskārāḥ kāryyāḥ | 

daśa saṃskārāstu vījasekapuṃsavanasīmantonnayanajātakarmma(-)
nāmakaraṇaniṣkramaṇānnaprāśanacūḍopanayanavivāhāḥ | 

tataḥ śūdrāṇāmadhamaśūdrāṇāmupanayanaṃ nāsti teṣāṃ navaiva saṃskārāḥ | 

brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyānāntu daśa saṃskārā iti ||

atha garbhādhānam | 

kṛtanityakriya āsane upaviśya dīkṣāpaddhatilikhiṣyamāṇamantrairghaṭān saṃsthāpya tatra 
brahmadurgāgaṇeśaviṣṇusūryyaśivādityādinavagrahendrādi(-)daśadikpālān sampūjya tato 
gauryyādiṣoḍaśamātṛkāḥ pūjayet | 

ṣoḍaśamātṛkāstu || 

gaurī padmā śacī medhā sāvitrī vijayā jayā | 

devasenā svadhā svāhā śāntiḥ puṣṭirdhṛtiḥ kṣamā || 

ātmadevatā kuladevatā etāvatyaḥ | 

pūjākramastu || 

arghyasthāpanaṃ kṛtvā baddhāñjaliḥ | 

hrīṃ āyāntu mātaraḥ sarvāstridaśānandakārikāḥ | 

<p. 159> vivāhavratayajñānāṃ sarvābhīṣṭaṃ prakalpyatām | 

yānaśaktisamārūḍhāḥ saumyamūrtidharāḥ sadā | 

āyāntu mātaraḥ sarvā yajñotsavasamṛddhaye || 

iti mātṛkāgaṇamāvāhya hrīṃ gauryye mātre nama iti pādyādibhiryathāśakti sampūjya 
evaṃ padmāyai mātre nama ityādibhiḥ ṣoḍaśamātṛkārccanaṃ svaśaktyāṃ vidhāya 
nābhimātrāyāṃ bhittau prādeśamātramadhye sindūreṇa raktacandanena vā sapta vā 
pañca vā bindūn dadyāt | 

tataḥ pratyekavindau klīmiti hrīmiti śrīmiti vījatrayeṇāvicchinnāṃ ghṛtadhārāṃ dattvā tatra 
vasuṃ yajet | 

tato homaprakaraṇalekhyasthaṇḍilādivahnisthāpanāntaṃ karmma kṛtvā homadravyāṇi 
saṃskṛtya prājāpatya caruṃ śrapayet | 

tato vāyunāmānamagniṃ dhyātvā āvāhya ghṛtena pādyādibhiḥ sampūjya 
dhārāhomāntaṃ karmma kṛtvā hrīṃ prajāpataye svāhā iti mantreṇa caruṇā 
vāratrayaṃ juhuyāt | 

tato hrīṃ viṣṇuryoniṃ kalpayatu tvaṣṭā rūpāṇi yacchatu | 

asiñcatu prajāpatirdhātā garbhaṃ dadhātu te | 

svāhā ityanena caruṇā vāramekaṃ juhuyāt | 

tata ājyena caruṇā sājyena na caruṇā vā sūryyaprajāpativiṣṇūn dhyātvā hrīṃ 
sūryyāya svāhā hrīṃ prajāpataye svāhā hrīṃ viṣṇave svāhā iti mantratrayeṇa 
pratyekaṃ juhuyāt | 

tataḥ sinīvālīsarasvatyaśvinān dhyātvā hrīṃ | 

garbhaṃ dhehi sinīvālī garbhaṃ dhehi sarasvatī | 

garbhaṃ te aśvinau devāvādhattāṃ puṣkarasrajau || 

svāhā ityanena ghṛtena juhuyāt | 

tato manasā raviṃ viṣṇuṃ dhyātvā klīṃ strīṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ humityamuṣyai 
putrakāmāyai garbhamādhehi svāhā | 

iti mantreṇājyena juhuyāt | 

tato viṣṇuṃ manasā dhyātvā hrīṃ | 

yatheyaṃ pṛthivī devī hyuttānā garbhamādadhe | 

tathā tvaṃ garbhamādhehi daśame kāsi sūtaye || 

svāhā iti mantreṇājyenaṃ juhuyāt | 

tataḥ punarapi viṣṇuṃ manasā dhyātvā hrīṃ | 

viṣṇorjyeṣṭhena rūpeṇa nāryyāmasyāṃ varīyasam | 

<p. 160> sutamādhehi | 

svāhā ityanenājyena juhuyāt | 

tataḥ klīṃ hrīṃ strīṃ hrīṃ klīṃ hrīmiti mantreṇa badhvāḥ śiraḥ spṛśet | 

tataḥ putravatībhirnārībhiḥ pariveṣṭito hastābhyāṃ badhvāḥ śira ālasya viṣṇuṃ 
durgāṃ vidhiṃ sūryyañca dhyātvā tasyāḥ kroḍāñcale phalatrayaṃ patirdadyāt | 

tataḥ sviṣṭikṛtaṃ hutvā prāyaścittahomaṃ kuryyāt | 

tataḥ pradoṣasamaye gaurīsahitaśaṅkaraṃ sampūjya bhāskarārghyaṃ dadyāditi | 

tatastadrātrāvanyarātrau vā yugmāyāṃ bhāryyayā saha bhavanāntaraṃ gatvā 
prajāpatiṃ manasā dhyātvā patnīṃ saṃspṛśya śayyāśodhanaṃ kuryyāt | 

yathā | 

hrīṃ āvayoḥ suprajāyai tvaṃ śayye (!) śubhakarī bhava | 

tato bhāryyayā saha śayyāmāruhya prāṅmukha udaṅmukho vā upaviśya striyaṃ 
paśyan tanmastake hastaṃ dattvā vāmena pāṇinā tāmāliṅgya tasyāḥ śīrṣe klīmiti 
śatadhā civuke aimiti śatadhā kaṇṭhe śrīmiti viṃśatidhā stanadvandve śrīmiti śatadhā 
hṛdaye hrīmiti pañcaviṃśatidhā japtvā yonau karaṃ dattvā klīṃ aimiti aṣṭottaraśataṃ 
japtvā liṅge'pi aiṃ klīmityaṣṭottaraśataṃ japet | 

tato hrīmiti mantreṇa yoniṃ vikāśya sutaprāptaye striyamupagacchet | 

tato retaḥpātasamaye viśvakarmāṇaṃ dhyātvā nābheradhastāt citkuṇḍe retaḥ pātayet | 

śukrapātāntasamaye imaṃ paṭhet |

hrīṃ |

yathāgninā sagarbhā bhūrdyauryathā vajradhāriṇā | 

vāyunā diggarbhavatī tathā garbhavatī bhava || 

iti garbhādhānam |

atha puṃsavanam | 

patiḥ kṛtanityakriyaḥ pañcadevatāpūjādivṛddhiśrādhāntaṃ garbhādhānapaddhatyuktaṃ 
vidhāya tato vahnisthāpanādi kṛtvā candranāmānamagniṃ dhyātvāvāhya sampūjya 
prājāpatyaṃ caruṃ pacet | 

tato gavye dadhi yavamekaṃ dvau māṣau ca nikṣipya striyaṃ pṛcchet | 

he bhadre (!) kiṃ tvaṃ triḥ kṛtaṃ pibasi iti | 

tataḥ strī brūyāt mayā puṃsavanaṃ kṛtvaṃ pīyate | 

<p. 161> ityuktvā yavamāṣayutadadhiprasṛtitrayaṃ pibet | 

tano jīvavatsābhirvanitābhistāṃ yāgasthānaṃ samānayet | 

tato vāmabhāge tāṃ saṃsthāpya carumānīya hrīṃ huṃ ye cātra vighnakarttāro ye ca 
garbhavināśakāḥ | 

bhūtāḥ pretāḥ piśācāśca vetālā bālaghātakāḥ | 

tān sarvānnāśaya nāśaya garbharakṣāṃ kuru svāhā | 

ityanena mantreṇa rakṣoghnaṃ vicintya prajāpatiñca dhyāyan caruṇā 
dvādaśāhutīrjuhuyāt | 

hrīṃ candramase svāheti mantreṇa pañcāhutīrdadyāt | 

tato bhāryyāhṛdi spṛṣṭvā hrīṃ śrīmiti mantraṃ śataṃ japet | 

tataḥ sviṣṭikṛddhomastadanantaraṃ prāyaścittahomādivahnivisarjjanāntaṃ karmma kṛtvā 
dakṣiṇācchidrāvadhāraṇāntaṃ kuryyāditi puṃsavanam ||

atha pañcāmṛtadānam | 

tattu garbhajananamāsāvadhike pañcame māsi garbhiṇyai deyam | 

pañcāmṛtantu samāṃsena dadhimadhudugdhaghṛtaśarkarāmiśritarūpam | 

anena striyā dehaśuddhirbhavati | 

tatra pratyekaṃ dravye aim klīṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ huṃ lamiti pañcadhā japtvā sammiśrya 
garbhiṇīṃ prāśayediti pañcāmṛtadānam ||

atha sīmantonnayanam | 

tattu ṣaṣṭhe'ṣṭame vā māsi karttavyam | 

tatrāśaktāvapatyajananāt pūrvaṃ kāryyam | 

tatra patiḥ pañcadevatāpūjādidhārohomāntaṃ garbhādhānaprakaraṇoktaṃ vidhāya 
striyā sahāsane upaviśya śivanāmānamangiṃ dhyātvā āvāhya yathāśakti sampūjya 
hrīṃ viṣṇave svāhā hrīṃ bhāsvate svāhā hrīṃ dhātre svāhā ityāhutitrayaṃ 
dadyāt | 

tataḥ somaṃ dhyātvā hrīṃ somāya svāhā iti saptadhā juhuyāt | 

aśvinau vāsavaṃ viṣṇuṃ śivaṃ durgāñca prajāpatiñca dhyātvā pratyekaṃ 
pañcāhutīrdadyāt | 

tataḥ patiḥ svarṇakaṅkatikāṃ dakṣiṇahastena gṛhītvā striyāḥ 
sīmantādbaddhakeśāntapāśe niveśya śivaṃ viṣṇuṃ vidhiñca dhyāyan hrīṃ | 

bhāryye | 

<p. 162> kalyāṇi (!) subhage (!) daśame māsi suvrate | 

suprasūtā bhava prītā prasādādviśvakarmmaṇaḥ | 

āyuṣmati (!) kaṅkatikā varccasvī te śubhaṃ kuru || 

iti paṭhet || 

tataḥ sviṣṭikṛddhomaprāyaścittahomau kṛtvā dakṣiṇāmagnivisarjjanādikañca kuryyāditi 
sīmantonnayanam ||

atha jātakarmma | 

jātamātrabālakaṃ pitā svarṇaṃ datvā paśyet | 

tato gṛhāntare garbhādhānapaddhatyuktaṃ pañcadevatāpūjādidhārāhomāntaṃ karmma 
kṛtvā pañcāhutīrhṛdyāt | 

hrīmagnaye svāhā hrīmindrāya svāhā hrīṃ prajāpataye svāhā hrīṃ viśvebhyo 
devebhyaḥ svāhā hrīṃ brahmaṇe svāhā | 

ityebhirmantrairiti | 

tataḥ kāṃsyapātrasamāṃśena madhusarpiṣī samānīya tadupari aimiti saptadhā japtvā | 

hrīṃ | 

āyurvarcco balaṃ medhā varddhatāṃ te sadā śiśo (!) | 

iti mantreṇa dakṣiṇahastānāmikayā śiśuṃ prāśayet | 

ityāyurjananaṃ kṛtvā pitā guptaṃ nāma kuryyāt | 

tato janmadināvadhi tridinābhyantare śvetadūrvābhiḥ svarṇaśalākayā vā madhunā 
bālakasya jihvāyāṃ vāgbhavakūṭaṃ likhet | 

anena vāgbhavakūṭena bālakaḥ paṇḍitaḥ sukaviḥ śabdālaṅkāravicca bhavati | 

yadīmaṃ mantraṃ kṛtapuraścaraṇo mūrkhasya śirasi hastaṃ dattvā aṣṭottaraśataṃ 
japettadā so'pi ślokaṃ kariṣyati | 

yadi cemaṃ mūkasya jihvāyāṃ nyasettadā so'pi kavirbhavati | 

atha vā dvitīyāyāstryakṣarīṃ dūrvayā bālakasya jihvauṣṭhau sammārjya paṃktidvayena 
likhet | 

tatra kramaḥ | 

nāḍīcchedāt prāgekādaśāhe dvādaśāhe māsānte vā yathāśakti upacāreṇa tāṃ 
devatāṃ sampūjya mantraṃ likhet | 

yadi pitā na deśasthastadā pitṛvyādiretat kuryyāt | 

athavā śvetadūrvayā kuladevatāmantraṃ bālakasyauṣṭhe likhet | 

sa bālo vāgmī drutakavirbhavet | 

tataḥ sviṣṭikṛddhomādivahnivisarjjanāntaṃ karmma kuryyāt | 

kumāryyā apyevaṃ sarvamamantrakaṃ kuryyāt | 

<p. 163> tata utsāhapūrvakaṃ dhātrī nāḍī chindyāt | 

tadanantaramaśaucaṃ bhavatīti jātakarmma ||

atha nāmakaraṇam | 

ṣaṣṭhe'ṣṭame vā māsi prakāśato nāma kuryyāt | 

tatra mātā śiśuṃ snāpayitvā nave ambare paridhāpya patyuḥ pārśvaṃ 
vāmakroḍāsanamāgatya bālaṃ prāṅmukhaṃ sthāpayet | 

tataḥ pitā sahiraṇyakuśajalena śiśormūrddhānamabhiṣiñcedebhirmantrairyathā | 

hrīṃ jāhnavī yamunā revā sapavitrā sarasvatī | 

narmmadā varadā kumbhī sāgarāśca sarāṃsi ca | 

ete tvāmabhiṣiñcantu dharmmakāmārthasiddhaye | 

hrīṃ | 

āpohiṣṭhāmayo bhuvastā na ūrje dadhātana maheraṇāya cakṣase | 

yo vaḥ śivatamo rasastasya bhājayateha naḥ |

uśatīriva mātaraḥ | 

tasmā araṅgamāma vo yasya kṣayāya jinvatha | 

āpo janayathā ca naḥ | 

ityabhiṣicya pūrvavadvahnisthāpanādidhārāhomāntaṃ karmma kṛtvā 
pārthivanāmānamagniṃ dhyātvā svalpākṣaraṃ sukhoccāryaṃ nāma tridhā pitā 
śrāvayitvā brāhmaṇebhyo nivedayet | 

tataḥ sviṣṭikṛddhomāntaṃ karmma samāpayet | 

etat karma kanyāyā apyamantrakaṃ karttavyamti nāma karaṇam ||

atha niṣkramaṇam | 

niṣkramaṇantu caturthe māsi ṣaṣṭhe vā kuryyāt | 

tatra pitā kṛtanityakriyo gaṇeśaṃ sampūjya śiśuṃ snāpayitvā 
vastrālaṅkārairbhūṣayitvā agre saṃsthāpya hrīṃ brahmā 
viṣṇurityādipramāṇaprakaraṇoktaṃ paṭhitvā bālakamaṅke nidhāya gītavādyapuraḥsaraṃ 
sānandaiḥ svajanairvṛto vahirniṣkrāmayet | 

tataḥ kiyaddūraṃ gatvā hrīṃ taccakṣurdevahitamiti mantreṇa bālakaṃ sūryaṃ darśayet | 

tato gṛhamāgatya sūryyāyārghyaṃ dattvā svajanān bhojayediti niṣkramaṇam ||

athānnāprāśanam | 

tatra ṣaṣṭhe māsi aṣṭame vā pitṛbhrātā pitā vā annaprāśanakriyāṃ kuryyāt | 

<p. 164> tatra kṛtanityakriyaḥ pūrvavat sagaṇādhipagauryyādikaṃ sampūjya 
śucināmānamagniṃ saṃskṛtya prāguktadhārāhomāntaṃ karma samāpya 
pañcāhutīrjuhuyāt | 

yathā hrīṃ agnaye svāhā hrīṃ indrāya svāhā hrīṃ prajāpataye svāhā hrīṃ 
viśvebhyo devebhyaḥ svāhā hrīṃ brahmaṇe svāhā | 

iti hutvā tantrānyasmin gṛhe vā bālakaṃ kroḍe kṛtvā pañcaprāṇāhutimantraiḥ 
pāyasaṃ pañcadhā bhojayitvā śiśormukhe kiñcidannavyañjanādi dattvā ācāmayet | 

tato gītavādyapuraḥsaraṃ prāyaścittahomādi samāpayedityannaprāśanam ||

atha cūḍākaraṇam || 

tṛtīye varṣe pañcame kulācāraprāpte vā cūḍākaraṇam | 

tatra kṛtanityakriyaḥ sagaṇādhipamātṛkādipūjāṃ vidhāya satyanāmānamagniṃ 
saṃskṛtya dhārāhutyantaṃ karma samāpyāgneruttare tilagodhūmasahitaṃ 
vṛṣagomayapūrṇaṃ navaśarāvaṃ sthāpayet kavoṣṇaṃ jalaṃ śāṇita kṣurañca | 

tataḥ pitā svavāme jananīkroḍe bālakaṃ nidhāya vamiti daśadhā japtvā tena 
kavoṣṇatoyena keśān sammārjya hrīmiti mantreṇa kuśapatradvayanirmitajuṣṭikāmekāṃ  
tatra badhnīyāt | 

tato māyālakṣmīvījadvayaṃ tridhā japtvā lauhajaṃ kṣuraṃ gṛhītvā 
sajuṣṭikakeśamupamūlaṃ chindyāt | 

tataḥ kumāramātā taṃ sajuṣṭikakeśaṃ hastābhyāṃ gṛhītvā tasmin 
gomayānvitanavaśarāve sthāpayet | 

tataḥ pitā nāpitāya brūyāt | 

kṣuramuṇḍe śiśoḥ kṣauraṃ mukhaṃ sādhaya svāhā | 

iti paṭhitvā bālakaṃ paśyan satyanāmāgnau hrīṃ prajāpataye svāhā iti trivāraṃ 
juhuyāt | 

tataḥ kṛtakṣaura snātaṃ samalaṅkṛtaṃ samālyaṃ bālaṃ vāmabhāge saṃsthāpya 
sviṣṭikṛtaprāyaścittahomaṃ hutvā pūrṇāhutiṃ dadyāt | 

tato hrīṃ śiśo (!) te viśvakarmā kuśalaṃ kurutāmiti paṭhitvā svarṇamayyā rājatyā 
śalākayā vā śiśoḥ karṇadvaye vedhaṃ kalpayet | 

<p. 165> tata āpohiṣṭhetyādimantreṇa tamabhiṣicya śāntyādikaṃ kṛtvā dakṣiṇāṃ 
kuryyāditi cūḍākaraṇam ||

garbhādhānādicūḍāntaṃ karma sarvajātiṣu samānaṃ kevalaṃ śūdrāṇāmidaṃ 
sarvamamantrakamiti || 

athopanayanam || 

garbhāṣṭame'ṣṭame'bde vā upanayanaṃ karttavyamiti | 

ṣoḍaśābdaparyyantamanupanītasya bālasya daivapaitryakriyāyāṃ nādhikāraḥ || 

tatra kṛtanityakriyaḥ sagaṇādhipamātṛpūjādivṛddhiśrāddhāntaṃ karma kṛtvā 
kuśaṇḍikoktavidhānena samudbhavanāmānamagniṃ saṃsthāpya dhārāhomāntaṃ karma 
kṛtvā prātaḥ kṛtāśanaṃ śikhāṃ vinā muṇḍitaśirasaṃ susnātaṃ 
kṣaumāmbaradharaṃ svalaṅkṛtaṃ bālaṃ chāyāmaṇḍapamanīya vahiḥ samīpe svavāme 
śuddhāsane upaveśayet | 

tato hrīṃ brahmacaryyaṃ kuru iti guruḥ | 

hrī.ṃ brahmacaryyaṃ karomīti śiṣyo brūyāt | 

tato gurustasmai śiṣyāya kāṣāye vāsasī kuśamayīṃ mekhalāñca dadyāt | 

hrīṃ subhagā mekhalā śubhapradā syāditi paṭhitvā śiṣyo mauni tiṣṭhet |

hrīṃ yajñopavītamiti mantraṃ paṭhitvā guruḥ śiṣyāya kṛṣṇājinayuktayajñopavītaṃ 
dadyāt | 

vedabhede sūtramānantu pramāṇamadhye anusandheyam | 

punaryajñopavītamātraṃ vaṃśadaṇḍañca dadyāt pālāśañca | 

tato māyāpuṭitāpohiṣṭhādimantreṇa tripatrakuśena dhṛtakāyaṃ 
vastramekhalājinayajñasūtradaṇḍaṃ śiṣyamabhiṣicya toyena tadañjaliṃ pūrayet | 

tataḥ sūryyāyāñjaliṃ dāpayitvā bālaṃ hrīṃ taccakṣuriti mantreṇādityaṃ darśayet | 

tato gururmāṇavakahṛdayaṃ spṛṣṭvā hrīṃ mama vrate te iti mantraṃ paṭhitvā kiṃ 
nāmāsi iti pṛcchet | 

tataḥ śiṣyaḥ śrī(-)amukanāmāhaṃ tvāmabhivādaye iti vadet | 

tato guruḥ kasya tvaṃ brahmacārīti | 

śiṣyaḥ bhavato brahmacāryyaham | 

tato guruḥ | 

indrasya brahmacārī tvamācāryyaste hutāśanaḥ | 

ityuktvā hrīṃ prajāpataye vatsa iti mantreṇa māṇavakaṃ samarpya dakṣiṇāvarttayogena 
vahniṃ pradakṣiṇīkārayitvā āsane upaveśayet | 

<p. 166> tataḥ śiṣyeṇa spṛṣṭo guruḥ hrīṃ prajāpataye svāhā | 

hrīṃ indrāya svāhā | 

hrīṃ viṣṇave svāhā | 

hrīṃ brahmaṇe svāhā | 

hrīṃ śivāyaṃ svāhā | 

tato māyādisvāhāntena nāmnā durgālakṣmīsundarī(-)
bhuvaneśvarīndrādidaśadikpālabhāskarādinavagrahebhyaṃ svasvanāmnā pratyekaṃ juhuyāt 
| 

tato māṇavakaṃ vāsasā ācchādya ko vāśramaste tanaya (!) brūhi kinte manogatam | 

tataḥ śiṣyo gurupādadvayaṃ dhṛtvā karotu māmāśramiṇaṃ brahmavidyopadeśataḥ | 

iti prārthayamānasya śiṣyasya dakṣakarṇe tridhā praṇavaṃ saṃśrāvya savyāhṛtikāṃ 
sāvitrīṃ śrāvayet | 

anyābrahmavidyā | 

gāyatryāḥ sadāśiva ṛṣiranuṣṭupchandaḥ sāvitrī devatā mīkṣārthe viniyogaḥ | 

tadarthañca jñāpayet | 

tato māṇavako bhikṣāṃ kuryyāt | 

tato guruḥ hrīṃ brahmacāryyuktaveśamiti paṭhet | 

tato māṇavakaḥ kaṣāyavastraṃ sakṛṣṇājinayajñasūtraṃ mekhalāṃ daṇḍaṃ 
bhikṣākaraṇḍamarjitabhikṣāñca gurave nivedya śuddhayajñopavītadvayaṃ 
śuklavastradvayañca paridhāya dhṛtagandhamālya ācāryyasannidhau tūṣṇīṃ tiṣṭhet | 

tato guruḥ | 

hrīṃ jitendriyaḥ satyavādītyādi paṭhitvā śiṣyeṇa hāvayet | 

yathā |

hrīṃ oṃ bhūḥ svāhā hrīṃ oṃ bhuvaḥ svāhā hrīṃ oṃ svaḥ svāhā ityanena | 

tataḥ sviṣṭikṛddhomādidakṣiṇāntaṃ karma samāpayedityupanayanam ||

atha vivāhaḥ | 

tatra kṛtanityakriyaḥ pañcadevatāpūjādivṛddhiśrādhāntaṃ karma kṛtvā rātrau 
gītavādyapuraḥsaraṃ varaṃ chāyāmaṇḍapamānīya pūrvābhimukhamāsane 
samupaveśayet | 

tato brāhmaṇaiḥ puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā hrīṃ sādhubhavānāstāmiti varaṃ pṛcchet | 

tato varaḥ | 

hrīṃ sādhvahamāse iti vadet | 

hrīṃ arccayiṣyāmo bhavantamiti varaṃ pṛcchet | 

hrīmarccaya iti varo vadet | 

hrīṃ pādyaṃ samarpayāmītyādinā 
pādyārghyācamanīyagandhapuṣpavastrālaṅkārayajñasūtrair(-)varamabhyarcya hrīṃ 
madhuparkaṃ samarpayāmīti varahaste madhuparkaṃ samarpayet | 

<p. 167> tato varo madhuparkaṃ gṛhītvā vāmahaste nidhāya 
dakṣiṇahastānāmikāṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ hrīṃ prāṇāya svāhā hrīṃ apānāya svāhā 
hrīṃ samānāya svāhā hrīṃ udānāya svāhā hrīṃ vyānāya svāhā iti mantreṇa 
tanmadhuparkadravyaṃ pañcadhā āghnāya tat pātramudīcyāṃ diśi sthāpayet | 

tato varāya punarācamanīyaṃ dattvā dātā dūrvākṣatābhyāṃ varasya dakṣiṇaṃ jānu 
spṛṣṭvā viṣṇurom tatsadadya amuke māsi amukarāśisthe bhāskare amuke pakṣe 
amukatithau amukagotraḥ śrīamukadevaśarmā 
amukagotrasyāmukapravarasyāmukadevaśarmaṇaḥ prapautraṃ 
punargotrādyullikhyāmukadevaśarmaṇaḥ pautraṃ punargotrādyullikhyāmukadevaśarmmaṇaḥ 
putraṃ punargotrādidvitīyāntamullikhya śrī(-)amukadevaśarmmāṇamarccitaṃ varam | 

evaṃ gotrapravaranāmaṣaṣṭhyantaṃ prapitāmahapitāmahapitṝṇāmuktvā yathākramaṃ 
prapautrīṃ pautrīṃ putrīñcoktvā amukagotrāmamukapravarāṃ śrīamukīdevīṃ 
brāhmavivāhena dātuṃ bhavantamahaṃ vṛṇe iti vadet | 

varastu hrīṃ vṛto'smīti vadet | 

tadā yathāvihitaṃ vivāhakarma kuru | 

varaḥ hrīṃ yathājñānataḥ karavāṇīti | 

tataḥ savastrālaṅkārāṃ kanyāṃ chāyāmaṇḍapamānīya varasammukhe sthāpayet | 

tato varahaste kanyāhastaṃ niyojya phalaratnādikaṃ dattvā prāgvat 
sauramāsādyullekhānantaraṃ prapitāmahapitāmahapitṝṇāṃ 
gotrapravaranāmaṣaṣṭhyantamullikhya yathākramaṃ prapautrāya pautrāya putrāya ityuktvā 
amukagotrāya amukapravarāya śrī(-)amukadevaśarmmaṇe varāya ityuccāryya kanyāyāśca 
prapitāmahapitāmahapitṝṇāṃ ṣaṣṭhyantaṃ gotrapravaranāmollikhya prapautrīṃ pauttrīṃ 
puttrīmiti yathāsambhavamuccāryya amukagotrāmamukapravarāṃ śrīamukīdevīṃ 
kanyāmityantaṃ triruccāryya enāmarcitāṃ sācchādanālaṅkārāṃ 
prajāpatidevatāmamukakāmastubhyamahaṃ sampradade | 

iti varāya dadyāt | 

<p. 168> varaḥ svastīti svīkuryyāt | 

tataḥ sampradātā dharme cārthe ca kāme ca bhavatā bhāryyayā saha varttitavyam | 

varo vāḍhamityuktvā kāmastutiṃ paṭhet | 

tataḥ sampradātā kanyājāmātarau hrīṃ prajāpati prasādenetyādi śrāvayitvā 
vastreṇācchādya sumaṅgalapuraḥsarairvarakanyayoḥ parasparamukhāvalokaṃ kārayet | 

tataḥ kāñcanādikaṃ varāya dakṣiṇāṃ dattvā acchidrāvadhāraṇaṃ kuryyāt | 

tato varastadrātrau paradivase vā kuśaṇḍikoktavidhānena yojakanāmāgniṃ saṃshāpya 
prājāpatyaṃ caruṃ śrapayet | 

tato dhārāhomāntaṃ karma kṛtvā hrīṃ śivāya svāhā | 

hrīṃ durgāyai svāhā hrīṃ viṣṇave svāhā hrīṃ brahmaṇe svāhā hrīṃ indrāya 
svāhā iti juhuyāt | 

tato hrīṃ pāṇiṃ gṛhṇāmīti mantraṃ paṭhitvā bhāryyāyāḥ pāṇiṃ gṛhṇīyāt | 

tataḥ kanyā hastasthena svāmidattena ghṛtena bhrātrādyāhṛtalājaiḥ prajāpatimuddiśya 
hrīṃ prajāpataye svāhā iti caturdhā juhuyāt | 

tato bhāryyayā saha utthāya vahniṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya hrīṃ durgāśivābhyāṃ svāhā 
hrīṃ brāhmībrahmabhyāṃ svāhā | 

iti tridhā hutvā aśmārohaṇasaptapadīgamane amantrakaṃ kārayet | 

rātrau ceddhomastadā strībhiḥ saha candramarundhatīñca paśyet || 

tataḥ sviṣṭikṛddhomādidakṣiṇāntaṃ karmma samāpayet || 

iti vivāhaḥ ||

[iti śrīprāṇatoṣiṇyāṃ dvitīye dharmakāṇḍe saṃskārarūpagranthikathanaṃ nāma 
prathamaḥ paricchedaḥ] ||




vakṣyamāṇavacanairdīkṣāyā api saṃskārarūpatvāt saṃskāramadhye vaktavyatvamucitam 
| 

tasyā gurumūlatayā tvādau guruprakaraṇamucyate | 

tatra gurulakṣaṇaṃ śārādāyāṃ dvitīyapaṭale || 

mātṛtaḥ pitṛtaḥ śuddhaḥ śuddhabhāvo jitendriyaḥ | 

sarvāgamānāṃ sārajñaḥ sarvaśāstrārthatattvavit | 

paropakāranirato japapūjāditatparaḥ | 

amoghavacanaḥ śānto vedavedāṅgapāragaḥ | 

yogamārgārthasandhāyī devatāhṛdayaṅgamaḥ | 

<p. 169> ityādiguṇasampanno gururāgamasammataḥ | 

devatāvat hṛdayaṅgamo manohara ityarthaḥ || 

viśvasāratantre dvitīyapaṭale'pi || 

sarvaśāstraparo dakṣaḥ sarvaśāstrārthavit sadā | 

suvacāḥ sundaraḥ svāṅgaḥ kulīnaḥ śubhadarśanaḥ | 

jitendriyaḥ satyavādī brāhmaṇaḥ śāntamānasaḥ | 

pitṛmātṛhite yuktaḥ sarvakarmaparāyaṇaḥ | 

āśramī deśasthāyī ca gururevaṃ vidhīyate || 

āśramī gṛhasthaḥ | 

tathāca matsyasūkte mahātantre trayodaśapaṭale || 

madhyadeśasamudbhūtaḥ śāntaḥ sarvaguṇairyutaḥ | 

putradāraiśca sampanno gururāgamasammataḥ || 

madhyadeśasamudbhūta iti śreṣṭhatvadarśanārtham | 

tatpramāṇantu tantrasārakāreṇaiva likhitamato'tra na prayojanamiti gurulakṣaṇam || 

rudrajāmale pūrvakhaṇḍīyapañcadaśapaṭalottarakhaṇḍīyadvitīyapaṭalayoḥ | 

ādau sādhakadevaśca sadācāramatiḥ sadā | 

paśubhāvastato vīraḥ sāyāhne divyabhāvavān | 

eteṣāṃ bhāvavargāṇāṃ gururvedāntapāragaḥ | 

śānto dāntaḥ kulīnaśca vinītaḥ śuddhaveśavān | 

śuddhācāraḥ supratiṣṭhaḥ śucirdakṣaḥ subuddhimān | 

āśramī dhyāniṣṭhaśca mantratantraviśāradaḥ | 

nigrahānugrahe śakto vaśī mantrārthajāpakaḥ || 

śānta ityādyetat sarvaṃ rāmārccanacandrikāyāmapi kevalaṃ śeṣacaraṇe vaśītyādau 
gururityabhidhīyata iti pāṭhaḥ || 

śānto viṣayotkaṭecchārahitaḥ śamādiguṇasaṃyuktaḥ | 

śamastu śravaṇādikavyatiriktaviṣayebhyo manaso nigrahaḥ | 

yathā tīvrāyāṃ bubhukṣāyāṃ jātāyāṃ bhojanādanyo vyāpāro manasi na rocate | 

bhojanavilambañca sahate tathā 
srakcandanādiviṣayeṣvatyantamabhirucistattvajñānamādhaneṣu 
śravaṇamananādiṣvatyantamabhiruciśca jāyate iti | 

śāntarūpeṇa prasiddho vā | 

dāntastapaḥkleśasahaḥ damayukto vā | 

damastu vāhyendriyāṇāṃ tadvyatiriktaviṣayebhyo nivarttanam | 

jñānasādhanaśravaṇādisādhanebhyo vilakṣaṇeṣu śabdādiviṣayeṣu pravarttamānāni 
śrotrādīni vāhyendriyāṇi yena vṛttiviśeṣeṇa nivarttante sa dama ityabhiprāyaḥ | 

<p. 170> ata   eva śānto'ntarindriyanigrahakarttā dānto vahirindriyanigrahakarttā iti 
vedāntavidbhiruktam || 

vṛhannīlamantre tṛtīyapaṭale'pi | 

vahirindriyaharttā ca guruḥ sarvatra durlabhaḥ | 

antarindriyaharttā ca guruḥ sarvatra śobhanaḥ | 

ācāravānmahāvidyārādhaniṣṭhaśca tatparaḥ | 

tatkalpācārasaṃsakto gururityabhidhīyatra iti | 

kulīnaḥ kaulaḥ | 

eteṣāmityādivacanānyadṛṣṭvā kulīnaḥ śuddhajanmā ācārādinavaguṇaśālī veti kaścit 
svabuddhyā vyācaṣṭe etadatīva mandam | 

vakṣyamāṇasārasaṃgrahavacane viśuddhamātāpitṛkakulīneti dvayorupādānāt | 

yenaivaṃ vyākhyāyate tasya sandehastu viṣṇumantropadeṣṭari kulācārābhāva iti | 

sa tu sandehaḥ kāmākhyākulārṇavarudrajāmalādīnāmadraṣṭureva sphurati | 

tatra tu sauragāṇapatyaśaivavaiṣṇavaśāktānāṃ sarveṣāṃ kulāvāropadeśasya 
suspaṣṭatvāditi tatpramāṇamagre likhitavyamiti | 

gurukaraṇe varjanīyaprakaraṇe śyāvadantaṃ kulācārarahitamityādipañcācāravivarjitamiti ca 
vakṣyamāṇaṃ tena na dṛṣṭamiti | 

ata eva kulīnaḥ sarvamantrāṇāmadhikārīti gīyate | 

dīkṣāprabhuḥ sa evātmā sarvamantrasya nāparaḥ | 

iti tantrasāradhṛtaṃ sādhu saṅgacchate || 

kāmākhyātanre tṛtīyapaṭale'pi || 

śānto dāntaḥ kulīnaśca śuddhāntaḥkaraṇastathā | 

pañcatattvātmako yastu sadguruḥ sa prakīrtitaḥ | 

siddho'sāviti vikhyāto bandhubhiḥ śiṣyapālakaḥ | 

camatkārī daivaśaktyā sadguruḥ kathitaḥ priye (!) | 

aśrutaṃ sambhṛtaṃ vakti vākyaṃ sādhu manoharam | 

tantramantraṃ samāvetti ya eva sadguruśca saḥ || 

mahānirvāṇatantre daśamollāse ca || 

śākte śākto guruḥ śastaḥ śaive śivo gururmataḥ | 

vaṣṇave vaiṣṇavaḥ saure sauro gururudāhṛtaḥ | 

gāṇape gāṇapaḥ khyātaḥ kaulaḥ sarvatra sadguruḥ | 

ataḥ sarvātmanā dhīmān kaulāddīkṣāṃ samācaret || 

<p. 171>  nānāvikalpavibhrāntirnāśamityādisadgurulakṣaṇaṃ vakṣyate | 

iti sadgurulakṣaṇam || 

tathā || 

paśorvaktrāllabdhamantraḥ paśurevaṃ na saṃśayaḥ | 

vīrāllabdhamanurvīraḥ kaulācca brahmavidbhavet || 

vṛhannīlatantre'pi || 

śaivo'pi paravidyānāmupadeṣṭā na saṃśayaḥ | 

vaiṣṇavaḥ svamatasthānāṃ sauraḥ sauravidāṃ satām || 

gāṇapatyastu deveśi (!) gaṇadīkṣāpravarttakaḥ | 

śaive śākte ca sarvatra dīkṣāsvāmī na saṃśayaḥ | 

kaulastasmāt prayatnena kulīnaṃ gurumāśrayet || 

iti kulīnagurupraśaṃsā || 

nanu rudrajāmalīyottarakhaṇḍīyaṣaṣṭhapaṭaloktena | 

bhāvastu trividho deva (!) divyavīrapaśukramāt | 

guravastividhāścātra tathaiva mantradevatāḥ | 

ityanena paśuguruviṣayakametadvyākhyādīnāṃ vāmasiddhāntakulamārgānusāriṇāṃ 
vacanairāmārccanacandrikādivacane pāribhāṣikaṃ kulīnamaṅgīkṛtya kathametādṛg 
vyākhyānaṃ karttavyamiti cenna || 

mahānirvāṇatantre kaulasya sarvamantropadeṣṭatvasadgurutvayoḥ spaṣṭamuktatvāt | 

etena śāntyā diguṇavattve sati abhiśastatvādidoṣarahitatvaṃ sadgurutvamiti 
sadgurulakṣaṇamarvācīnaiḥ kṛtamapyapāstam | 

kaulasyaiva sadgurutvenoktatvāt | 

atha kulīnaḥ śuddhavaṃśajanmā ācārādinavaguṇaśālīva 
itītaralokaprasiddhakulīnavyākhyānena ko doṣa iti cenna | 

tantraśāstre kūṭārthakalpanāyā nindāśruteḥ | 

yathā mahānirvāṇatantre ekādaśollāse || 

etasmin śāmbhave śāstre vyaktārthapadavṛṃhite | 

kūṭenārthaṃ kalpayantaḥ patitā yāntyadhogatim || 

rudrajāmale uttarakhaṇḍe dvitīyapaṭale || 

nīrogī nirahaṅkāro vikārarahito mahān | 

tapasvī satyavādī ca sadā dhyānaparāyaṇaḥ | 

āgamārthaviśiṣṭajño nijadharmaparāyaṇaḥ | 

avyaktaliṅgacihnastho bhāvako bhadradānavān | 

lakṣmīmān dhṛtimānnātho gururityabhidhīyate || 

gurunāthācāryyaśabdānāṃ vyutpattistu kulārṇave pañcamakhaṇḍe saptadaśollāse | 

guhyāgamārthatattvānusandhānādbodhanādapi | 

<p. 172> rudrādidevarūpatvādgururinyabhidhīyate | 

śrīmokṣajñānadātṛtvānnādabrahmātmabodhanāt | 

sthagitajñānacihnatvāt śrīnāthaḥ kathitaḥ priye (!) | 

svayamācarate śiṣyānācāre sthāpayatyapi | 

ācinoti hi śāstrārthānācāryyastena kathyate | 

carācarasamāsannamadhyāpayati yaḥ svayam | 

yamādiyogasiddhatvādācāryya iti kathyate || 

śāktānandataraṅgiṇīdhṛtasārasaṃgrahe || 

viśuddhamātāpitṛko jitendriyaḥ sarvāgamajñaḥ paraduḥkhakātaraḥ | 

yathārthavāgvedavidaṅgapāragaḥ śāntaḥ kulīno gururīrito dvijaḥ || 

dvija ityupādānānnānyebhyo mantragrahaṇam | 

anācāro dvijo yastu varṇānāṃ gurureva saḥ || 

iti vacanāt | 

sa ityanenaivakāraḥ sambadhyate | 

sa eva gururityanena jātyantaraṃ vyavacchidyate | 

guruśabdenaivakāraḥ sambadhyate vyāvṛtterabhāvāt || 

kulamūlāvatārakalpasūtraṭīkādhṛtanavacakreśvare || 

śivarūpaṃ samādāya bhavapāśanivṛttaye | 

siddhāntasāravettāhaṃ siddhāntasārabhedakaḥ | 

avicchinnāpramattaśca sadānugrahanigrahe | 

śivodivyākṛtirdevi (!) na ca dṛggocaro'pyaham | 

tathā śrīgururūpeṇa śiṣyān śāsyāmi pārvati (!) | 

paraṃ saṃvittijananaṃ parānandasamudbhavam | 

tattattvaṃ viditaṃ yena sa guruḥ kulanāyike (!) | 

bhūtabhavyau mantratantrau śāmbhavaṃ vetti yaḥ sadā | 

vedhasya ṣaḍvidhaṃ devi (!) sa guruḥ kulanāyike (!) | 

varṇaṃ kalāṃ padaṃ tattvaṃ mantraṃ bhuvanameva ca | 

bodhayedyaḥ ṣaḍadhvānaṃ sa guruḥ kulanāyike (!) | 

ṣaḍādhāraṃ navadvāraṃ ṣoḍaśādhāranirṇayam | 

yo jānāti vidhānena sa guruḥ kulanāyike (!) | 

jāgratsvapnasuṣuptiñca rūpañca tadatītakam | 

yo vetti pañcakaṃ devi (!) sa guruḥ kulānāyike (!) || 

sammohanatantre prathamapaṭale'pi || 

ṣaṭcakraṃ ṣoḍaśādhāraṃ trilakṣaṃ vyomapañcakam | 

svadehe yo vijānāti sa guruḥ kathito budhaiḥ || 

krameṇaitāni vyākhyāyante | 

<p. 173> yaḥ ṣaḍadhvānaṃ śiṣyaṃ bodhayet sa gururityanvayaḥ | 

adhvānamāha varṇamityādi | 

etān ṣaḍadhvano dīkṣāprakaraṇe spaṣṭīkariṣye | 

ṣaḍādhāraṃ ṣaṭcakrāṇi pṛthivyādīnāmādhārarūpatvāt | 

navadvāraṃ dṛkśrotrāsyanāsikāguhyaliṅgacchidrarūpam | 

uktañca sammohanatantre dvitīyapaṭale'pi | 

navadvāraṃ navacchidraṃ puraṃ vapuḥ prakīrttītam | 

navapadmāṇi cakrāṇi trisūtraṃ nāḍikātrayamiti | 

ṣoḍaśādhārasvarūpamapi tatraiva || 

mūlādhārasvādhiṣṭhānaṃ maṇipūramanāhatam | 

viśuddhamājñācakrañca bindurbhūyaḥ kalāpadam | 

nibodhikā tathārddhendurnādo nādānta eva ca | 

unmanī viṣṇuvaktrañca dhruvamaṇḍalikā tataḥ | 

ityetat ṣoḍaśādhāraṃ kathitaṃ yogidurlabhamiti | 

vidhānena prayatnena prāṇāyāmādineti yāvat | 

jāgratsvapnasuṣuptimiti buddherāvarttatrayarūpam | 

bindustadatītaṃ nirañjanamityagre spaṣṭībhaviṣyati | 

trilakṣādikamapi tatraiva svayambhūrvāṇa itarastrilakṣaṃ parikīrttitam | 

pṛthivyādīni bhūtāni kathitaṃ vyomapañcakamiti | 

tatra svayambhūliṅgaṃ mūlādhāre vāṇaliṅgamanāhate | 

itaramājñācakre || 

navacakreśvare || 

piṇḍaṃ padaṃ tathā rūpaṃ rūpātītaṃ catuṣṭayam | 

yo vā samyag vijānāti sa guruḥ parikīrtitaḥ | 

piṇḍādicatuṣṭayaṃ spaṣṭīkṛtaṃ gurugītāyām | 

piṇḍaṃ kuṇḍalinīśaktiḥ padaṃ haṃsaḥ prakīrttitaḥ | 

rūpaṃ binduriti jñeyaṃ rūpātītaṃ nirañjanam || 

navacakreśvare || 

yo vā parāñca paśyantīṃ madhyamāmapi vaikharīm | 

catuṣṭayaṃ vijānāti sa guruḥ parikīrttitaḥ || 

iti guruviśeṣalakṣaṇam || 

rudrajāmale uttarakhaṇḍe prathamapaṭale || 

gurumūlaṃ jagat sarvaṃ gurumūlaṃ paraṃ tapaḥ | 

guroḥ prasādamātreṇa mokṣamāpnoti sadvaśī || 

muṇḍamālātantra prathamapaṭale || 

gururekaḥ śivaḥ sākṣāt guruḥ sarvārthasādhakaḥ | 

gurureva paraṃ tattvaṃ sarvaṃ gurumayaṃ jagat | 

vinā guruprasādena koṭipuraścaraṇena kim | 

<p. 174> piñchilātantre pūrvakhaṇḍe prathamapaṭale'pi | 

gurumūlamidaṃ śāstraṃ nānyaḥ śivatamaḥ prabhuḥ | 

ata(-)eva maheśāni (!) yatnato gurumāśrayet || 

iti gurumāhātmyam || 

rudrajāmale pūrvakhaṇḍe caturdaśapaṭale || 

guruṃ vinā yastu mūḍhaḥ pustakādivilokanāt | 

japabandhaṃ samāpnoti kilviṣaṃ parameśvari (!) | 

na mātā na pitā bhrātā tasya ko vā gatiḥ priye (!) | 

gurureko varārohe (!) pāpaṃ nāśayati kṣaṇāt | 

guruṃ vinā yatastantre nādhikāraḥ kathañcana | 

ata   eva prayatnena guruḥ karttavya uttamaḥ || 

iti śāstre gurorāvaśyakatvam | 

jāmale || 

gururekaḥ śivaḥ proktaḥ so'haṃ devi (!) na saṃśayaḥ | 

gurustvamapi deveśi (!) mantro'pi gururucyate | 

ato mantre gurau deve na hi bhedaḥ prajāyate | 

kadācit sasahasrāre padme dhyeyo guruḥ sadā | 

kadācit hṛdayāmbhoje kadācidṛṣṭigocare || 

muṇḍamālātantre || 

devatāgurumantrāṇāmaikyaṃ sambhāvayan dhiyā | 

tadā siddho bhavenmantraḥ prakaṭe hānireva ca || 

pārvatyuvāca || 

aikyajñānaṃ mahādeva (!) kathamutpadyate prabho (!) | 

narākṛtiṃ guruṃ manye devatā dhyānarūpiṇī | 

mantraścākṣararūpo hi kathamaikyaṃ bhavecchiva (!) || 

śiva uvāca || 

ekajātisvarūpeṇa svabhāvādekajanmataḥ | 

eteṣāṃ bhāvayoge tu ekasādhanameva hi | 

gurorjātaśca mantraśca mantrājjātā tu devatā | 

ata   eva varārohe (!) devatāyāḥ pitāmahaḥ | 

pituśca bhāvanāddevi (!) yathā caiva pituḥ pituḥ |

tadudbhavastoṣameti viparīte viparyyayaḥ || 

ayamabhiprāyaḥ | 

ekajātisvarūpeṇeti nirākārasvarūpeṇeti tātparyyam || 

tathā hi mahākālasyaiva gurutvena vakṣyamāṇatvānmantrasya dhvanimayatvāddevatāyā 
ākārasya kalpitatvācca trayāṇāṃ nirākāratvamavagantavyam | 

ekaprakārasādhanatveneti vā | 

gurorjātaśca mantraścetyanena yathā putrapautrābhyāṃ pituraṃśajatvenaikyaṃ tathā 
gurumantradevatānāmiti mantatrayeṇaikyaṃ darśitam || 

yadvāmātṛkābhedatantre daśamapaṭale || 

<p. 175> śrīdevyuvāca || 

narākṛtiṃ guruṃ nātha (!) mantravarṇātmakaṃ sadā | 

dhyānānurūpiṇaṃ devamekatvaṃ vā kathaṃ vada || 

śrīśiva uvāca || 

guruvaktrānmahāmantro labhyate sādhakottamaiḥ | 

yaddevājjāyate vījastasya muktirbhaveddhruvam | 

devatāyāḥ śarīraṃ hi vījādutpadyate priye (!) | 

gurorājñānusāreṇa cānyamūrttistu jāyate | 

gurvādibhāvanāddevi (!) bhāvasiddhiḥ prajāyate | 

ata   eva maheśāni (!) caikatvaṃ parikathyate || 

muṇḍamālātantre || 

mantre vā gurudeve vā na bhedaṃ yastu kalpate | 

tasya tuṣṭā jagaddhātrī kinna dadyāddine dine || 

iti gurumantradevatānāmekatvaprakārakathanam || 

gurutantre || 

guruḥ kartā gururhartā guruḥ pātā mahītale | 

gurusantoṣamātreṇa tuṣṭāḥ syuḥ sarvadevatāḥ | 

vāmakeśvaratantre ekapañcāśatpaṭale'pyetadvacanam | 

kintu pātetyatra gururdāteti pāṭhaḥ | 

gurau tuṣṭe śivastuṣṭo ruṣṭe ruṣṭastrilocanaḥ | 

gurau tuṣṭe śivā tuṣṭā ruṣṭe ruṣṭā ca sundarī | 

ato gururmaheśāni (!) saṃsārārṇavalaṅghane | 

karttā pātā ca harttā ca gururmokṣapradāyakaḥ || 

iti gurutuṣṭyādiphalam || 

śāktānandataraṅgiṇīdhṛtarudrajāmale | 

gakāraḥ siddhidaḥ prokto rephaḥ pāpasya dāhakaḥ | 

ukāraḥ śambhurityuktastritayātmā guruḥ smṛtaḥ || 

kaṅkālamālinītantre prathamapaṭale || 

nirguṇañca paraṃbrahma gururityakṣaradvayam | 

mahāmantraṃ mahādevi (!) gopanīyaṃ parātparam || 

gurutantre || 

gururityakṣaraṃ yasya jihvāgre devi (!) varttate | 

tasya kiṃ vidyate mohaḥ pāṭhairvedasya kiṃ vṛthā | 

gakāroccāraṇamātreṇa brahmahatyā vyapohati | 

ukāroccāramātreṇa mucyate janmapātakāt | 

rephoccāraṇamātreṇa ukāroccāraṇāt punaḥ | 

visargoccāraṇāt koṭijanmajaṃ pātakaṃ haret | 

gururityakṣaraṃ devi (!) japato mama niścitam |

brahmahatyā purā muktā satyametanna saṃśayaḥ | 

paraśurāmo mātṛbadhāt devendro brahmahiṃsanāt || 

pātakādapi mukto'bhūt guroruccāramātrataḥ || 

<p. 176> iti guruśabdoccāraṇaphalam || 

tatraiva || 

brahmā viṣṇuśca rudraśca pārvatī parameśvarī | 

indrādayastathā devā yakṣādyāḥ pitṛdevatāḥ | 

gaṅgādyāḥ saritaḥ sarvā gandharvāḥ sarpajātayaḥ | 

sthāvarā jaṅgamāścānye parvatāḥ sārvabhautikāḥ | 

ete cānye ca tiṣṭhanti nityaṃ gurukalevare | 

śrīgurostṛptimātreṇa tṛptireṣāñca jāyate || 

iti gurau tṛpte jagattṛptikathanam || 

tatraiva || 

na guroradhikaṃ śāstraṃ na gororadhikaṃ tapaḥ |

na guroradhiko mantro na guroradhikaṃ phalam | 

na guroradhikā devi na guroradhikaḥ śivaḥ | 

na guroradhikā mūrttirna guroradhiko japaḥ || 

viśvasāratantrīyagurugītāyām || 

gurormūrtteḥ sadā dhyānaṃ gurostattvaṃ sadā japet | 

kāśīkṣetraṃ nivāso'sya jāhnavī caraṇodakam | 

gururviśveśvaraḥ sākṣāt tārakaṃ brahma niścitam | 

tathā | 

dhyānamūlaṃ gurormūrtiḥ pūjāmūlaṃ guroḥ padam | 

mantramūlaṃ gurorvākyaṃ siddhimūlaṃ guroḥ kṛpā | 

tathā | 

munibhiḥ pannagairvāpi surairvā śāpito yadi | 

kālamṛtyubhayāccā(?)pi gurūrakṣati pārvati (!) || 

guptasādhanatantre dvitīyapaṭale || 

gururbrahmā gururviṣṇurgururdevo maheśvaraḥ | 

gurustīrthaṃ gururyajño gururdānaṃ gurustapaḥ | 

gururagnirguruḥ sūryyaḥ sarvaṃ gurumayaṃ jagat || 

iti sarvebhyo gurorādhikyakathanam || 

kiṃ dānena kiṃ tapasā kimanyattīrthasevayā | 

śrīgurorarccitau yena pādau tenārccitaṃ jagat | 

brahmāṇḍabhāṇḍamadhye tu yāni tīrthāni santi vai | 

guroḥ pādatale tāni nivasanti hi santatam | 

ityanena gurupādārccanavaśāt viśvapūjāsiddhiḥ spaṣṭamavagamyate || 

iti gurupūjane jagat pūjanasiddhikathanam || 

piñchilātantre || 

gurustu dvividhaḥ prokto dīkṣāśikṣāprabhedataḥ | 

ādau dīkṣāguruḥ proktaḥ śeṣe śikṣāgururmataḥ | 

yanmukhāttu mahāmantraḥ śrūyate'bhyasyate'pi vā | 

sa guruḥ paramo jñeyastadājñā siddhidāyinī | 

kulamūlāvatārakalpasūtraṭīkādhṛtakulāgame || 

<p. 177> prerakaḥ sūcakaścaiva vācako darśakastathā | 

śikṣako bodhakaścaiva ṣaḍete guravaḥ smṛtāḥ | 

pañcaite kāryyabhūtā syuḥ kāraṇaṃ bodhako bhavet || 

iti gurubhedakathanam | 

tahā | 

tattvajñairupadiṣṭā ye tattvajñāste na saṃśayaḥ | 

paśubhiścopadiṣṭā ye devi (!) te paśavaḥ smṛtāḥ | 

abhijñaścoddharenmūrkhaṃ na mūrkho mūrkhamuddharet | 

śilāṃ santārayellauho na śilā tārayecchilām || 

ata(-)eva gurutantre || 

guravo bahavaḥ santi śiṣyavitāpahārakāḥ | 

tamekaṃ durlabhaṃ manye śiṣyahṛttāpanāśakam | 

ekaḥ śreṣṭho bhavetteṣāṃ bhuktimuktipradāyakaḥ | 

iti tattvajñagurukaraṇavījam || 

yoginītantre pūrvakhaṇḍe prathamapaṭale || 

śrīdevyuvāca || 

guruḥ ko vāṃ maheśāna (!) vada me karuṇāmaya (!) | 

tvatto'pyadhika evāyaṃ gurustvayā prakīrttitaḥ | 

śrī(-)īśvara uvāca || 

ādinātho mahādevi (!) mahākālo hi yaḥ smṛtaḥ | 

guruḥ sa eva deveśi (!) sarvamantreṣu nāparaḥ | 

śaive śākte vaiṣṇave ca gāṇapatye tathaindave | 

mahāśaive ca saure ca sa gururnātra saṃśayaḥ | 

mantravaktā sa eva syānnāparaḥ parameśvari (!) | 

mantrapradānakāle hi mānuṣe girinandini (!) | 

adhiṣṭhānaṃ bhavettasya mahākālasya śaṅkari (!) | 

ato na gurutā devi (!) mānuṣe nātra saṃśayaḥ | 

mantradātā śivaḥ padme yaddhyānaṃ kurute guroḥ | 

taddhyānanaṃ kurute devi (!) śiṣyo'pi śīrṣaśaṅkaje |

taddhyānaṃ śiṣyaśirasi copadiṣṭaṃ na cānyathā | 

iti kāmākhyātantre tṛtīyapaṭale śeṣārddham || 

ata(-)eva maheśāni (!) eka eva guruḥ smṛtaḥ | 

adhiṣṭhānavaśāttasya mānuṣasya maheśvari (!) | 

māhātmyaṃ kīrttitaṃ tasya sarvaśāstreṣu śaṅkari (!) || 

kāmākhyātantre  tṛtīyapaṭale'pi || 

ata(-)eva maheśāni (!) kṛto hi mānuṣo guruḥ | 

mānuṣe gurutā devi (!) kalpanā na tu mukhyataḥ | 

mokṣo na jāyate devi (!) mānuṣe gurubhāvanāditi || 

tathā | 

ata(-)eva gururnaiva manujaḥ kintu kalpanā | 

dīkṣādau sādhakānāñca vṛkṣādau pūjanaṃ yathā | 

<p. 178> nanu yadi mānuṣaścenna gurustadā | 

avidyo vā savidyo vā guruḥ paramadaivatam | 

ityāditantrasāradhṛtavacanasya kā gatiriti cet satyaṃ prāguktavṛkṣādivanmānanīyatvenoktam 
| 

na tu vastutastathātve vakṣyamāṇagurau manuṣyabuddhītyādivacanaṃ gurau 
manuṣyatābuddhimityāditantrasārakāradhṛtañca na saṅgacchate | 

ghaṭasya chidravraṇādidoṣavanmahāpātakādidoṣo manuṣyasya iti sudhībhirdhyeyam || 

gurutantre'pi || 

eka eva gururdevi (!) sarvatra parigīyate | 

bhedastasya na karttavyaḥ sarvaṃ gurumayaṃ jagat || 

iti vacanādgurornityatvena gurumaraṇe trirātrāśaucamiti vadanto mūrkhā eva | 

aśaucādeḥ smṛtyuktatvena gurumaraṇāśaucaṃ smṛtyuktaguruparaṃ vaidikaguruparaṃ vā | 

yadi ca matsyasūktādivacanānusāreṇa tāntrikaguruparamapyaṅgīkurvate | 

tadapi kulāgamaproktaprerakādiguruparam | 

paśvācārayuktaśiṣyasya karttavyatvenoktaṃ veti kecit | 

na tu bodhakaguruparamiti sudhībhiryeyam | 

viśeṣaṃ śṛṇu vakṣyāmi māhātmyaṃ gurugocaram | 

paśumantrapradāne tu maryyādā daśapauruṣī | 

vīramantrapradāne tu pañcaviṃśatipauruṣī | 

mahāvidyāsu sarvāsu pañcāśatpauruṣī matā | 

brahmayogapradāne tu maryādā śatapauruṣī | 

ayamabhiprāyaḥ | 

pāśvācārādimatapradeyamantro yadi śiṣyeṇa guruto gṛhītastadanantaraṃ śiṣyasantatyā 
gurvantarād yadi mantro gṛhītastathāpi paitṛkagurutyāgajanyaduradṛṣṭaśālinā 
paitṛkanurudaśamādivaṃśaparyyantaṃ svīyadaśamādivaṃśāvadhikena guruvanmaryyādā 
kāryyā | 

anyathā pratyekena pratyekato yadi mantro gṛhyate tadā teṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ gurutayā 
vaṃśamaryyādākalpanamanarthakaṃ syāditi tyaktaguruvaṃśamaryyādākathanam | 

yadvā vakṣyamāṇakāmākhyātantroktavacanena lubdhaduṣṭādigurutyāgasya vaidhatvena 
yadi śiṣyeṇa sa gurustyajyate tathāpi tadvaṃśamaryyādārthametaduktam | 

divyavīragurvorapi doṣaduṣṭatve sthājya(?)tvaṃ vakṣyate | 

<p. 179> evañca gurutyāgādbhavenmṛtyurityāditantrasārakāradhṛta(-)
vacanamaduṣṭādigurutyāge bodhyam || 

śāktānandataraṅgiṇīdhṛtamahiṣamardinītantre || 

mānavasya maheśāni (!) saṅkṣepātvigadāmi te | 

guruḥ paraguruścaiva parāparagurustathā | 

śvaguruḥ parameśāni (!) sākṣādvaśca na saṃśayaḥ | 

tatpitā paramaguruḥ svayaṃ viṣṇuḥ kṣitau sadā | 

tatpitā parāparagururmaheśvarasamaḥ sadā | 

vṛhannīlatantre dvitīyapaṭale tu || 

parāparagurūṇāñca nirṇayaṃ śṛṇu pārvati (!) | 

ādau sarvatra deveśi (!) mantradaḥ paramo guruḥ | 

parāparagurustvaṃ hi parameṣṭhī tvahaṃ guruḥ || 

kulārṇavatantre | 

pāramparyāgamāmnāyaṃ mantrācārādikaṃ priye (!) | 

sarvaṃ gurumukhāllabdhaṃ saphalaṃ syānna cānyathā || 

pāramparyyaśabdavyutpattistu saptadaśollāse || 

pāśacchedakarāddevi (!) rañjanātparatejasaḥ | 

jātibhiśchidyamānācca pāramparyyamitīritam || 

atha strīgurulakṣaṇam || 

rudrajāmale uttarakhaṇḍadvitīyapaṭalapūrvakhaṇḍapañcadaśapaṭalayoḥ || 

sādhvīcaiva sadācārā gurubhaktā jitendriyā | 

sarvamantrārthatattvajñā suśīlā pūjane ratā | 

sarvalakṣaṇasampannā rūṣikā padmalocanā | 

ratnālaṅkārasaṃyuktā svarṇābharaṇabhūṣitā | 

śāntā kulīnā kulajā candrāsyā sarvabuddhigā | 

anantaguṇasampannā rudratvadāyinī priyā | 

gururūpā śaktidātrī śivajñānavirūpiṇī | 

guruyogyā bhavet sā hi vidhavā parivarjitā | 

striyā dīkṣā śubhā proktā mātuścāṣṭaguṇā smṛtā | 

puttriṇī vidhavā grāhyā kevalānandakāriṇī | 

siddhamantraṃ yadi tadā gṛhṇīyādvidhabāmukhe | 

kevalaṃ suphalaṃ tatra māturaṣṭaguṇaṃ smṛtam | 

svayaṃ vā svapnavilaye dadāti yadi manmanum | 

tatrāṣṭaguṇamāpnoti yadi sā putriṇī satī | 

yadi mātā svīyamantraṃ dadāti tanujāya ca | 

tadāṣṭasiddhimāpnoti bhaktimārge na saṃśayaḥ | 

tadeva durlabhaṃ deva (!) yadi mātrā pradīyate | 

<p. 180> ādau bhaktiṃ tato muktiṃ samprāpya kāmarūpadhṛk | 

sahasrakoṭividyārthaṃ jānāti nātra saṃśayaḥ | 

svapne vā yadi vā mātā dadāti ca svamantrakam | 

punardīkṣāṃ so'pi kṛtvā dānavatvamavāpnuyāt | 

yadi bhāsyavaśenaiva jananīcchānuvarttinī | 

tadā siddhimavāpnoti tatra mantraṃ vicārayet | 

svīyamantropadeśena na kuryyādgurucintanam || 

iti strīgurukathanam ||

gurau karttavye varjjanīyamāha rudrajāmalīyottarakhaṇḍīyadvitīya(-)
paṭalapūrvakhaṇḍīyapañcadaśapaṭalayoḥ || 

varjjayecca parānandarahitaṃ rūpavarjjitam | 

ninditaṃ rogiṇaṃ krūraṃ mahāpātakinaṃ gurum | 

aṣṭaprakārakuṣṭhe ca galatkuṣṭhinameva ca | 

śvitriṇaṃ janahiṃsārthaṃ sadārthagrāhiṇaṃ tathā | 

svarṇavikrayiṇaṃ cauraṃ buddhihīnaṃ sukharvaram | 

śyāvadantaṃ kulācārarahitaṃ śāntivarjjitam | 

sakalaṅkaṃ netrarogapīḍitaṃ paradāragam | 

asaṃskārapravaktāraṃ strījitañcādhikāṅkam | 

kapaṭātmānamevañca viśiṣṭaṃ bahujalpakam | 

bahvāśinaṃ hi kṛpaṇaṃ mithyāvādinameva ca | 

aśāntaṃ bhāvahīnañca pañcācāravivarjitam | 

doṣajālaiḥ pūjitāṅgaṃ pūjayenna guruṃ vinā | 

guruṃ vinā na pūjayedityabhidhānāt gurau kṛte yaḥ kuṣṭhādidoṣaduṣṭo bhavati taṃ 
pūjayet | 

gurukaraṇasamaye tu yaḥ kuṣṭhādistaṃ varjayediti vacanasya tātparyyam | 

taccoktaṃ mahāpātakinaṃ guruṃ varjayediti tadapi bhāvini bhūtavadupacāra iti 
karttavyaguruparamanyathā pūrvāparalikhitaikagranthayorvaiṣamyaṃ syāt || 

niruttaratantre dvādaśapaṭale || 

paśuṃ śaṭhañca dhūrttañca cumbakañca viśeṣataḥ | 

dharmārthakāmamokṣārthī guratvena na cārccayet || 

jāmale pūrvakhaṇḍe pañcadaśapaṭale tu || 

gurorvicāraḥ sarvatra tātamātāmahaṃ vinā | 

pramādācca yadā tābhyāṃ dīkṣāvidhimupācaret | 

prāyaścittaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā punardīkṣāṃ samācaret | 

sāvitrī mantrajāpañca lakṣasaṃkhyaṃ jagatpateḥ | 

<p. 181> viṣṇorvā praṇavaṃ lakṣaṃ prāyaścittamiti smṛtam | 

aśaktaścenmahādevi (!) cāyutaṃ prajapenmanum | 

daśasāhasryajapyena sarvakilviṣanāśinī | 

gāyatrī chandasāṃ mātā pāparāśitulānalaḥ | 

kaṭhinañca ripuñcaiva sodaraṃ vairipakṣiṇam | 

mātāmahañca pitaraṃ yatinaṃ vanavāsinam | 

varjjayitvā ca śiṣyendro dīkṣāvidhimudācaret | 

anyathā tadvirodhena kāyanāśo bhaveddhruvam | 

siddhamantrañca gṛhṇīyādduṣkulādapi bhairavi (!) | 

svapne tu niyamo nāsti dīkṣāsu guruśiṣyayoḥ | 

svapnalabdhe striyā datte saṃskāreṇaiva śudhyati || 

rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtamahākapilapañcarātre || 

nātibālo na vṛddhaśca na khañjo na kṛśastathā | 

nādhikāṅgo na hīnāṅgo na khalvāṭo na danturaḥ | 

vīramitrodayadhṛtakalpacintāmaṇau || 

kṣayarogī ca duścarmā kunakhī śyāvadantakaḥ | 

karṇāndhaḥ kusumākṣaśca khalvāṭaḥ khañjarīṭakaḥ || 

karṇāndho vadhiraḥ | 

khañjarīṭaḥ khañjaḥ || 

aṅgahīno'tiriktāṅgaḥ piṅgākṣaḥ pūtināsikaḥ |

vṛddhāṇḍo vāmanaḥ kubjaḥ śvitrī caiva napuṃsakaḥ |

pūtināsiko durgandhināsikaḥ | 

ityādyairdehajairdoṣaiḥ saṃyukto nindito guruḥ | 

saṃskārarahito mūrkho vedaśāstravivarjitaḥ | 

śrautasmārttakriyāśūnyaḥ śuṣkabhāṣaḥ sukutsitaḥ | 

purayājanajīvī ca naro vaidyaśca kāmukaḥ | 

krūro dambhī matsarī ca vyasanī kṛpaṇaḥ khalaḥ | 

kusaṅgī nāstiko bhīto mahāpātakacihnitaḥ | 

devāgniguruvidyādipūjāvidhiparāṅmukhaḥ | 

sandhyātarpaṇapūjādimantrajñānavivarjjitaḥ | 

ālasyopahato bhogī dharmmahīna upaśrutaḥ | 

ityādyairbahubhirdoṣairāgamoktaiśca yatnataḥ | 

varjanīyo guruḥ prājñairdīkṣāsu sthāpanādiṣu || 

matsyasūkte trayodaśapaṭale || 

aputraścāsutapatnīkaḥ śaktihīno'tha vāmanaḥ | 

kubjaḥ kuṣṭhaḥ śyāvadanto vṛṣalīpatireva ca | 

mātāmahasyānujasya sodarasya tathaiva ca | 

pitrormantraṃ na gṛhṇīyāttathā caivāśritasya ca | 

<p. 182> kadācidyadi gṛhṇāti punaḥ saṃskāramarhati || 

tyājyagurusāha kāmākhyāyāṃ tṛtīyapaṭale || 

mānuṣe gurutvasya kalpanāmātramuktvā | 

yathā bhoktre tu bhojyaṃ hi svarṇādipātrake na ca | 

dīyate ca yathā devi (!) tasmai sarvasamarpaṇam | 

yadi nindyañca tatpātraṃ svarṇaṃ vāpi kuleśvari (!) | 

tadā tyajettu tatpātramanyapātreṇa bhojayet | 

ato hi manujaṃ lubdhaṃ duṣṭaṃ śiṣyo hi santyajet | 

sarveṣāṃ bhuvane satyaṃ jñānāya gurureva hi | 

jñānānmokṣamavāpnoti tasmājjñānaṃ parātparam | 

ato yo jñānadānaṃ hi na kṣamettaṃ tyajedgurum | 

annākāṅkṣī nirannaṃ hi yathā santyajati priye (!) | 

jñānaṃ tatra samābhāti sa guruḥ śiva eva hi | 

a jñāninaṃ varjjayitvā śaraṇaṃ jñānināṃ vrajet | 

madhulubdho yathā bhṛṅgaḥ puṣpāt puṣpāntaraṃ vrajet | 

jñānalubdastathā śiṣyo gurorgurvantaraṃ vrajet || 

tathā | 

pūrvoktadoṣayuktaśceddivyo vā vīra eva vā | 

tayorapi na karttavyā śiṣyeṇa gurubhāvanā | 

kintu kāryya hitaiṣitvaṃ gurutākalpanaṃ tyajet || 

kaulānāntu tatraiva || 

mahāvidyāṃ sampradāya vīrācāraṃ dadāti na | 

sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ śiṣyo'pi patito bhavet | 

paśubhāve sthito yo hi kālikātāriṇīmanum | 

dattvācāraṃ vadennaiva narakānna nivarttate | 

tasmāt paśugurustyājyaḥ sādhakaiḥ sarvadā priye (!) | 

yadi daivāt paśordīkṣāṃ labhate śākta eva ca | 

kaulāttu kaulikīṃ kṛtvā tanmanuṃ punarālabhet || 

ityuktavacanādabhiṣekādinā punarguruḥ karaṇīya ityagre'pi vakṣyate || 

gurutā śiṣyatvāpekṣaṇītyato guruprakaraṇānantaraṃ śiṣyaprakaraṇamucyate || 

tatra śiṣyaśabdavyutpattistu kulārṇave pañcamakhaṇḍe saptadaśollāse || 

śarīramatha prāṇāṃśca sadgurubhyo nivedya yaḥ | 

gurubhyaḥ śiṣyato yogaṃ śiṣya ityabhidhīyate || 

atha śiṣyalakṣaṇam | 

rudrajāmale uttarakhaṇḍe dvitīyapaṭale || 

gurulakṣaṇamuktvā | 

śiṣyastu tādṛśo bhūtvā sadguruṃ paryyupāśrayet || 

<p. 183> kulamūlāvatārakalpasūtraṭīkāyām | 

caturbhirādyaiḥ saṃyuktaḥ śraddhāvān susthirāśayaḥ | 

alubdhaḥ sthiragātraśca prekṣākārī jitendriyaḥ | 

āstiko dṛḍhabhaktiśca gurau mantre ca daivate | 

evaṃvidho bhavet śiṣyastvitaro duḥkhakṛdguroḥ || 

caturbhirādyaiḥ saṃyuktaḥ śamādicatuṣkayukta ityarthaḥ || 

śāradātilake dvitīyapaṭale || 

śiṣyaḥ kulīnaḥ śuddhātmā puruṣārthaparāyaṇaḥ | 

adhītavedaḥ kuśalo dūramuktamanobhavaḥ | 

hitaiṣī prāṇināṃ nityamāstikastyaktanāstikaḥ | 

svadharmmanirato bhaktyā pitṛmātṛhitodyataḥ | 

vāṅmanaḥkāyavasubhirguruśuśrūṣaṇe rataḥ | 

etādṛśaguṇopetaḥ śiṣyo bhavati nāparaḥ | 

samācāratantre dvīiyapaṭale || 

śiṣyo'pi svaguṇairyukto gurubhaktirataḥ sadā | 

dharmakāmādisaṃyukto gurumantraparāyaṇaḥ |

satyabuddhirgurormantre devapūjanatatparaḥ | 

gurūpadiṣṭamārge ca satyabuddhirudāradhīḥ | 

evaṃlakṣaṇasaṃyuktaḥ śiṣyaścāpi parīkṣitaḥ || 

matsyasūkte mahātantre trayodaśapaṭale'pi | 

śiṣyaḥ kulīnaḥ śuddhātmā puruṣārthaparāyaṇaḥ | 

adhītavedaḥ kuśalo dūramuktamanobhavaḥ | 

hitaiṣī prāṇināṃ nityamāstikastyaktanāstikaḥ | 

svadharmanirato bhaktyā pitṛmātṛhitodyataḥ | 

vāṅmanaḥkāyavasubhirguruśuśrūṣaṇe rataḥ | 

etādṛśaguṇopetaḥ śiṣyo bhavati śaṅkari (!) || 

iti śiṣyalakṣaṇam ||

rudrajāmalīyagurumānanādiprakāravidhāyakāni | 

guruḥ pitetyādijanmaheturityādi | 

sadguruḥ svāśritamityādivacanānyasmadatyativṛddhaprapitāmaha(-)kṛṣṇānandāgamavāgīśena 
svakṛtatantrasāre likhitānyato naivātra prayojanamiti || 

varjyaśiṣyamāha rudrajāmalapūrvakhaṇḍapañcadaśapaṭalottara(-)khaṇḍadvitīyapaṭalayoḥ || 

kāmukaṃ kuṭilaṃ lokaninditaṃ satyavarjjitam | 

avinītamasamarthaṃ prajāhīnaṃ ripupriyam | 

sadā pāpakriyāyuktaṃ vidyāśūnyaṃ jaḍātmakam | 

<p. 184> kalidoṣasamūhāṅgaṃ vedakriyāvivarjitam | 

āśramācārahīnañcāśuddhāntaḥkaraṇodyatam | 

sadā śraddhāvirahitamadhamaṃ krodhinaṃ bhramam | 

asaccaritraṃ viguṇaṃ paradārāturaṃ tathā | 

sambaddhāṅgaṃ samūhogramabhaktaṃ dvaitacetasam | 

nānānindāvṛtāṅgañca taṃ śiṣyaṃ varjayedguruḥ || 

iti nindyaśiṣyalakṣaṇam || 

jāmale || 

yadi na tyajyate vīro dhanādidānahetunā | 

nārakī śiṣyavat pāpī tadviśiṣṭamavāpnuyāt | 

kṣaṇādasiddhaḥ sa bhavecchiṣyāsāditapātakaiḥ | 

akasmānnarakaṃ prāpya kāryyanāśāya kevalam | 

vicāryya yatnādvidhivacchiṣyasaṃgrahamācaret | 

anyathā śiṣyadoṣeṇa narakastho bhavedguruḥ | 

iti varjyaśiṣyakaraṇanindā || 

śiṣyakarttavyākarttavye ukte rudrajāmale uttarakhaṇḍe prathamapaṭale | 

yathā | 

na laṅghayedgurorājñāmuttaraṃ na vadettathā | 

divā rātrau gurorājñāṃ dāsavat pratipālayet || 

iti gurvājñāpālanavidhiḥ || 

na śṛṇuyādgurorvākyaṃ śṛṇuyādvā parāṅmukhaḥ | 

ahitaṃ vā hitaṃ vāpi rauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet | 

ājñābhaṅgaṃ gurordevi (!) yaḥ karoti sa mūḍhadhīḥ | 

prayāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ śūkaratvamavāpnuyāt || 

tathā ekaviṃśatipaṭale || 

vṛthā dharmaṃ vṛthā caryyaṃ vṛthā dīkṣāṃ vṛthā tapaḥ | 

vṛthā sukṛtimākhyātiṃ gurvājñālaṅghane nṛṇām || 

iti gurvājñālaṅghanadoṣaḥ | 

dvitīyapaṭale | 

ājñābhaṅgaṃ tathā nindāṃ gurorapriyavarṇanam |

gurudrohañca yaḥ kuryāttatsaṃsargaṃ na kārayet | 

kārayet ityatra svārtheṇic na kuryādityarthaḥ | 

kulārṇave pañcamakhaṇḍe ekādaśollāse || 

gurudravyābhilāṣī yo gurustrīgamanotsukaḥ | 

sa tiryyagyonimāpnoti kravyādyairbhakṣyate priye (!) || 

rudrajāmale || 

gurudravyābhilāṣī ca gurustrīgo bhaved yadi | 

pātakañca bhavettasya prāyaścittaṃ na kārayet || 

prāyaścittaṃ na kārayedityanenātyantaniṣiddhamiti dhvanitam || 

yathā | 

kāmato brāhmaṇabadhe niṣkṛtirna vidhīyate | 

iti manuvacanatātparyyamuktam |

<p. 185> bhaviṣyapurāṇe || 

kāmato brāhmaṇabadhe yadetanmanunoditam | 

ekāntato viprabadhavarjjanārthamudīritam || 

jāmale || 

guruṃ duṣkṛtya ripuvannirgatya parivādataḥ | 

araṇye nirjane deśe sa bhavedbrahmarākṣasaḥ || 

iti gurvaniṣṭakaraṇadoṣaḥ | 

gurugītāyām || 

aśaktā hi surāḥ sarve aśaktā munayastathā |
