guruśāpamṛtāḥ kṣīṇāḥ kṣayaṃ yānti na saṃśayaḥ || 

iti guruśāpajanyadoṣaḥ || 

rudrajāmale pūrvakhaṇḍe caturdaśapaṭale || 

gurornindāñca paiśunyaṃ yaḥ śṛṇoti dināntare | 

tasya taddinajāṃ pūjāṃ na tu gṛhṇāti sundarī || 

iti gurunindāśravaṇadoṣaḥ || 

kulārṇave dvādaśollāse || 

yatra śrīgurunindā syānnidhāya śravaṇe svake | 

sadyastasmādviniṣkrameddūraṃ na śravaṇaṃ yathā | 

gurornāma smaret paścāt śravaṇe sāyasikisā | 

iti gurunindāśravaṇapratīkāraḥ || 

jāmale || 

gataśrīśca gatāyuśca gurornindākaro naraḥ | 

kalpakoṭiśataṃ devi (!) narake patati dhruvam || 

tathā | 

evaṃ śrutvā mahādevi (!) gurunindāṃ karoti yaḥ | 

sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ yāvaccandradivākarau || 

iti gurunindākaraṇe doṣaḥ || 

gurutantre || 

gurau manuṣyatābuddhiḥ śiṣyāṇāṃ yadi jāyate | 

na hi tasya bhavet siddhiḥ kalpakoṭiśatairapi || 

iti gurau manuṣyajñānajanyadoṣakathanam || 

kulārṇave || 

ājñābhaṅgo'rthaharaṇaṃ gurorapriyavarṇanam | 

guradrohamidaṃ prāhuryaḥ kuryyāt sa tu pātakī | 

gurukopāttu nāśaḥ syādgurudrohāttu pātakam | 

vimṛtyurgurunindāyāṃ gurvaniṣṭānmahāpadaḥ | 

jīvedagniṃ praviṣṭo vā naraḥ pītaviṣo'pi vā | 

mṛtyuhastagato vāpi nāparādhakaro guroḥ || 

iti gurvaparādhādidoṣaḥ || 

rudrajāmale pūrvakhaṇḍe || 

gurostu santatiṃ tyaktvā na gurvantaramāśrayet | 

gurunindā na karttavyā na śrotavyā kadācana || 

iti gurusantatityāganiṣedhaḥ || 

gurvādimānanaprakāraśca puraścaraṇasollāse daśamapaṭale || 

guruñca bhāvayennityaṃ sadā śivamayaṃ sadā | 

<p. 186> guroḥ sutañca cārvaṅgi (!) gaṇeśasadṛśaṃ sadā | 

guroḥ sruṣā varārohe (!) vāṇī lakṣmīriva priye (!) | 

guroḥ kulaṃ maheśāni (!) bhairavāṇāṃ gaṇaṃ yathā || 

kalpasūtraṭīkādhṛtakulāgame śāktānandataraṅgiṇyāñca || 

gandhairmālyaiśca cārvaṅgi (!) pūjayet sarvadā gurum | 

guroḥ patnīṃ tathā devi (!) pūjayedvidhināmunā || 

gurupatnīpadaṃ guruyoṣidupalakṣaṇam | 

na tu patyurno yajñasambandhe iti pāṇinisūtrānusāreṇa vivāhasaṃskṛtāparam | 

sutrārthastu patiśabdasyānte nakārādeśo bhavati yajñasambandhe gamyamāne | 

yajñaśabdenātra vivāhāṅgayajña ucyate | 

tathātve pañcavidhaguruyoṣitpūjāvidhānaṃ kulārṇavoktamasaṅgataṃ syāttathā ca | 

kulārṇavatantre pañcamakhaṇḍe prathamollāse || 

ūḍhā dhṛtā tathā krītā mūlyena ca samāhṛtā | 

sakṛt kāmagatā cāpi pañcadhā guruyoṣitaḥ | 

alaṅghyāḥ pūjanīyāścāgamyāśca guruyoṣitaḥ || 

vṛhannīlatantre tṛtīyapaṭale || 

gurupatnī ca yuvatī na saṃvāhyeta pādayoḥ | 

punarviṃśativarṣeṇa guṇadoṣau vijānatā | 

ata(-)eva maheśāni (!) na kuryyāt pādasevanam || 

kulāgame || 

guruvadguruputreṣu guruvattatsutādiṣu | 

pūjayet pratyahaṃ bhaktyā amunā vidhinā priye (!) || 

pūjākramastu tatraiva || 

gurorabhāve cārvaṅgi (!) gurupatnīṃ prapūjayet | 

abhāve alābhe ||

tadabhāve ca cārvaṅgi (!) guruputraṃ samarcayet |

tadabhāve varārohe (!) gurukanyāñca pūjayet |

tadabhāve ca cārvaṅgi (!) gurusnuṣāṃ prapūjayet |

eṣāmabhāve cārvaṅgi (!) gurugotraṃ prapūjayet | 

tadabhāve varārohe (!) tathā mātāmahasya ca | 

mātulaṃ mātulānīṃ vā pūjayedvidhināmunā || 

iti gurukulapūjākramakathanam | 

tathā | 

pūjākāle ca cārvaṅgi (!) āgacchecchiṣyamandire | 

gururvā gurupatnī vā putro vāpi samāgataḥ |

jyeṣṭho vāpyarcanāmadhye śiṣyaḥ sarvārcanāṃ tyajet | 

tameva pūjayecchiṣya iti śāstrasya nirṇayaḥ || 

iti pūjākāle gurvādāvāgate sati śiṣyakarttavyam tatraiva || 

<p. 187> śiṣyasya taddinaṃ devi (!) koṭisūryyagrahaiḥ samam | 

candragrahaṇakālaṃ hi taddinaṃ varavarṇini (!) | 

gurordarśanamātreṇa sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate | 

tatkṣaṇāccañcalāpāṅgi (!) dānaṃ kuryyādvicakṣaṇaḥ || 

iti gurudarśanadinapraśaṃsā || 

gurau prītisamāpanne devatā prītimāpnuyāt | 

deve ca prītimāpanne mantrasiddhirbhaveddhruvam || 

iti guruprītiphalam || 

muṇḍamālātantre || 

gurupūjāṃ vinā devi (!) iṣṭapūjāṃ karoti yaḥ | 

mantrasya tasya tejāṃsi harate bhairavaḥ svayam || 

rudrajāmale || 

gurupūjāṃ vinā nātha (!) koṭipuṇyaṃ vṛthāvaham | 

ataḥ sarvajane khyātaṃ guruṇā siddhimāpnuyāt || 

rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtam || 

gurvanuktāḥ kriyāḥ survā niṣphalāḥ syuryato dhruvam | 

japo homārccanavidhiḥ kāryyo dīkṣānvitaistataḥ || 

tathā | 

praṇamya gurupādābjaṃ dhyātvā ca gurupādukām | 

jñātvā paramatattvaṃ vai yo japet kulacaṇḍikām | 

sa kṛtārthaḥ sa dhanyaśca sa kulajñaḥ sa paṇḍitaḥ | 

sa bhāvajño mahādevi (!) jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 

gurugītāyām || 

saṃsārasāgarasamuddharaṇaikamantraṃ brahmādidevamunisevitasiddhimantram | 

dāridryaduḥkhabhayaśokavināśamantraṃ vande mahābhayaharaṃ gurudevamantram || 

gurutantre || 

guroḥ sevā gurordhyānaṃ guroḥ stotraṃ gurorjapaḥ | 

guroḥ pūjā gurostṛptirgurorbhaktirnṛṇāṃ yadi | 

janmabhāgyavaśāddevi (!) yeṣāṃ sañjāyate kvacit | 

teṣāṃ mantro bhavet siddho jīvanmuktāśca te narāḥ | 

gurorgehe sthitaḥ śiṣyo yat puṇyaṃ samupācaret | 

tat puṇyamakṣayaṃ proktaṃ puṇyatīrthe śatādhikam || 

iti gurugṛhaniṣpāditapuṇyakarmaphalam || 

tatraiva || 

guroḥ pādarajo yastu sudhīrmūrddhani dhārayet | 

sa tīrthakoṭijaphalāt phalaṃ daśaguṇaṃ labhet | 

guroḥ pādodakaṃ yastu nityaṃ pibati bhaktitaḥ | 

sārddhatrikoṭitīrthānāṃ phalaṃ sa labhate dhruvam || 

guptasādhanatantre || 

trisandhyaṃ pibate yastu gurupādodakaṃ sudhīḥ | 

<p. 188> tasya nāsti punarjanma saṃsāre mohavartmani | 

brahmā viṣṇuśca rudraśca sarvāśca pitṛdevatāḥ | 

tiṣṭhati śrīguroḥ vādapadmārccitajale śubhe | 

puroḥ pādodakaṃ yastu nityaṃ pibati mānuṣaḥ | 

dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāmadhipo jāyate ca saḥ || 

yoginītantre || 

subhaktyā vigalatpādaprakṣālanajalaṃ yadi | 

pibedamṛtabhāvena yaḥ sa devīpuraṃ vrajet || 

guptasādhanatantre || 

guroḥ pādodakaṃ yastu śirasā dhārayennaraḥ | 

sa sarvatīrthajaṃ puṇyaṃ labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 

gurutantre || 

trisandhyaṃ pūjayed yastu gandhapuṣpairjagadgurum | 

tasya kiṃ mantrapūjādividhānairnyāsajāpakaiḥ |

gurorannaṃ mahādevi (!) yastu bhakṣaṇamācaret | 

koṭijanmārjitaṃ pāpaṃ tatkṣaṇāttasya naśyati | 

na snānaṃ pādaśaucañca na caivācamanañcaret | 

prāptimātreṇa bhoktavyaṃ naiva sthānaṃ vicārayet | 

na brāhmaṇyaṃ na kaulīnyaṃ na jātīnāṃ vicāraṇam | 

na jñātisutabandhūnāṃ sabhyānāṃ tatra cintanam | 

ekasthānasthitaṃ hyannaṃ tattīrthamaṇḍalīkṛtam | 

bhoktavyaṃ mokṣadaṃ devi (!) śūdraspṛṣṭaṃ na cintayet | 

gurorannaṃ sudhābuddhyā yastvadyānmatimānnaraḥ | 

śivo'pi tasya deveśi (!) tuṣṭo bhavati nānyathā | 

gurorucchiṣṭakaṃ devi (!) bhaktimuktipradaṃ bhavet | 

avaśyam vaiṣṇavaiḥ śiṣyerbhoktavyaṃ taddine dine || 

yoginyāṃ prathamapaṭale || 

gurūcchiṣṭañca deveśi (!) tatsutocchiṣṭameva ca | 

bhojanīyaṃ na sandeho vikāraścedadhogatiḥ | 

tavocchiṣṭaṃ mahādevi (!) brahmādīnāṃ sudurlabham | 

gurūcchiṣṭaṃ tathā proktaṃ mahāpūtaṃ parātparam | 

nanvetāvatā gurupatnyucchiṣṭaṃ na bhakṣaṇīyamiti cenna | 

gurupatnī maheśāni (!) gurureva na saṃśaya ityetadvacanena tasyā gurutvena 
gurūcchiṣṭabhojanavidhinā taducchiṣṭabhojanavidheḥ siddhatvāt | 

guruṇā gurupatnyaḥ vā guruputtreṇa vā priye (!) | 

sṛṣṭānnaṃ muṣṭimātraṃ vā yo'śnīyāharṣaviṃśatim | 

cirajīvī jarārogavimukto'sau śivo bhavet || 

<p. 189> gurutantre || 

gurorucchiṣṭakairyastu sarvāṅgamanulepayet | 

vajro'pi tasya gātrāṇi na bhinatti kadācana | 

gurorucchiṣṭamannaṃ yo bhakṣayedbhaktiṃbhāvataḥ | 

annapūrṇā jagaddhātrī tasyeśvaryaṃ prayacchati | 

iti gurūcchiṣṭabhojanaphalaṃ tatraiva || 

kadācit parameśāni (!) yadi tatra bhramo bhavet | 

na tasya niṣkṛtirdṛṣṭā kalpakoṭiśatairapi | 

na bhakṣayed yastu mohādgurorucchiṣṭakaṃ priye (!) | 

tasya kruddhā bhavestvaṃ hi vipattiśca pade pade || 

iti gurūcchiṣṭābhakṣaṇajanyadoṣakathanam || 

tatraiva || 

pādukāṃ vasanaṃ chatraṃ śayanaṃ bhūṣaṇādikam | 

dṛṣṭvā gurornamaskuryyādātmabhogaṃ na kārayet || 

kulārṇave pañcamakhaṇḍe dvādaśollāse || 

pādukāmāsanaṃ chatraṃ vāhanaṃ vastracāmare | 

dṛṣṭvā  gurornamaskuryāt nātmabhogāya kalpayet |

pādāprakṣālanaṃ snānamabhyaṅgaṃ dantadhāvanam | 

mūtraniṣṭhīvanaṃ kṣauraṃ śayanaṃ strīniṣevaṇam | 

vīrāsanañca durvākyamāsanaṃ hāsyarodanam | 

keśamocanamuṣṇīṣaṃ kañcukaṃ lagnatāṃ tathā | 

pādaprasāraṇaṃ vādaṃ kalahaṃ dūṣaṇaṃ priye (!) | 

aṅgabhaṅgāṅgavādyādikarāsphālavidhūnanam | 

dyūtakukkuṭamallādiyuddhamityādi cāmbike (!) | 

gurusiddhamahāsidhapīṭhakṣetrāśrayeṣu ca | 

nācaredācarenmohāt devatāśāpamāpnuyāt | 

upacāraṃ vinā viṣṭhed gurvagre necchayā viśet || 

iti gurusamīpe mahāpīṭhādau pādaprakṣālanādiniṣedhaḥ || 

yoginītantre pūrvakhaṇḍe prathamapaṭale || 

surāṃ yadyapyasaṃskārāṃ gurorājñāvaśāt pibet | 

prāyaścittaṃ na tatrāsti vede'pi sthitameva hi | 

api tantraviruddhaṃ vā guruṇā kathyate yadi | 

tatsammataṃ bhavedvedairmahārudracaco yathā | 

iti gurvājñayā niṣiddhācaraṇe doṣābhāvaḥ | 

nirgataṃ yad gurorvaktrāt sarvaṃ śāstraṃ taducyate | 

gurukāryye svayaṃ śakto nāparaṃ prerayet priye (!) | 

rudrajāmale | 

bandhubhṛtyapurairbhṛtyaiḥ sahito'pyatibhaktimān | 

gacchan tiṣṭhan svapan jāgrat japan juhvat prapūjayet | 

<p. 190> gurvājñāmeva kurvīta tadgatenāntarātmanā | 

abhimāno na karttavyo jātividyādhanādabhiḥ | 

sarvadā sevayennityaṃ śiṣyaḥ śrīgurusannidhau || 

iti gurvagre mānaniṣedhaḥ || 

tatraiva || 

sadā ca pādukāmantraṃ jihvāgre yasya varttate | 

anāyāsenaṃ dharmārthaṃkāmamokṣaṃ labhennaraḥ || 

śrīguroścaraṇāmbhojaṃ dhyāyed yaśca sadaivatam | 

bhuktaye muktaye vīro nānyo bhaktastato'dhikaḥ | 

vidyāṅgamāsanaṃ mantra mudrāṃ tantrādikaṃ prabho (!) | 

sarvaṃ gurumukhāllabdhaṃ saphalaṃ nānyathā bhavet || 

vākyabhedenānvayaḥ || 

kambale kamale vāpi prāsāde saṃsthite tathā | 

dīrghakāṣṭhe tathā pṛṣṭhe gurorekāsanaṃ tyajet | 

śrīguroḥ pādukāmantraṃ mūlamantraṃ svapādukām || 

śiṣyāya naiva deveśa (!) pravadedyasya kasyacit | 

yadyadātmahitaṃ vastu taddravyaṃ naiva vañcayet | 

gurorlabdhaikavarṇo yastasya tuṣyāmi suvrata (!) | 

bhaktyā vittānusāreṇa gurumuddiśya yat kṛtam | 

tayoreva phalaṃ deva (!) tulyāmāḍhyadaridrayoḥ | 

svalpe mahati vā puṇyamiti tṛtīyacaraṇe kulārṇave pañcamakhaṇḍe dvādasollāse 
pāṭhaḥ || 

sarvasvamapi yo dadyād gurubhaktivivarjitaḥ | 

narakaṃ tadavāpnoti bhaktireva hi kāraṇam | 

kulārṇave tu |

śiṣyo na phalamāpnoti iti tṛtīyacaraṇe pāṭhaḥ |

gurubhaktyā ca śakratvaṃ madbhaktyā śūkaro bhavet || 

guruṃ vinā mayyeva yā bhaktistayeti tātparyyam | 

garubhaktiparaṃ nāsti sarvaśāstreṣu tattvataḥ || 

iti gurubhaktiphalam || 

dhigdhanaṃ dhigbalaṃ teṣāṃ dhikkulaṃ dhigviceṣṭitam | 

teṣāṃ notpadyate bhaktirgurudeve maheśvari (!) | 

iti bhaktyanutpattau doṣaḥ | 

jāmale || 

gurustrīputtrabandhūnāṃ doṣaṃ naiva prakāśayet | 

bhakṣayenna ca taddravyaṃ dattaṃ naiva parityajet | 

gurugītāyām || 

guruṇā darśite mārge manaḥśuddhiñca kārayet | 

guruṇā sadasadvāpi yaduktaṃ tanna laṅghayet | 

guroragre na vaktavyamasatyañca kadācana || 

picchilātantre ||

<p. 191> paitraṃ gurukulaṃ yastu tyajedvai dharmamohitaḥ | 

sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ yāvaccandrārkatārakam || 

kulārṇave pañcamakhaṇḍe ekādaśollāse || 

brahmādistambaparyantaṃ yasya yā gurusantatiḥ || 

tasya me sarvaśiṣyasya kaulaḥ pūjyo mahītale | 

iti niścitabuddhiryaḥ sa bhavedāvayoḥ priyaḥ || 

śrīguruṃ kulaśāstrāṇi pūjāsthānāni yāni ca | 

bhaktyā śrīpūrvakaṃ devi (!) praṇamya ca prakīrttayet | 

guruṃ nāmnā na bhāṣeta japakālādṛte priye (!) | 

śrīnāthadevasvāmīti vivāde sādhane vadet | 

atha guruśuśrūṣā | 

tathā dvādaśollāse || 

ātmārthamānyasadbhāvaiḥ śuśrūṣā syāccaturvidhāḥ | 

śuśrūṣayā dhiyā devi (!) śiṣyaḥ santoṣayedgurum | 

pade pade'śvamedhasya phalaṃ prāpnotyasaṃśayam | 

kevalaṃ guruśuśrūṣā tvatkṛpākāriṇī priye (!) | 

madbhaktisahitā cet sā sarvakāmaphalapradā | 

kṣīyante sarvapāpāni varddhante puṇyarāśayaḥ | 

sidhyanti sarvakāryyāṇi guruśuśrūṣayā priye | 

yadātmahitaṃ vastu tattadvittamavañcayan | 

gurudevārcako yastu tasya puṇyaṃ na gaṇyate | 

gurumitrasuhṛddāsīdāsādyānnāvamānayet | 

na nindedasya samayān vedaśāstrāgamādikān | 

tathā | 

vivikṣurdeśikāvāsaṃ sāntacitto'tibhaktimān | 

vāhanaṃ pādukāṃ chatraṃ cāmaraṃ vyajanādikam | 

tāmbūlaṃ veśamutsṛjya praviśecca śanaiḥ śaniḥ || 

deśikavyutpattistu saptadaśollāse || 

devatārūpadhāritvācchiṣyānugrahakāraṇāt | 

karuṇāmayamūttitvāddeśikaḥ kathyate priye (!) || 

deśikāvāsaṃ gurvāvāsamityarthaḥ | 

tathā | 

riktahastaśca nopeyādrājānaṃ daivataṃ gurum | 

phalapuṣpāmbarādīni yathāśaktyā samarpayet ||

[iti śrīprāṇatoṣiṇyāṃ dvitīye dharmakāṇḍe guruśiṣyaprakaraṇarūpaśākhādvayakathanaṃ 
nāma dvitīyaḥ paricchedaḥ] |



darapatijayacaryyāsvarodayadhṛtabrahmajāmale || 

aśvinyādi likheccakraṃ sarpākāraṃ trināḍikam | 

tatra vedhavaśājjñeyaṃ vivāhādiśubhāśubham | 

trināḍīvedhanakṣatramaśvinyārdrā yugottarā | 

hastendramūlavāruṇyaḥ pūrvābhādrapadāstathā | 

yāmyaḥ saumyo gururyoniścitrāmitrajalāhvayam | 

dhaniṣṭhā cottarābhādramadhyanāḍī vyasthitā | 

kṛttikā rohiṇī sarpo maghā svātīviśākhake | 

uttarā śravaṇā pauṣyakroḍanāḍī vyavasthitā | 

aśvinyādināḍīvedharkṣe ṣaṣṭhaṃ dvitīyakaṃ kramāt | 

yāmyādituryyaṃ turyyañca kṛttikādau dviṣaṭkakam | 

evaṃ nirīkṣayedvedhaṃ kanyāmantre gurau sure | 

puṇyastrīsvāmimitreṣu deśe grāme pure gṛhe | 

ekanāḍīsthadhiṣṇyāṇi yadi syurvarakanyayoḥ | 

tadā vedhaṃ vijānīyād gurvādiṣu tathaiva ca | 

prakaṭaṃ yasya janmarkṣaṃ tasya janmarkṣato vyadhaḥ | 

praśastaṃ janmabhaṃ yasya tasya nāmarkṣato vadet | 

dvayorjanmabhayorvedhe na karttavyaṃ kadācana | 

ekanāḍīsthitā cet syādbharttṛnāśāya cāṅganā | 

ekanāḍīsthitā yatra gururmantraśca devatā | 

tatra dveṣaṃ rujaṃ mṛtyuṃ krameṇa phalamādiśet | 

prabhuḥ puṇyāṅganā mitraṃ deśo grāmaḥ puraṃ gṛham | 

ekanāḍīgatā tyājyā bhavyā vedhavivarjjitā || 

ityādivacanād guruśiṣyamantrāṇāṃ nāḍīnakṣatravicāro'vaśyakaraṇīya iti ||

dīkṣāyāṃ kartavyāyāṃ cakrādivicārasyāvaśyakatvam | 

tattu asmadgoṣṭhīgariṣṭhakṣṇānandāgamavāgīśena svakṛtatantrasāre sarvaṃ likhitamato 
nātra prayojanam || 

yattu nātra siddhādyapekṣāstītyādivacanamātrapradarśibhiridaṃ praśaṃsāparaṃ sarvatra 
vicārasyāvaśyakatvamiti tantrasārakārasya svakapolakalpitamityupahasitam | 

taiḥ rudrajāmalottarakhṇḍīyacaturthapaṭalādyuktaṃ na dṛṣṭaṃ na śrutañca tadyathā | 

kālītārādimantrasya siddhādīnnaiva śodhayet | 

tathāpi śodhayenmantraṃ praśaṃsāparameva tat | 

vicārābhāvapratipādakavacanamātraṃ yatyādiparaṃ na gṛhasthaparamityagre vakṣyate || 

<p. 193> cakravicārapratiprasavastvannadākalpe dvitīyapaṭale ukto yathā || 

haracakrādicakranarakamuktvā || 

navacakraviśuddhātmā mantro'bhīṣṭāya kalpyate | 

upāsitā yadi bhavet paitṛkī puruṣāḥkramāt | 

tadā tadgrahaṇe dhīro vicāraṃ naiva kārayet | 

tattu mantrañca vanitāśrayedbhāgyavaśādyadi | 

tadā bhartturvicāreṇa vicāro'syā na tu svataḥ | 

kulacakre samutpanne vīrān māheśvarīmukhāt | 

kṛpayā dīyamānasya vicāro nāsti bhairava (!) | 

svāntaḥkaraṇavṛttairvā yatra śraddhā gariyasī | 

saivopāsyā prayatnena vicārastatra niṣphalaḥ || 

dīkṣākarmmaṇi maṇḍapakaraṇasyāvaśyambhāvena bhūmiparīkṣāṃ vinā 
maṇḍapādikaraṇaniṣedhadarśanena cādau bhūmiparīkṣocyate || 

tattu rāghavaghaṭṭadhṛtamahākapilapañcarātre || 

tatra bhūmīṃ parīkṣeta vāstutattvaviśāradaḥ | 

sphuṭitā ca saśalyā ca valmīkārohiṇī tathā | 

dūrataḥ parivarjyā bhūḥ kartturāyurdhanāpahā || 

sphuṭitā cet cakāreṇoṣareti na labhyate | 

sphuṭitā maraṇaṃ kuryyādūṣarā dhananāśinī | 

saśalyā kleśadā nityaṃ viṣamā śatruto bhayam || 

iti varjanīyabhūmikathanam || 

tatraiva || 

īśakoṇaplavāsā ca karttuḥ śrīdā muniścitam | 

pūrvaplavā vṛddhikarī dhanadā tūttaraplavā |

vidveṣaṃ maraṇaṃ vyādhiṃ kuryādagniplavā mahī |

dharmarājaplavā bhūmirnityaṃ mṛtyubhayapradā | 

gṛhakṣayakarī sā ca bhūmiryā nai(-)ṛtaplavā | 

dhanahānikarī pṛthvī kīrttitā varuṇaplavā | 

vātaplavā tathā bhūmirnityamudvegakāriṇī | 

iti dikplavabhūmiśubhāśubhe || 

tatraiva | 

śvetā tu brāhmaṇī bhūmiraktā vai kṣatriyā smṛtā | 

vaiśyā pītā tu vijñeyā śūdrā kṛṣṇā prakīrttitā | 

brāhmaṇī ghṛtagandhā syāt kṣatriyā raktagandhikā | 

kṣīragandhā bhavedvaiśyā śūdrā viḍgandhinī kṣitiḥ | 

madhurā brāhmaṇī bhūmiḥ kaṣāyā kṣatriyā smṛtā | 

vaiśyā tiktā ca vijñeyā śūdrā syāt kaṭukā mahī | 

<p. 194> brāhmaṇī bhūḥ kuśopetā kṣatriyā syācchavākulā | 

kuśakāśākulā vaiśyā śūdrā sarvatṛṇākulā || 

iti brāhmaṇyādi bhūmijñānakaraṇakathanam tatraiva | 

sitā pītā tathā raktā kṛṣṇavarṇasamanvitā | 

sthirodakā dṛḍhā snigdhā bhūmiḥ sarvasukhāvahā | 

śītasparśoṣṇakāle ca vahnisparśā himāgame | 

varṣāsu cobhayasparśā sā śubhā parikīrttitā | 

taddhṛtahayaśīrṣaṣañcarātre'pi || 

surabhīṇāṃ ratiryatra savatsānāṃ vṛṣaiḥ saha | 

sundarīṇāṃ ratiryatra puruṣaiḥ saha sattamaḥ (!) | 

kāśmīracandanāmodā karpūrāgurugandhinī | 

kamalotpalagandhā ca jāticampakagandhinī | 

pāṭalā mallikāgandhā nāgakeśaragandhinī | 

dadhikṣīrājyagandhāḍhyā maṃdirāsavagandhinī | 

sugandhivrīhigandhāḍhyā śubhagandhayutā ca sā | 

sarveṣāmeva varṇānāṃ bhūmiḥ sādhāraṇī matā | 

iti śubhabhūmikathanam | 

madagrajarāmalocanavidyābhūṣaṇakṛtavāstuyāgapradīpikā(-)dhṛtahayaśīrṣapañcarātre || 

jñātvā bhūmīṃ parīkṣeta pūrvodakplavanāṃ śubhām | 

asaṅkaṭāṃ tathācchannāṃ kṛtaistoyapariplutām | 

sampūryya mārge khāte tu tathādhikamṛdaṃ śubhām | 

kusumaprakarastadvadyasyāṃ na glānimṛcchati | 

na nirvāti tathā dīpaḥ śīghraṃ puṣpaṃ na śīryate | 

śvetāruṇā pītakṛṣṇā viprādīnāṃ viśiṣyate || 

praśasyate ityarthaḥ || 

tyājyā bhūmiḥ kṣāragandhā pūtigandhā ca yā bhavet | 

madhurā ca kaṣāyā ca amlā ca kaṭukā ca yā | 

kuśaiḥ śaraistathā kāśairdūrvābhiryā ca sambhṛtā | 

sambhṛtā atyantasaṅkīrṇā || 

tathādhikamṛdamityādyuktavivaraṇamuktaṃ prayogasāre || 

vitastimātravistāraṃ nirmāya vivaraṃ bhuvi | 

nikṣipettāṃ mṛdaṃ tasmin tāsu śiṣṭāsu śobhanam | 

samāsu madhyamaṃ vidyānnūnāsvadhamamucyate | 

parīkṣyaivaṃ prayatnena tyaktvā bhūmīṃ kanīyasamīm | 

aṅgāratuṣakeśāsthihīnaṃ katvātha bhūtalam | 

aṅgāretyādiniṣiddhadravyamātropalakṣaṇam | 

hīnaṃ kṛtvetyanena śalyoddhārakramaḥ sūcitaḥ | 

<p. 195> akaraṇe doṣo'pi | 

ataeva kuṇḍakaraṇe duṣṭā || 

khanyamāne yadā kuṇḍe pāśo na prāpyate bhuvim | 

tadāpamṛtyave cāsthikeśāṅgārairdhanakṣayaḥ | 

bhasmanāgnibhayaṃ proktaṃ tuṣaiḥ proktā daridratā | 

iti duṣṭaphalamupadarśitavān || 

śubhaphalamapi sa eva | 

gośṛṅgaṃ pītamaṇḍūkaḥ śaṅkhaḥ śūktiśca kacchapaḥ | 

śambūkaśca praśastāḥ syurye cānye ratnajātayaḥ ||

atha śalyoddhāradhanoddhārau || 

svarodayadhṛtabrahmajāmale || 

ahicakraṃ pravakṣyāmi yathā sarvajñabhāṣitam | 

dravyaṃ śalyaṃ tathā śūnyaṃ yena jānanti sādhakāḥ | 

nidhirnirvarttanaikasthaḥ sambhrānto yatra bhūtale | 

tata cakramidaṃ sthāpyaṃ sthānadvāramupasthitam || 

ūrddhvarekhāṣṭakaṃ lekhyaṃ tiryyak pañca tathaiva ca | 

ahicakraṃ bhavetyevamaṣṭāviṃśatikoṣṭhakam | 

tatra pauṣṇāśviyāmyarkṣaṃ kṛttikāmaghabhāgyabham | 

uttarāphālgunī lekhyaṃ pūrvapaṃktyā bhasaptakam || 

ahibradhnājapā dakṣaṃ patabhaṃ brahmasarpabham | 

puṣyaṃ hastaṃ samālekhyaṃ dvitīyāṃ paṃktimāsthitam | 

abhijidviṣṇuvasvṛkṣaṃ  saumyaṃ raudrapunarvasū | 

citrabhañca tṛtīyāyāṃ paṃktau dhiṣṇyasya saptakam | 

viśvarkṣaṃ toyabhaṃ mūlaṃ jyeṣṭhā maitraṃ viśākhabham | 

svātīṃ paṃktyāṃ caturthyāntu kṛtvā cakraṃ vilokayet | 

evaṃ sañjāyate cakraṃ saṃsthāne pannagākṛti | 

dvāradeśe maghāyāmyau dvārasthā kṛttikā matā | 

aśvīśapūrvāṣāḍhāditrikapañcatuṣṭayam | 

pauṣṇaprauṣṭhapadā cendorbhāni śeṣāṇi bhāsvataḥ | 

1 | 2 | 3 | 6 | 7 | 8 | 9 | 10 | 20 | 21 | 22 | 27 | 25 |

iti candrasya | 

4 | 5 | 11 | 12 | 13 | 14 | 15 | 16 | 17 | 18 | 19 | 23 | 24 | 26 | 

iti sūryyasya | 

udayādigatā nāḍyo bhaghnāḥ ṣaṣṭyāptaśeṣake | 

dinendubhuktayukto'sau bhavettatkālacandramāḥ | 

candravat sādhayet sūryyamṛkṣasthaṃ ceṣṭakālikam |

paścādvilokayettau ca svasvarkṣe vānyataḥ sthitau | 

candra(-)ṛkṣe dāyarkendū tadāste niyataṃ nidhiḥ | 

<p. 196> bhānu(-)ṛkṣasthite tau tu tadā śalyaṃ vinirdiśet | 

svasvabhe dvitayaṃ jñeyaṃ nāsti kiñcidviparyyaye | 

sthitaṃ na labhyate dravyaṃ candre krūragrahānvite | 

puṣṭe candre bhavetpuṣṭaḥ kṣīṇacandre'lpako nidhiḥ | 

grahadṛṣṭivaśāt so'pi vijñeyo navadhā budhaiḥ | 

hema tāmrañca tārāraṃ ratnakāṃsyāyasaṃ trapu | 

nāgaṃ candre vijānīyād bhāskarādigrahekṣite | 

miśre miśraṃ bhaved dravyaṃ śūnyaṃ duṣṭivivarjite | 

sarvagrahekṣite candre vijñeyo'sau mahānidhiḥ | 

hema tārañca tāmrāraṃ pāṣāṇaṃ mṛṇmayāyasam | 

sūryyādigrahage candre dravyabhāṇḍaṃ prajāyate | 

śubhakṣetragate candre dravyalābho na saṃśayaḥ | 

pāpakṣetre na lābhaḥ syājjñātavyaṃ jyotiṣāṃ budhaiḥ | 

bhuktarāśyaṃśamānena bhūmānaṃ kārmikaiḥ karaiḥ | 

nīce vighnaṃ pare nīce jalastho'sau bhavennidhiḥ | 

uccasthe ūrddhvagaṃ dravyaṃ navāṃśakakrameṇa ca | 

paramocce pare tuṅge bhittisthamṛkṣasaṃkrame | 

candrāṃśabhuktamānena dravyasaṃkhyā vidhīyate | 

tasmāccejjāyate vṛddhiḥ ṣaḍvargeṣu balakramāt | 

adhiṣṭhitaṃ bhaveddravyaṃ yatra candro grahānvitaḥ | 

tadadhiṣṭhāyako jñeyo bhāskarādigrahaiḥ kramāt | 

grahā hyupagrahāścaiva kṣetrapālaśca mātṛkā | 

dīpeśo bhīṣaṇo rudro yakṣo nāgaḥ kramānmataḥ | 

grahahomaśca karttavyo mukhe nārāyaṇe baliḥ | 

kṣetrapāle surā māṃsaṃ mātṛkāyāṃ mahābaliḥ | 

dīpe pradīpajā pūjā bhīṣaṇe bhīṣaṇārccanam | 

rudre rudrajapo japyo yakṣe yakṣādiśāntikam | 

nāge nāgagaṇāḥ pūjyā gaṇanāthena saṃyutāḥ | 

lakṣmīṃ dharāditattvāni sarvakāryyeṣu pūjayet | 

evaṃ kṛte vidhānena duḥsādhyaḥ sādhyate nidhiḥ | 

nidhiprāptyā naro loke pūjyate sarvadā budhaiḥ || 

asyārthaḥ || 

sarvajñabhāṣitaṃ śrīsadāśivabhāṣitam | 

ahicakram ahaṃ pravakṣyāmi prakarṣeṇa kathayiṣyāmītyanvayaḥ | 

yena cakreṇa dravyaṃ śalyaṃ tadubhayaṃ śūnyaṃ dravyaśalyahīnaṃ sthānaṃ paṇḍitā 
jānanti bhaviṣyatsāmīpye varttamānanirdeśāt jñāsyantīti tātparyyam || 

<p. 197> nidhiriti nivarttanaikastho dīrghaprahastataḥ dviśatahastamāne bhūtale 
daśādhikadviśatahastaparimite vā yatra sambhrānto bhramaviṣayo nidhistatra idaṃ cakraṃ 
sthāpyam | 

nivarttanapramāṇantu siddhāntaśiromaṇau līlāvatyabhidhe pāṭīgaṇite tathā karaṇād 
daśakena viṃśaḥ nivarttanaṃ viṃśativaṃśasargākhyaḥ kṣetraṃ caturbhiśca 
bhujairnibaddhamiti | 

svarodayaṭīkākārastu || 

saptahastena hastena triṃśaddaṇḍo nivarttanamityāha | 

tadubhayamataṃ prāmāṇyam | 

asya dīrghataḥ prahastataśca ekādaśavārṣikanavāṅgulaiścaturdaśahastaiśca 
parimitadvayamabadhāryyam | 

svarodayaṭīkākṛnmate dīrghaprahastādikametadanusāreṇa paṇḍitairvivicya karttavyam | 

cakraṃ kīdṛśaṃ sthānadvāramukhasthitaṃ kṣetrādau dvārājñāne evaṃ prajāyate | 

cakre prastāraṃ pannagākṛtirityupasaṃhāravacanārthato bhādrāditriṣu māseṣu 
pūrvādicaturdikṣu śiraḥ kṛtvā nāgaḥ śete tena tattanmāsi praśne sati tasyāṃ tasyāṃ 
diśi ahibalacakramukaṃ kṛttikānakṣatravyavasthitaṃ kṛtvā vyavaharaṇīyamityupadeśaḥ | 

ūrddhveti || 

ūrddhvarekhāṣṭakaṃ ūrddhā ca krameṇa rekhāṣṭakaṃ lekhyam | 

tathaiva pañcarekhāstiryyakkrameṇa likhet | 

evamaṣṭāviṃśatikoṣṭham | 

tatra koṣṭheṣu pauṣṇo revatī aśvinī yāmyarkṣaṃ bharaṇī kṛttikā maghā bhāgyabhaṃ 
pūrvaphalgunī uttaraphalgunī ceti nakṣatrasaptakaṃ prathamapaṃktyāṃ lekhanīyam | 

ahibradhneti || 

ahibradhnaḥ uttarabhādrapadaḥ ajapādaḥ pūrvabhādrapadaḥ śatabhiṣā brāhmaṃ rohiṇī 
sarpabhamaśleṣā puṣyā hastāśceti nakṣatrasaptakaṃ dvitīyapaṃktimāsthitaṃ lekhyam | 

vidhiriti | 

vidhiḥ abhijit viṣṇuḥ śravaṇā saumyaṃ mṛgaśirā raudra ārdrā punarvasu citrā ceti 
nakṣatrasaptakaṃ tṛtīyapaṃktau | 

viśvakṣamuttarāṣāḍhā mūlaṃ jyeṣṭhā anurādhā viśākhā svātī ceti 
nakṣa(?)trasaptakaṃ caturthapaṃktau | 

evaṃ kṛtvā arthādālokya cakraṃ vilokayediti | 

<p. 198> evamanena prakāreṇa kṛttikānikṣatrānukramasthityā pannagākṛtiḥ sarpākṛtiḥ | 

dvāraśākheti maghābharaṇyau | 

dvāraśākhe dvārobhayapārśvasthite | 

kṛttikādvārasthanāgamukham | 

aśvīśeti | 

aśvinī īśa ārdrā pūrvāṣāḍhā etāni ādibhūtāni kṛtvānukrameṇa 
tripañcacatuṣṭayaṃ nakṣatraṃ yathā aśvinī bharaṇī kṛttikā iti trīṇi | 

ārdrā punarvasu puṣyā aśleṣā maghā ceti pañca | 

pūrvāṣāḍhottarāṣāḍhā abhijit śravaṇeti catvāri nakṣatrāṇi | 

aśvinī bharaṇī kṛttikārdrā punarvasu puṣyā aśleṣā maghā pūrvāṣāḍhā 
uttarāṣāḍhā abhijit śravaṇā revatī pūrvabhādrapadā etāni nakṣatrāṇi | 

indroścandrasya | 

śeṣāṇi bhāni rohiṇī mṛgaśirāḥ pūrvaphalgunyuttaraphalgunī hastā citrā svātī 
viśākhā anurādhā jyeṣṭhā mūlā dhaniṣṭhā śatabhiṣā uttarabhādrapadasaṃjñakāni 
| 

bhāsvataḥ sūryyasya jñātavyāni || 

udayādīti | 

udayo nakṣatrapravṛttisamayastadādiḥ | 

iṣṭasamayena ca yāvanto daṇḍāścandreṇa bhuktāstadavadhigatā nāḍyo bhaghnāḥ 
saptaviṃśatyā pūritāḥ ṣaṣṭyā hṛtāḥ kāryyāḥ | 

gṛhītabhāgenāptaśeṣake āpte labdhe śeṣe cāsau tatkālacandramā bhavati | 

sa kimbhūtaḥ dinendubhuktayuktaḥ | 

dine dine indunā candreṇa bhuktena nakṣatreṇa yuktaḥ | 

saptaviṃśatyadhike saptaviṃśatimapāsya adhikamaśvinīto bhuktanakṣatrasaṃkhyā bhavati | 

evaṃ suryyo'pi svabhuktāśvinyādinakṣatrayogena tātkāliko bhavati | 

evameva svarodayaṭīkāyāṃ narahariṇā vyākhyātam | 

sūryyabhujyamānanakṣatrajñānantu mānasapañjikāgaṇanavamaślokenoktam | 

saṅkṣepataḥ pravakṣyāmi tatkālenduṃ parisphuṭam | 

yena vijñānamātreṇa budhyate ca śubhāśubham | 

ahorātrasya madhye tau viśrāntau dhiṣṇyamaṇḍale | 

sa caiva praśnakāle ca yātrākāle viśeṣataḥ | 

daṇḍeśca pañcabhīrāśireva syurdvādaśa kramāt | 

lagnavadbhuktabhāgasya tyāgāccheṣaḥ kramotkramaḥ | 

<p. 199> pravāsaḥ susthatā mṛtyurhāsyo jayo jvaro ratiḥ | 

śūnyatā hrāsitā krīḍā suptirbhuktiśca meṣataḥ | 

etāśca dvādaśāvasthāḥ śaśāṅkasya dine dine | 

śubhāśubheṣu kāryyeṣu phalaṃ nāmānurūpataḥ || 

nakṣatramekaṃ pratipañcadaṇḍakrameṇa dvādaśacandrāstātkālikā bhavanti cenna | 

yasya nakṣatrasya udayādigatā nāḍī grāhyā tasyādau pañca daṇḍān vyāpya tasya 
nakṣatrasya candro bhavati | 

tatparapañcadaṇḍān vyāpya tatparacandramā bhavati | 

evaṃ krameṇa dvādaśa candramaso bhavanti | 

kasyacinmate sūryyodayāvadhiṣaṣṭidaṇḍātmakakāle dvādaśa candramaso bhavanti | 

tanmate sūryyodayāvadhipañcadaṇḍe yannakṣatraṃ tasya candramā bhavati |

tatparapañcapañcadaṇḍe krameṇa candramā divārātrau dvādaśacandrāstātkālikā 
bhavanti | 

yātrāyāmeveti | 

udayādigatā ityasyārthaḥ | 

ṣaṣṭyāptaśca śeṣaḥ ṣaṣṭyāptaśeṣakaṃ tasmin ṣaṣṭyāptaśeṣake | 

etena ṣaṣṭyā daṇḍaṣaṣṭyā hṛtaśeṣake ceti yāvat | 

tasmin ṣaṣṭyā hṛtalabdhāṅko dinendubhuktayuktastatkālacandramā bhavati | 

tatṣaṣṭyā hṛtabhāgaśeṣāṅke udayādigatanāḍīyuktena yaḥ saṃkhyāṅko bhavati sa 
tasya candramaso bhogyadaṇḍo bhavatīti bhāvaḥ | 

śeṣaka iti svārthe kaḥ | 

atra yadi śeṣo na labdhastadā udayādigatā nāḍīmātrasaṃkhyāṅkastasya bhogyadaṇḍa 
iti bhāvaḥ | 

etena khaṇḍanakṣatrasyaivaṃ bhogavaśāttātkālikacandrasya sthānaniścayo boddhavyaḥ | 

ṣaṣṭyāptaśeṣakairiti pāṭhe tu ṣaṣṭyāptena ṣaṣṭyā hṛtalabdhāṅkena 
dinendubhuktayuktaścet tatkālacandramā bhavati | 

śeṣakaiḥ ṣaṣṭyā hṛtabhāgaśeṣāṅkairudayādigatānāḍīsaṃkhyāṅkayuktena yaḥ 
saṃkhyāṅkaḥ sa tasya candramaso bhogyadaṇḍo bhavati || 

candravaditi || 

ṛjñasthaṃ nakṣatrasthaṃ ceṣṭanāḍīkaṃ praśnasamaya iṣṭadaṇḍasthaṃ kṛtvā 
ekadvitridinakrameṇa pratyekaṃ dinaṃ daṇḍānāṃ sārddhacatuṣkaṃ saṅkalayya grāhyam 
| 

evaṃ krameṇa nakṣatrasya praśnakāle sūryyeṇa yāvanto daṇḍā bhuktāstāneva 
saptaviṃśatisaṃkhyayā saṅguṇya ṣaṣṭyā haraṇāllabdhaṃ śeṣañca bhavati labdhañca 
sūryyabhuktanakṣatrasaṃkhyayā saṃyojya saptaviṃśatyadhike sati 
saptaviṃśatimapāsyāvaśiṣṭaṃ bhuktanakṣatraṃ tatsaṃkhyā aśvinīto bhavati | 

<p. 200> ṣaṣṭiharaṇaśeṣaṃ bhujyamānanakṣatram | 

tatra koṣṭhe tannakṣatrasthatātkālikaḥ sūryo vedhanīyaḥ paścāt 
svanakṣatracandranakṣatrayorvyavasthitaścintanīyaḥ | 

nakṣatre sūryyabhuktaṣaṣṭidaṇḍapūrṇatā tu catvāriṃśaddaṇḍanyūnacaturdaśadinena 
sakalanakṣatrabhogaḥ pratyahaṃ saptadaśapalādhikāścatvāro daṇḍā bhavanti 
nyūnabhogyena kiñcit pale hrasati | 

tena nakṣatrabhogapravṛttisamayādārabhya praśnasamayaparyyantaṃ yāvanto daṇḍāsteṣu 
pratiṣaṣṭi catvāro daṇḍāḥ saptatriṃśatpalāni ṣaṣṭimānena tadanumānena nyūnatā 
sūkṣmakālasthasūryyamaṇḍalādigrahanakṣatratātkālikīkaraṇe paitāmahaślokau likhyete || 

ṛkṣapraveśā diṣṭāntanāḍyaḥ ṣaṣṭyā bhatāḍitāḥ |

ṛkṣapradeśādātyāgaghaṭikābhirvibhājitāḥ | 

labdhaṃ daṇḍādi yatra tu tadbhagnaṃ ṣaṣṭibhirhṛtam aśvyādisūryabhuktena 
yuktastātkāliko raviḥ | 

paścāttātkālikīkaraṇānantaraṃ sūryyācandramasau svasvarkṣavyavasthitau svanakṣatre 
vāparanakṣatre sthitau vilokayet | 

candra iti candra(-)ṛkṣe aśvīśetyādikathitanakṣatre yadi arkendū tātkālikau stastadā 
nidhirniyataṃ niścitamasti cedbhānunakṣatre sūryyanakṣatre tau sūryyācandramasau sthitau 
tadā śalyaṃ bhavati | 

svasvabha iti svasvabhe candre ravau ca svasvanakṣatre sthitau dvitīyaṃ nidhiḥ | 

śalyañca candrasthāne nidhiṃ sūryyasthāne śalyaṃ vadet || 

viparyyaye candranakṣatre sūryye sūryyanakṣatre candre sthite sati na kiñcit nidhiḥ 
śalyañca nāstītyarthaḥ | 

krūrānvite tātkālikacandre sthite dravyaṃ na labhyate pāriśeṣyāt śubhagrahayoge 
labhyate iti || 

puṣṭa iti || 

candrapuṣṭe śuklapakṣaikādaśīparyyantaṃ puṣṭastatra puṣṭo'tiśayito nidhirbhavet | 

<p. 201> kṣīṇacandre tu nidhiralpaka iti | 

kṣīṇenduśca kṛṣṇaikādaśītaḥ śuklapañcamīṃ yāvaditi || 

ubhayapakṣaṣaṣṭhīdaśamīparyyantaṃ na tu madhyabalatvam | 

tena madhyatvaṃ dravyasya grāhyam | 

evaṃ vyavasthitau kiṃ dravyamityākāṅkṣāyāmāha || 

hemeti || 

sūryyādinā vīkṣite tātkālike candre hemādidravyaṃ vijānīyādityarthaḥ | 

tatra sūryyavīkṣite hema suvarṇaṃ sthūlacandrekṣite tāraṃ rajataṃ kujekṣite tāmraṃ 
budhekṣite āraṃ pittalaṃ jīvekṣite ratnaṃ śukrekṣite kāṃsyaṃ śanaiścarekṣite āyasaṃ 
lauhavikāraḥ rāhuvīkṣite vaṅga iti ketuvīkṣite nāgaṃ sīmakamiti prasiddhaṃ dravyam || 

miśreti || 

miśrairnānāgrahairdṛṣṭe miśraṃ nānāprakāraṃ vadet | 

ye ye grahāḥ paśyanti tasya tasya dravyāṇi bhavantīti tātparyyam | 

dṛṣṭivarjite candre śūnyaṃ na kimapītyarthaḥ | 

sarvagrahekṣite candre asau mahānidhirvijñeya iti | 

asau nidhiḥ grahāṇāṃ sūryyādigrahāṇāṃ dṛṣṭivaśānnavadhā navaprakāro 
budhairjñātavya iti || 

dravyādhāramāha hemeti || 

candre sūryyādirāśisthe dravyabhāṇḍamādhāro bhājanaṃ prajāyate ityarthaḥ | 

yathā sūryyagṛhe siṃhe hema | 

candragṛhe karkaṭe rajatam | 

kujagṛhe meṣe vṛścike vā tāmram | 

budhagṛhe kānyāyāṃ mithune vā āram | 

gurugṛhe dhanuṣi mīne vā pāṣāṇaḥ | 

śukragṛhe vṛṣe tulāyāṃ vā mṛṇmayam | 

śanigṛhe makare kumbhe vā āyasaṃ lauham | 

evaṃ rāhugṛhaṃ kanyā tatra budhadṛṣṭivihīne vaṅgam | 

ketugṛhe mīne jīvānalokite sīsakamityapi jñeyam || 

śubhakṣetra iti || 

śubhāścandrabudhaguruśukrāsteṣāṃ kṣetre rāśau candre sati dravyasya lābho na 
saṃśaya eva | 

evaṃ pāpakṣetre pāpagraharāśige sati tadā na lābhaḥ | 

iti svaravidbhirjñātavyamityarthaḥ || 

bhukteti | 

bhuktarāśyaṃśamānena tātkālikacandreṇa yāvanto rāśyaṃśā bhuktāstanmānena 
tatsaṃkhyayā kārmikairbaddhamuṣṭibhiḥ karairhastairbhūmānaṃ dhṛtadravyagatabhūmānaṃ 
bhuktāṃśakasaṃkhyakahastapramāṇaṃ karttavyaṃ khāṃte dravyamastīti vaktavyam | 

<p. 202> nīce nīcarāśivṛścikasthe candre tadeva mānaṃ dvighnaṃ dviguṇitaṃ pareṃ nīce 
vṛścikatṛtīyāṃśasthe ca candre asau nidhirjalastho vaktavyaḥ || 

tāvat khātantu yāvajjalasthaṃ dravyaṃ labhyate || 

uccasthe iti | 

vṛṣarāśidvitīyāṃśasthe candre navāṃśakakrameṇa yāvanto navāṃśā 
bhuktāstāvantaḥ pūrvavadūrddhvagaṃ paramocce vṛṣatṛtīyāṃśasthe candre 
atyuccapradeśe dravyamastīti | 

ṛkṣasaṃkrame tātkālikanakṣatraśeṣapalagate bhittisthaṃ bhavati || 

candrāṃśeti || 

candrāṃśabhuktakālena tātkālikacandreṇa rāśeryāvanto'ṃśā bhuktāstanmānena 
dravyasaṃkhyā vidhīyate | 

pratyekaṃ bhuktāṃśe daśasaṃkhyā ca ṣaḍvargeṣu gṛhe 
horādrekkāṇanavāṃśadvādaśāṃśatriṃśāṃśeṣu balakramāddaśaguṇe vṛddhiḥ yadi 
svakīyaṣaḍ vargabhāge tiṣṭhati tadā aṃśāduditadravyaṃ daśaguṇaṃ syāt | 

adhiṣṭhitamiti varṇapraśne candro grahānvito bhāti tatra dravyadhiṣṭhitaṃ 
bhāskarādigrahaiḥ pratyekaṃ sahite kramāttadadhiṣṭhāyako dravyādhiṣṭhito jñeyaḥ | 

yathā candre sūryyānvite grahasthūlacandrasahite upagrahaḥ kujasahite kṣetrapālaḥ 
budhasahite mātṛkā gurusahite dīpeśaḥ śukrasahite bhīṣaṇaḥ śanisahite rudraḥ 
rāhusahite yakṣaḥ  ketusahite nāga iti kramāt || 

graha iti | 

grahe adhiṣṭhite hemagrahasyaiva mukhe mukhagrahaḥ nārāyaṇo nārāyaṇasambandhī 
baliḥ pūjopahārādiriti || 

kṣetrapāle adhiṣṭhite tasmai surā madyaṃ māṃsañca deyamiti | 

mātṛkāyāmadhiṣṭhitāyāṃ mahābaliḥ | 

mānuṣabalistadabhāve chāgādayo'pi aviruddhā balitvenopakalpyāḥ | 

dīpeśe adhiṣṭhite dīpajā dīpapradhānā pūjā | 

bhīṣaṇe bhīṣaṇārccanaṃ bhayajanakārccanam | 

lohitapuṣpādikṛte rudre'dhiṣṭhite rudrajāpo rudramantrasya japaḥ jāpyo rudrādhyāyaḥ | 

yakṣe'dhiṣṭhite yakṣādiśākaṃ yakṣādipūjanaṃ | 

nāge'dhiṣṭhite nāgagaṇāḥ pūjyāḥ | 

<p. 203> gaṇanāthena gaṇeśena saṃyutāḥ pratiśāntike gaṇeśapūjā | 

lakṣmīdharādibhūtyādipañcaviṃśatisaṃkhyakāni sarvakāryyeṣu pūjayet || 

evamiti | 

vimāne yathāvidhi krameṇa evaṃ kṛte pūjāhomādau duḥsādhyo nidhiḥ sādhyate | 

etannirūpakasya dṛṣṭaprayojanamāha evaṃ prakāreṇa naro manuṣyo nidhiprāptyā 
vandanīyaḥ namaskaraṇīyaḥ stutyo vā bhavatyatra na saṃśayaḥ iti ||
	
athavā mahākapilapañcarātroktakrameṇa śalyoddhāraḥ karttavyaḥ | 

sa kramo yathā | 

prāsādārambhakāle ca gṛhādau ca viśeṣataḥ | 

śalyoddhārastu karttavyo yadīcchecchubhamātmanaḥ | 

prāsādārambhakāle ca yadaṅgaṃ spṛśate pumān | 

vāstudehe dṛḍhaṃ tatra śalyaṃ dadyādvicakṣaṇaḥ | 

kaṇḍūyati śiraḥ puṃsi śiraḥśalyaṃ samuddharet | 

śalyaṃ tatrāsti vijñeyaṃ khanyamāne karatraye | 

agnidāhaśca rogaśca dhanahāniśca jāyate | 

yatnenotpāṭayecchalyaṃ yadīcchecchubhamātmanaḥ | 

bāhau kaṇḍūyamāne tu nirdiśellauhaśṛṅkhalam | 

hastadvayena mānaṃ tallakṣaṇaṃ kathitaṃ tava | 

svāmino maraṇaṃ vidyādvideśagamanaṃ tathā | 

yatnenotpāṭayecchalyaṃ yadīcchecchubhamātmanaḥ | 

ūrau kaṇḍūyamāne tu kāṃsyaśalyaṃ vinirdiśet | 

hastaikena ca santiṣṭhellakṣaṇaṃ kathitaṃ tava | 

asatī ca bhavedbhāryyā yaśohāniḥ prajāyate | 

yatnenotpāṭayecchalyaṃ yadīcchecchubhamātmanaḥ | 

haste kaṇḍūyamāne kaṅkālañca vinirdiśet | 

trihastena tu santiṣṭhet khanyamāne na cānyathā | 

agnidāhaśca rogaśca pīḍā ca maraṇaṃ bhavet | 

yatnenotpāṭayecchalyaṃ yadīcchecchubhamātmanaḥ | 

pṛṣṭhe kaṇḍūyamāne tu bāhuśalyaṃ vinirdiśet | 

hastenaikena santiṣṭhennātra kāryyā vicāraṇā | 

svāmināśo bhavettatra bhāryyā vā jāyate'satī | 

pade kaṇḍūyamāne tu hastaśalyaṃ vinirdiśet | 

sārddhahastena santiṣṭhellakṣaṇaṃ gaditaṃ tava | 

aṅganāśo rājadaṇḍaḥ śasyahāniśca jāyate | 

yatnenotpāṭayecchalya yadīcchet siddhimātmanaḥ | 

<p. 204> kukṣau kaṇḍūyamāne tu pāṣāṇaṃ tatra nirdiśet | 

hastadvitayamānena lakṣaṇaṃ gaditaṃ tava | 

bhujaṅgadaṃśastatra syāt tasmācchalyaṃ samuddharet | 

jānau kaṇḍūyamāne tu bhasma tatra vinirdiśet | 

hasta dvayena santiṣṭhellakṣaṇaṃ gaditaṃ tava | 

agnidāho manastāpaṃ kleśaduḥkhabhayāni ca | 

karotyevaṃvidhaṃ karmma tasmāttaṃ vai samuddharet || 

jyotiṣe || 

suniścitāṃ mandirabhūmimādau nikhāya toyāvadhiyatnatastām | 

kuryyādviśalyāmathavā na mānaṃ syādvāthavā praśnavaśādvidhikṣaḥ | 

dūrvāprabālākṣatapuṣpapāṇiḥ śuciḥ śuciṃ daivavidaṃ sametya | 

pṛcchedvinīto madhurasvareṇa śalyasya tattvaṃ bhavane tadīśaḥ | 

tatpraśnasyādimo varṇaḥ sandhāryyo yatnato'tha vā | 

kramāt pu.pāparādeva phalānāṃ brāhmaṇāditaḥ | 

kramādityanena brāhmaṇaḥ puṣpanāma vadet kṣatriyo nadīnāma vaiśyo devanāma 
śūdraḥ phalanāma ityāyātam | 

praṇavo dharaṇau dhāriṇī karau na tadanantaram | 

bhūtyai namaśca ityeṣa mantro vahnipriyāntakaḥ | 

vahnipriyā svāhā | 

mantreṇānena kaṭhinīmabhimantrya vibhājayet | 

navadhā sadanakṣetraṃ tayā paścādvilekhayet | 

vakacata(-)ehāḥ śapayā nava cet praśnākṣarāṇi jāyante prāgādikoṣṭhanavake varṇāste 
śalyamākhyānti | 

praśne vakāraḥ purato narāsthi bravīti śalyaṃ marakapradāyi || 1 || 

kṣauṇīśadaṇḍoragahetumṛtyupradaṃ kakāraḥ kharaśalyamagnau || 2 || 

yāmyāṃ cakāraḥ plavagāsthiveśma prabhorvināśāvahamāha śalyam || 3 || 

rakṣodiśi śvāsthigṛhasthitānāṃ mahadbhayaṃ vakti suniścitaṃ taḥ || 4 || 

eḥ pāśidiśyasthiśiśorbravīti mṛtyupravāsāhavamāha śalyam || 5 || 

ho vāyukoṇe nararūpamāha dāridryamiṣṭakṣayakṛdvidhatte || 6 || 

dhanapadiśi śakāraḥ prāha viprāsthivittakṣayakṛt || 7 || 

atha pakāro vakti ṛkṣāsthiśambhau || 8 || 

tadiha kulavināśaṃ godhanānāñca hāniṃ vitarati gṛhanāthasyāpi guptasya devaiḥ | 

<p. 205> yo madhyabhāge bhasitaṃ kapālaṃ kālāyasañcāha kulakṣayāya || 9 || 

yatnādapāsyānyadhunā pramāṇaṃ sarvatra tathyaṃ kathayāmi śalye | 

indrarakṣojaleśāne śalyaṃ sārddhakare kaṭau | 

vahnyantakakuvereṣu puruṣe madhyavātayoḥ || 

devīpurāṇe || 

puruṣādhaḥsthitaṃ śalyaṃ na gṛhe doṣadaṃ bhavet | 

prasāde doṣadaṃ śalyaṃ bhaved yāvajjalāntikam || 

iti śalyoddhāraḥ ||

pūrvādijñānaniścayameva maṇḍapādi kuryyādanyathā doṣa ukto 
rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtavacanena yathā | 

yadi kuryyād yathā dṛṣṭaṃ vipanno nirayaṃ vrajet | 

bhānorgatyā diśo jñātvā kuryyāt karmāṇi deśikaḥ | 

vāstuvaiṣamyato yatra samyaṅ na jñāyate kila | 

tatra śaṅkuṃ pratiṣṭhāpya jānīyāt śuddhadiksthitim || 

śaṅkupramāṇantu jyotistattve | 

arkāṅgulātha sūcyagrā kāṣṭhī dvyaṅgulamūlikā | 

śaṅkusaṃjñā bhavettasyāstacchāyāṃ parikalpayet || 

rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtam || 

tadgatyā dikparijñānaṃ śṛṇu vakṣye yathāvidhi | 

susame bhūtale kṛtvā vṛttaṃ bhramaṇarūpataḥ | 

tanmadhyabindau śaṅkuntu sthāpayed dvādaśāṅgulam | 

agracchāyānvayavaśādvṛtte | 

pūrvāparāhvayoḥ kṛtvā cihne tamabhitastathā | 

susamānaparibhrāntyā kāryyaṃ vṛttadvayaṃ punaḥ | 

tayoḥ saṃśleṣasañjātamadhyadakṣottare sthite | 

sandhidvaye tu prāk pratyak sūtraṃ madhye ca vinyaset | 

sūtradakṣottaraṃ teṣāmagraiḥ prāgādi kalpayet || 

kriyāsāre'pi || 

kṛtvā bhūmiṃ samāṃ tatra vṛttaṃ hastamitaṃ samam | 

dvādaśāṅgulamānoccaṃ śaṅkuṃ khādiranirmitam | 

alābhe yajñakāṣṭhasya tatra saṃsthāpayet sudhīḥ | 

tacchāyā saṃśpṛśedyatra tanmadhye madhyamaṃ smṛtam | 

tiryak prasārayet sūtraṃ madhye yāmyottare smṛte | 

koṇāḥ syuranye catvāraścatuḥsūtraprasāraṇāt | 

evamāśāparijñānaṃ samākhyātaṃ sadā sphuṭam | 

<p. 206> jñātvaivaṃ maṇḍapādīni kuryyāt samyag vicārataḥ || 

mahākapilapañcarātre tu viśeṣaḥ | 

viṣuve'nugate sūryye śaṅkumānaṃ samācaret | 

khādiraṃ vinyasecchaṅkuṃ dvādaśāṅgulavistṛtam | 

iti pūrvādidigjñānanirṇayaḥ || 

śaṅkunikhananaprakāro'pi tatraiva | 

niścalīkṛtya hantavyaṃ gṛhītvā lauhamudgaram | 

aṣṭadhā ca samāhanyāt praśastaṃ kramato laghu | 

hanyamāne yadā śaṅkau hastāt patati mudgaraḥ | 

tadā tāḍayituḥ śoko jāyate dustaro mahān || 

iti śaṅkunikhananaprakāraḥ || 

śaṅkusūtrakaraṇamapi tatraiva || 

mauñjakauśeyakārpāsaṃ prāṇibālajameva vā | 

caturyavaparīṇāhaṃ sūtraṃ śaṅkau tu veṣṭayet | 

veṣṭyamānaṃ yadā sūtraṃ śaṅkuṃ muñcati lakṣaṇam | 

puttrasya maraṇaṃ vidyāt chinne vaiśyavināśanam || 

tatrāpi nārasiṃhena homenāśubhanāśanamiti || 

tathā || 

śaṅkuṃ sāraṃ bhūmeḥ proktaṃ tasyāgraṃ citravṛttakam | 

tasya vṛttādinādau tu sthāpayet samabhūtale | 

śaṅkudviguṇamānena tanmadhye varttulaṃ likhet | 

pūrvāparāhṇayośchāyā yadā tanmaṇḍalāntagā | 

tadbindudvayagaṃ sūtraṃ pūrvaparā digiṣyate | 

bindudvayāntarabhrāntaśaraphadvayapucchagam | 

dakṣiṇottaragaṃ sūtramevaṃ sūtradvayaṃ nyaset | 

tadagrāṇyaparāntāni sūtrāṇi ca vinikṣipet || 

sūtrāṇi sthāpayet prājñaḥ prāguttaramukhāni ceti || 

hayaśīṛṣapañcarātre'pi || 

bhūmiṃ toyasamāṃ kṛtvā darpaṇodarasannibhām | 

dvādaśāṅgulamānena tatra vṛttañca bhrāmayet | 

madhye tu niścalaṃ śaṅku sthāpya chāyāṃ nirīkṣayet | 

vṛttarekhā yā vāhyasthā śaṅkucchāyā prakalpitā | 

praveśanirgame tasyāṃ śaṅkucchāyāṃ nirūpayet | 

śaṅkucchāyāgracihnābhyāṃ prākpratīcyau prasādhayet | 

rātrau tu prācīsādhanaṃ yathā || 

kṛttikā śravaṇā puṣyā citrāsvātyoryadantaram | 

etat prācyā diśo rūpaṃ yugamātrodite pura iti || 

trikāṇḍamaṇḍale'pi || 

śravaṇasyodaye prācī kṛttikāyāstathodaye | 

<p. 207> citrāsvātyantare candrasūryyayoḥ | 

iti śaṅkuprakaraṇam ||

atha maṇḍalaprakaraṇam | 

viśvasāre dvitīyapaṭale || 

dīkṣārthaṃ maṇḍapaṃ kūryāt sundaraṃ sumanoharam | 

nānāvarṇaiścitravarṇaiścitritaṃ sumanoharam | 

bhittistambhādisarveṣu svarṇaratnavibhūṣitam | 

nānāvarṇaiścitravarṇaiścitritaṃ maṇimaṇḍapam | 

vitānapatraśālapyairalaṅkuryyādvidhānataḥ | 

patākābhirdhvajaṃ kuryyāccaturdikṣu manoharam | 

aṣṭau kumbhānaṣṭadikṣu maṇḍapaṃ racayet sudhīḥ || 

atretikarttavyatā uktā śāradātilake dvitīyapaṭale || 

puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā tu maṇḍapam racayecchubham || 

puṇyāhamiti yajamānasya | 

puṇyāhaṃ bhavanto'dhibruvantu evaṃ svasti bhavanta iti ṛddhiṃ bhavanta iti tribhiḥ 
puṇyāhavācanam || 

taduktaṃ rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtabaudhāyanena || 

puṇyāhaṃ svasti ṛddhimityoṅkārapūrvaṃ tristrirekaikāmāśiṣaṃ vācayitveti | 

āśiṣaṃ vācayitveti nirdeśāt sutarāṃ yajamānanāmollekha āyāti | 

tena nāmollekhamakṛtvā kevalaṃ puṇyāhamityādivacanamanubhavaviruddhamiti | 

śāradāyām || 

pañcabhiḥ saptabhirhastairnavabhirvā mitāntaram | 

ṣoḍaśastambhasaṃyuktaṃ catvārasteṣu madhyagāḥ | 

aṣṭahastasamucchrāyāḥ saṃsthāpya dvādaśābhitaḥ | 

pañcahastapramāṇāste niñchidrā ṛjavaḥ śubhāḥ || 

madhyastambhasyāṣṭahastaparimāṇamitareṣāṃ dvādaśānāṃ pañcahastaparimāṇamiti || 

tatpañcamāṃśaṃ nikhanenmedinyāṃ mantravittamaḥ || 

nārikeladalairvaṃśaiśchādayettatsamantataḥ || 

vaṃśairvaṃśadalairubhayatra bahuvacananirdeśenetyādibahuvacanāntā gaṇasya saṃsūcakā 
bhavantīti nyāyācchādanopayuktamanyadapi labhyate | 

dvāreṣu toraṇāni syuḥ kramāt kṣīrimahīruhām | 

stambhocchrāyaḥ smṛtasteṣāṃ saptahastaiḥ pṛthak pṛthak | 

daśāṅgulapramāṇena tatparīṇāha īritaḥ | 

tiryyak phalakamānaṃ syāt stambhānāmūrddhamānataḥ | 

<p. 208> mūlāni kalpayenmadhye toraṇe hastamānataḥ | 

dikṣu dhvajāni badhnīyāt lokapālasamaprabhān | 

vitānadarbhamānādyairalaṅkurvīta maṇḍapam || 

kṣīrimahīruhāmiti aśvatthoḍumbaraplakṣavaṭānām || 

taduktaṃ rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtena || 

nyagrodhatoraṇaṃ pūrve yāmye tūḍumbaraṃ matam | 

paścime'śvatthasambhūtamuttare plakṣatoraṇam || 

pūrve vā plakṣasambhūtaṃ nyagrodhañcottare matamiti || 

toraṇakaraṇakāraṇantu mahākapilapañcarātre | 

devāstoraṇarūpeṇa saṃhitā yajñamaṇḍape |

vighnavidhvaṃsanārthañca rakṣārthaṃ tvadhvarasya ca | 

adhvarasya yajñasya | 

iti maṇḍapādikaraṇam ||

athāṅkurārṇapaṇam | 

prapañcasāre pañcamapaṭale || 

saptāhato vā navamāhato vā prāgeva dīkṣādivaśādyathāvat | 

sapālikāpañcamukhīśarāvacatuṣṭaye vījanivāpayuktam | 

anyasmin bhavane ca bhūtalatale śuddhe sthale maṇḍalaṃ kuryyāt prāgvaruṇāyataṃ 
padacatuṣkoṇe tu bhānūdayam | 

pītāraktasitāsitaṃ pratipadaṃ vahnyādisaptāntikam yāmyodīcyasamāyataṃ 
praṇigadantyanye'pi tanmantriṇaḥ | 

vaiṣṇavyastvatha pālikā api caturviṃśāṅgulivyāpakā vairiñcyo ghaṭikāstu 
pañcavadanādyaṣṭāṅgulocchrāyakāḥ | 

śaivāḥ syurdviṣaḍaṅgulā api śarāvāstrā jalakṣālitāḥ sūtraiśca prakalayya paṃktiṣu 
ca tāḥ proktakramādvinyaset || 

pṛthagapi śālītaṇḍulapūrṇāsu sadarbhabaddhakūrccāsu | 

mṛdbālukākarīṣaiḥ krameṇa pūrṇāni pātrāṇi || 

śālīkaṅkuśyāmākatilasarṣapamudgamāṣaniṣpāvāḥ | 

khalvāḍhakīsametā vījāni viduḥ prarohayogyāni || 

prakṣālya tāni nivapedabhimantrya mūlavījena sādhakavarastvapi pātrakeṣu | 

viprāśiṣā ca vidhivat pratipādyamānaśaṅkhādimukhyatamapañcakanisvanaiśca || 

hāridrādbhiḥ samyagabhyukṣya vastrairācchādyādbhiḥ sicyatāṃ pañcaghoṣaiḥ | 

sāyaṃ prātaḥ śarvarīṣu pradadyāduktairdravyaistadbaliṃ sādhakendraḥ || 

bhūtapitṛyakṣanāgabrahmaśivā devatāśca viṣṇvantāḥ | 

<p. 209> tābhyaḥ krameṇa rātriṣu sapta surān vasu vā tadbalirdeyaḥ || 

lājatailaraktarajanīdadhiśaktvannāni bhūtakrūrākhyam | 

paitryaṃ tilataṇḍulakaṃ sānnapūpaṃ karambhalājakaṃ yākṣam || 

nārikelodakaśaktupiṣṭaṃ nāgaṃ padmākṣatañca vairiñcyam | 

annāpūpaṃ śaivaṃ dugdhānnañca vaiṣṇaveyam || 

yadi navarātraṃ krameṇa baliruktaṃ | 

tārādikairnamo'ntaiḥ svaiḥ svairapi nāmabhirbalimantraḥ | 

pātrāṇi trividhānyapi paritaḥ punaraṣṭadikṣu balikḷptiḥ | 

vījāropaṇakarmma prathitamidaṃ sārvakāmikaṃ bhavati | 

sārvakāmikamityupādānādvakṣyamāṇadīkṣāyāmityādi(-)vacanāccāsyāvaśyakatvam | 

eteṣāmarthastu viśvasāraśāradāvakṣyamāṇapramāṇaiḥ spaṣṭībhaviṣyatyato'tra 
vyākhyā na kṛtā | 

viśvasāratantre tṛtīyapaṭale ca | 

jātāṅkurāḥ prakurvīta caturdikṣu ca pālikāḥ | 

pālikāḥ pañca mukhyaśca śarāvāśca tataḥ kramāt | 

ṣoḍaśadvādaśāṣṭābhiraṅgulībhiḥ kramādimāḥ | 

sugandhitoyapūrṇena jātāṅkuramudāhṛtam | 

sudhāvījena vījāni nikṣipettatra sarvataḥ | 

ācāryyo vidhinā kuryyāt sarvaśāstrārthapāragaḥ | 

śyāmākamāṣakaṅgvaśca vrīhayaḥ sarvataḥ smṛtāḥ | 

dīkṣāyāḥ saptame pūrvaṃ divase saṃyataḥ sadā | 

saptarātreṣu kurvīta pūjanaṃ sarvadevatāḥ | 

nānopahārabalibhiḥ pūjayet parameśvari (!) | 

praṇavādyā namo'ntāśca pūjayet sarvataḥ sudhīḥ | 

bhūtāni pitaro yakṣā nāgā brahmā śivo hariḥ | 

krameṇa pūjayet sarvān saptarātriṣu deśikaḥ | 

saptānāmapi rātrīṇāṃ devatāḥ sarvasammatāḥ | 

upahāraiḥ pūjayettān tāni vakṣyāmi pārvati (!) | 

lājaiśca pūjayedbhūtān gandhapuṣpādicandanaiḥ || 

bhūtādhipaṃ bhūtagaṇaṃ bhūteśañca krameṇa tān | 

tailaharidrādadhiśaktubhiḥ pūjopahārakaiḥ | 

taṇḍulān tilasammiśrān sānnapūpaiḥ prapūjayet | 

pitṝṇāṃ pūjayennityaṃ kathitaṃ padmayoninā | 

karambhalājau yakṣebhyo nārikelodakānvitam | 

śaktuṃ piṣṭañca nāgebhyaḥ pūjayedvidhināmunā | 

<p. 210> paṅkajātapataṇḍuladūrvāśālibhiruttamā | 

pūjayet kathitā tevi (!) brahmaṇaḥ sarvasammatā | 

sāpūpamannaṃ sarvāya sarvatantrasya sammatam | 

guḍodakaṃ guḍānnañca nānārasasamanvitam | 

dadhi dugdhaṃ pāyasānnaṃ dadyāt priyatamaṃ mahat | 

viṣṇave ca nivedyātha sarvasiddhiṃ labhennaraḥ || 

atra balidravyādhikyadarśanāt prāguktaprapañcasāroditabalidravyamupalakṣaṇamātraṃ 
śaktāśaktaparaṃ vā | 

śāradātilake tṛtīyapaṭale'pi || 

prāgeva dīkṣādivaśāt saptabhirvidhivaddinaiḥ | 

sarvamaṅgalasampattyai vidadhyādaṅkurārpaṇam | 

maṇḍalasyottare bhāge śālāṃ pūrvāparāyatām | 

gūḍhāṃ kuryyāttatastasyāṃ maṇḍalaṃ racayet sudhīḥ | 

pañcahastapramāṇāni pañcasūtrāṇi pātayet | 

pūrvāparāyatānyeṣāmambharuṃ dvādaśāṅgulam | 

dakṣiṇottarasūtrāṇi tadvadekādaśārpayet | 

padāni tatra jāyante catvāriṃśat pramārjjayet | 

paṃktyā vīthīścatasro'ntaścatuṣkobhayapārśvayoḥ | 

vīthyau dve ca catuṣkoṣṭhamekamatrāvaśiṣyate | 

padāni rañjayettāni śvetapītāruṇāsitaiḥ | 

rajobhiḥ śyāmalenātha vīthīrāpūrayet sudhīḥ | 

pātrāṇi trividhānyāhuraṅkurārpaṇakarmmaṇi | 

pālikāḥ pañcamukhyaśca śarāvāśca tataḥ kramāt | 

proktāḥ syuḥ sarvatantreṣu haribrahmaśivātmakāḥ | 

eṣāmutsedhato'nveyaiḥ ṣoḍaśadvādaśāṣṭabhiḥ | 

aṅgulaiḥ kramaśastāni śubhānyāveṣṭya tantunā | 

prakṣālya deśikasteṣu pādeṣvāhitaśāliṣu | 

sagandhadarbhakūrcceṣu paścimādi niveśayet | 

karīṣabālukāmṛdbhistāni pātrāṇi pūrayet | 

sudhāvījena vījāni dugdhaiḥ prakṣālya tattvavit | 

mūlamantratrijaptāni pañcaghoṣapuraḥsaram | 

āśīrvāgbhirdvijātīnāṃ maṅgalācārapūrvakam | 

nirvapetteṣu pātreṣu deśiko yatamānasaḥ | 

śāliśyāmāḍhakīmudgatilaniṣpātrasarṣapāḥ | 

kulatthakaṅgumāṣāśca vījānyaṅkurakarmmaṇi | 

haridrādbhiḥ samabhyuśya varunnairācchādya deśikaḥ | 

baliṃ vividhapātrāṇāṃ dikṣu pūrvāditaḥ kṣipet | 

<p. 211> praṇavādyairnamo'ntaiśca rātrau rātrīśanāmabhiḥ | 

bhūtāni pitaro yakṣā nāgā brahmā śivo hariḥ | 

saptānāmapi rātrīṇāṃ devatāḥ samudīritāḥ | 

bhūtebhyaḥ syurlājatilaharidrādadhiśaktavaḥ | 

śālyaḥ pitṛbhyaḥ satilāstaṇḍulāḥ parikīrttitāḥ | 

karambhalājau yakṣebhyo nārikelodakānvitam | 

śaktuṃ piṣṭañca nāgebhyo brahmaṇe paṅkajākṣatam | 

sapūpamannaṃ sarvāya viṣṇave syādguḍaudanam | 

tato lokeśvarebhyo'pi vidhivadvitaredbalim | 

dīkṣāyāmabhiṣekeṣu navaveśmapraveśane | 

utsaveṣu ca sampattyai vidadhyādaṅkurārpaṇam || 

eteṣāmarthaḥ || 

dīkṣādivaśāt pākasaptabhirdinairityanena dīkṣādinamaṣṭamaṃ yathā bhavati tathā 
karttavyamityuktam | 

vidhivadityanena navabhiḥ pañcabhistribhirvā ityāyātam || 

taduktaṃ siddhāntaśekhare || 

pratiṣṭhāyāñca dīkṣāyāṃ sthāpane cotsave tathā | 

samprokṣaṇe ca śāntyarthaṃ vivāhe mauñjibandhane | 

sarvamaṅgalakāryyeṣu kārayedaṅkurārpaṇam || 

mahākapilapañcarātre'pi || 

puṇyāhaghoṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā brāhmaṇaiḥ saha deśikaḥ | 

maṅgalāṅkurayajñañca kuryyāttatraiva vāhani || 

praśastayāgadivasāt purastāt saptame'hani śubhe navame vā | 

pañcame'ṭha sudine sumuhūrte maṅgalāṅkuravidhiṃ vidadhīta || 

gururviśuddhaḥ prāgeva śuddhāhāt saptame'hani | 

saṅkalpyopoṣya karttavyamaṅkurāropaṇaṃ śubham | 

kuryyānnāndīmukhaśrāddhaṃ pūrvedyuḥ svastivācanam | 

svagṛhyoktaprakāreṇa tadetadvidadhīta vai | 

saṃhitāyāmapi || 

sarvatrābhyudayaśrāddhamaṅkurotpādanaṃ tathā | 

ādāveva prakurvīta karmmaṇo'bhyudayātmanaḥ || 

śālāmiti viṃśatyā tu karairmānaṃ daśāyāmena viṣtṛteti śālāyā uttamamānam | 

atraitāvatyāḥ prayojanābhāvādarddhena madhyamamānena śālā kāryyā | 

tena daśahastadīrghā pañcahastāyāmā atra śālā karttavyā tāmeva 
pūrvāparāyātāmiti | 

dīrghaprahastacaturasrarūpāṃ gūḍhāṃ kapāṭādiparivṛtāṃ dakṣiṇaikadvāravatīṃ 
nivātāñca kuryyāt | 

<p. 212> taduktaṃ prayogasāre ||

arvādudaksthitāṃ kṛtvā nivātāṃ tāṃ kuṭīṃ dṛḍhāmiti | 

tatra maṇḍalakaraṇaprakāramāha | 

pañcahasteti pūrvapaścimakrameṇa pañcasūtrāṇi pātayitvā 
dvādaśāṅgulānyekaikāntaritānyekādaśasūtrāṇi dakṣiṇottarakrameṇa pātayet tena 
catvāriṃśatkoṣṭhā bhavanti | 

mārjayediti paścādanveti || 

paṃktyāvāhya catasro vīthīrmārjjayet mārjjanaprakārastu || 

prathamaṃ pūrvasyāṃ catuṣkoṣṭhātmikāmekāṃ vīthīm tato 
dakṣiṇasyāmaṣṭakoṣṭhātmikāmaparām paścimāyāṃ catuṣkoṣṭhātmikāmanyām 
uttarasyāmaṣṭakoṣṭhātmikāmitarāñca mārjayet | 

tataḥ antaścatuṣkasya ubhayapārśvayordve vīthyau dvidvikoṣṭharūpe punarmārjayet | 

tenātra maṇḍale catuṣkoṣṭhatrayamavaśiṣyate | 

tāni śiṣṭāni catuṣkoṣṭhatrayasthāni dvādaśapadāni pratyekaṃ catuṣkoṣṭhaṃ 
śvetādibhīrajobhīrañjayet | 

tatra vāyupade śvetaṃ āgneye pītaṃ nair(-)ṛte aruṇaṃ īśāne kṛṣṇam | 

tataḥ śyāmalena vīthīrāpūrayet | 

patrāṇīti sthūlānyuccāni śarāvāṇyeva pālikāśabdenocyante | 

pālikā eva nīcāḥ pañcamukhasthitāḥ pañcamukhya ucyante śarāvāḥ prasiddhā eva |

pālikā harirūpāḥ pañcamukhyo brahmarūpāḥ śarāvāḥ  śivarūpāḥ |

etena haribrahmeśā eṣu pātreṣu pūjyā ityuktaṃ bhavati | 

taduktaṃ sārasvatatantre || 

prokteṣu teṣu pātreṣu brahmaviṣṇuśivān yajet || 

siddhāntaśekhare'pi || 

sampūjayet śarāveṣu rudraṃ candanapuṣpakaiḥ | 

pālikāsu tathā viṣṇuṃ brahmāṇaṃ ghaṭikāsu ceti | 

utsedhata aunnatyena || 

mahākapilapañcarātre || 

pālikā vaktravistārā ṣoḍaśāṅgula ucyate | 

bhavetkaṇṭhavilañcāsyā gude'ṣṭāṅgulavistṛtam | 

pādapīṭhasya vistāraḥ ṣaḍaṅgula udāhṛtaḥ | 

caturaṅgula utsedhastatsandhiścāṅguladvayam | 

tatsandheśca bhavennāhaḥ pādapīṭhārddhameva ca | 

bhavet pañcamukhī caivaṃ ghaṭikā sarvakāmadā | 

<p. 213> caratuṅgulavistārāṇyāhurvaktrāṇi pañca vai | 

catvāri ca caturdikṣu ūrddhvamekaṃ yathāvidhi | 

ghaṭikāyāmavistāro dvādaśāṅgula ucyate | 

ācāryyāḥ kathayantyeke ṣoḍaśāṅgulameva vā | 

dvādaśāṅgulavistāraṃ śarāvasya mukhaṃ smṛtam | 

caturaṅgulavistāramadhastānmūlamucyate | 

rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtam | 

tālamātramapi pañcamukhī syādvyāsatocchrayamitā ghaṭikā syāt | 

dikṣu tanmukhacatuṣṭayamekaṃ madhyamantu samavarttitamāgam | 

alavistṛtamukhe tu śarāvaṃ vyāsatocchrayagatārddhamitāṅghriḥ | 

daṇḍamasya caraṅgulanāhaṃ kaṇṭhamadhyavilavarjjamadagram | 

sambhave kanakarūpyatāmrato mārttikāṇyabhinavānyathavā syuḥ || 

siddhāntaśekhare tu || 

yathāsambhavamānantu pālikādi samācaredityuktam | 

śubhānīti kṛṣṇavarṇavraṇādirahitānīti rāghavabhaṭṭaḥ | 

tantuneti triguṇena pūrvaṃ prakṣālanaṃ paścāttriguṇatantunā veṣṭanam | 

darbhakūrccamagre vakṣyamāṇam | 

paścimādīti paścimacatuṣke pālikācatuṣṭayam madhyamacatuṣke pañcamukhīcatuṣṭayam 
pūrvacatuṣke śarāvacatuṣṭayaṃ niveśayet | 

tanmadhye āgneyādisthāpanam | 

deśika ityanenoktam || 

taduktaṃ prayogasāre || 

evaṃ kṛtvā sādhakastu tatra pātrāṇi vinyaset | 

vahnyādīśānaparyyantaṃ catuṣkeṣu pṛthak pṛthak | 

karīṣetyādi || 

karīṣaṃ śuddhagomayacūrṇam || 

etairuttarottaraṃ sarvāṇyapi pātrāṇi pūrayet | 

taduktaṃ hayaśīrṣapañcarātre || 

pūrayeduttarottara iti | 

prayogasāre'pi | 

mṛdbālukākarīṣaiścorddhvataḥ pātrāṇi pūrayediti ||

atra viśeṣaḥ siddhāntaśekhare || 

gandhādibhiśca kuddālaṃ pūjayitvā dināntare | 

gītanṛtyasamāyuktaṃ gajavājisamanvitam | 

gurvādayo rathārūḍhā gajārūḍhāstathā pare | 

gatvā tīraṃ taḍāgasya nadyāḥ puṣpavanasya vā | 

tatra śuddhaṃ bhuvo bhāgaṃ darbhaiḥ sammārjya vāstrataḥ | 

abhyukṣya cārghyatoyena tattanmantramanusmaran | 

hṛdā bhūmiṃ samāvāhya gandhapuṣpaiḥ samarccayet | 

kuddālīmantramantreṇa khātvā bhūmimatho mṛdam |

<p. 214> gṛhītvā vāmadeśena pūrayet kāṃsyapātrake | 

hṛdā mṛdantu sammṛjya vastreṇācchādya dhārayet | 

puraṃ vā nilayaṃ vāpi sarvamaṅgalanisvanaiḥ | 

guruḥ pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā maṇḍapaṃ vānayettataḥ | 

tattatkarmma divākāle kuryyādrātrau na buddhimān | 

teṣu vījāropaṇamāha sudheti || 

sudhāvījena vamitimantreṇa | 

dugdhaiḥ prakṣālyeti | 

mahākapilapañcarātre tu viśeṣaḥ || 

dvādaśākṣaramantreṇa kṣālayitvā tu vāriṇeti || 

sārasvatamate'pi || 

vījāni tāni prakṣālya jalakṣīreṇa ca kramāditi | 

etatsarvaṃ tantravidityanena sūcitam | 

mūleti mūlamantreṇa abhijaptāni aṣṭottaraśatamityarthaḥ | 

taduktaṃ mahākapilapañcarātre || 

sakhyānuktau śataṃ sāṣṭaṃ sahasraṃ vā japādiṣviti || 

pañcaghoṣastu || 

paṭahakāṃsyamṛdaṅgamukhavādyaśaṅkhāḥ | 

maṅgalācāreti | 

caturaṅgaiḥ suprasiddhahulahuladhvanyādibhiḥ | 

tatastāni vījānyekīkṛtya rātrau prāṅmukha udaṅmukho vā mūlamantreṇa pālikādiṣu 
nirvapedityuktam | 

taduktaṃ siddhāntaśekhare | 

vījamukhyena mūlena prāṅmukho vāpyudaṅmukhaḥ | 

vāpayet sarvavījāni pālikādiṣvanukramāt | 

vījānāmadhipaḥ somastasmādrātrau tu nirvapet || 

sārasvatamate'pi || 

vījebhyo daivatebhyaśca sa rātrau kāntimān yataḥ | 

tasmād gurustu vījāni niśāyāmeva vāpayediti || 

vījānyāha śālīti | 

śālayo haimantikāḥ śyāmāḥ śyāmākāḥ | 

kānyakubjabhāṣāyāṃ sāmbā iti | 

gauḍabhāṣāyāṃ śāmā iti prasiddhiḥ | 

āḍhakī tuvarī arahara iti gauḍabhāṣāprasiddhiḥ | 

niṣpāvā vorā iti gauḍabhāṣāprasiddhiḥ |

sārasvatamate pratyekaṃ vījeṣu devatāpūjoktā yathā | 

skandaṃ priyaṅgau niṣpāve vāyumagniṃ kulatthake | 

āḍhakyāṃ nir(-)ṛtiṃ somaṃ mugdhe (!) vaivasvataṃ tile | 

prajāpatiṃ śālivīje tvanantaṃ sarṣape'rccayet | 

imaṃ śyāme ca māṣe ca varuṇantu nagātmaje (!) iti | 

<p. 215> siddhāntaśekhare tu pratyahaṃ somapūjājāpyuktā yathā | 

somaṃ sampūjayennityamadhivāsadināvadhi | 

adhivāsadine prāpte somamullāsayedgururiti | 

haririndreti tattanmantre uktaḥ | 

prayogasāre | 

tripañcakāya sarvāya śaṅkarāya śivāya ca | 

sarvaloka pradhānāya śāśvatāya namo namaḥ | 

vikīryyānena mantreṇa haridrā cūrṇamiśritam | 

toyaṃ pravarṣayetteṣu siñcet toyairdinaṃ prati | 

pratidinamityarthaḥ | 

vastrairnūtanavastraiḥ | 

bahuvacanaṃ kapiñjalādhikaraṇanyāyena tritve paryyavasyati | 

ācchādyeti pātracatuṣṭayamekaikena rātrau baliṃ kṣipedityanvayaḥ | 

prātaḥ punaḥ sthalamārjanādi kṛtvā dvitīyarātrādau balidānaṃ praṇavādyairiti tatra 
mantraḥ | 

oṃ bhūtebhyo nama evamanyatrāpi mantraḥ | 

mahākapilapañcarātre tu viśeṣaḥ | 

tato gandhavimiśreṇa siñcedvai śuddhavāriṇā | 

trirātrantu yathānyāyaṃ pañcarātramathāpi vā | 

sārasvatamate tu | 

prarūḍhānyaṅkurāṇyanyo na vīkṣeta kadācana | 

ācāryya eva praviśet śiṣyo vāpi tadā jñayeti || 

sidhāntaśekhare'pi || 

vastrairācchādya yatnena suguptāni ca kārayet | 

saptasu rātriṣu pṛthagbalidravyāṇyāha bhūtebhya iti | 

karambho dadhiśaktavaḥ | 

akṣatā akhaṇḍataṇḍulā iti rāghavabhaṭṭaḥ | 

śrāddhatattvadhṛtavacane akṣatā yavā iti | 

yadā navasu rātriṣu balidānaṃ tadā rātridvaye balidānam | 

dravyaṃ devatā ca puraivoktam | 

tattaddiśi pāyasādinā balirvidheyaḥ | 

tatreśānapūrvayormadhye brahmaṇe nair(-)ṛtapaścimayormadhye'nantāya balirdeyaḥ || 

athāṅkuraparīkṣā || 

siddhāntaśekhare | 

yajamānābhivṛddhyarthamaṅkurāṇi parīkṣayet | 

samyagarddhaṃ prarūḍhāni komalāni sitāni ca | 

dhūmravarṇānyapūrvāṇi tathā tiryyaggatāni ca | 

śyāmalāni ca kubjāni varjayedaśubhāni tu | 

āvṛṣṭiṃ kurute kṛṣṇaṃ dhūmrābhaṃ kalahaṃ tathā | 

apūrvaṃ jananāśañca kubhikṣaṃ śyāmalāṅkuram | 

tiryyaggate bhavedvyādhiḥ kubje śatrubhayaṃ tathā | 

<p. 216> sārasvatamate'pi || 

prarūḍhairaṅkuraiḥ kartturnirdiśecca śubhāśubham | 

śyāmaiḥ kṛṣṇairaṅkurairarthahānistiryyagrūḍhairvyādhirāndolitaistaiḥ | 

kubjairdu.khaṃ duṣprarūḍhairmṛtiśca rogaṃ bhugne sthānadeśeṣṭahāniriti || 

aśubhe cāṅkare jāte śāntihomaṃ samācaret | 

mūlamantreṇa juhuyād guruśca brāhmaṇaiḥ saha | 

aghorāstreṇa cāstreṇa śataṃ vātha sahasrakamityaṅkurāropaṇam ||

[iti śrīprāṇatoṣiṇyāṃ dvitīye dharmakāṇḍe 
guruśiṣyavicārādyaṅkurāropaṇāntakarmakathanarūpa(-)pallavakathanaṃ nāma tṛtīyaḥ 
paricchedaḥ] |



dīkṣāvyutpattistu rudrajāmale | 

dadāti śivatādātmyaṃ kṣiṇoti ca malatrayam | 

ato dīkṣeti samproktā dīkṣātantrārthavedibhiḥ || 

laghukalpasūtre ca || 

dīyate paramaṃ jñānaṃ kṣīyate pāpapaddhatiḥ | 

tena dīkṣocyate mantre svāgamārthabalābalāt | 

yoginī tantre tṛtīyabhāge ṣaṣṭhapaṭale'pi | 

dīyate jñānamatyarthaṃ kṣīyate pāśabandhanam | 

ato dīkṣeti deveśi (!) kathitā tattvacintakaiḥ | 

manasā karmaṇā vācā yat pāpaṃ samupārjitam | 

teṣāṃ viśeṣakaraṇī paramajñānadā yataḥ | 

tasmāddīkṣeti loke'smin gīyate śāstravedakaiḥ | 

vijñānaphaladā saiva dvitīyā layakāriṇī | 

tṛtīyā muktidā caiva tasmāddikṣeti dhīyate || 

viśvasāratantre dvitīyapaṭale ca || 

atha dīkṣāṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣva kamalānane (!) | 

yasya vijñāmātreṇa devatvaṃ labhate naraḥ | 

divyajñānaṃ yato dadyāt kuryyāt pāpakṣayaṃ tataḥ | 

tasmāddīkṣeti sā proktā savatantrasya sammatā || 

atha dīkṣāmāhātmyam | 

rudrajāmalapūrvakhaṇḍe tṛtīyapaṭale || 

devi (!) dīkṣāvihīnasya na siddhirna ca saṅgatiḥ | 

tasmāt sarvaprayatnena guruṇā dīkṣito bhavet | 

vicāraṃ cakrasārasya karaṇīyamavaśyakam | 

adīkṣito'pi maraṇe rauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet | 

<p. 217> tasmāddīkṣāṃ prayatnena sadā kuryyācca tāntrikīm || 

pañcadaśapaṭale || 

adīkṣitā ye kurvanti japapūjādikāḥ kriyāḥ | 

na bhavanti priye (!) teṣāṃ śilāyāmuptavījavat | 

kalpasūtraṭīkādhṛtakulārṇavatantre || 

rasendreṇa yathā viddhamayaḥ suvarṇatāṃ vrajet | 

dīkṣāviddhastathaivātmā śivatvaṃ labhate priye (!) | 

dīkṣāgnidagdhakarmāsau yāyādvicchinnabandhanaḥ | 

gatastasya karmabandho nirjīvaśca śivo bhavet | 

gataṃ śūdrasya śūdratvaṃ viprasyāpi ca vipratā | 

dīkṣāsaṃskārasambhinne jātibhedo na vidyate | 

śivaliṅge śilābuddhiṃ kurvan yatpāpamāpnuyāt | 

dīkṣitasyāpi pūrvatvaṃ smaran tatpāpamāpnuyāt | 

dārvaśmalauhamṛdratnajātiliṅgaṃ pratiṣṭhitam | 

yathocyate tathā śuddhāḥ sarve varṇāśca dīkṣitāḥ | 

yena pūjitamātreṇa cābrahmabhuvanāntikam | 

pūjitaṃ tena sarvaṃ syāddīkṣitena na saṃśayaḥ | 

dīkṣitasya na kāryyaṃ syāttapobhirniyamavrataiḥ | 

na tīrthagamanenāpi na ca śārīrayantraṇaiḥ || 

pañcamakhaṇḍe ṣaṣṭhollāse'pi | 

gavāṃ sarpiḥ śarīrasthaṃ na karotyātmapoṣaṇam | 

svakarmacaritaṃ daṇḍaṃ punastāmeva poṣayet | 

evaṃ sarvaśarīrasthasarpirvat parameśvari (!) | 

vinā copāsanāddevi (!) na dadāti phalaṃ nṛṇām || 

puraścaraṇarasollāse prathamapaṭale | 

na ca dīkṣāparaṃ jñānaṃ na ca dīkṣāparaṃ tapaḥ | 

na ca dīkṣāparaṃ kālaṃ tasmāddīkṣā garīyasī || 

iti dīkṣāpraśaṃsā || 

dīkṣāyāṃ vāratithinakṣatramāsādibhadrābhadre 
asmadatyativṛddhaprapitāmahakṛṣṇānandāgamavāgīśena tantrasāre likhite te tatra 
draṣṭavye | 

viśeṣastu puraścaraṇarasollāse || 

dīkṣāyāṃ cañcalāpāṅgi (!) na kālaniyamaḥ kvacit | 

sadgurordarśanādeva sūryyaparva ca sarvadā | 

śiṣyamāhūya guruṇā kṛpayā yadi dīyate | 

tatra lagnādikaṃ kiñcinna vicāryyaṃ kadācana | 

etat sarvaṃ divyavīraviṣayam | 

tathā ca itaḥ pūrvam | 

divyavīraguruṃ labdhvā dīkṣāṃ kṛtvā vicakṣaṇaḥ | 

<p. 218> tataḥ samāhito bhūtvā puraścaryyāṃ kariṣyati | 

ityuktam etena paśuguruto dīkṣāyāṃ sarvaṃ vicāryyamityāyātam | 

kalpasūtraṭīkādhṛtakulārṇave || 

dvijo'pi dīkṣitaḥ paścādantyajaḥ pūrvadīkṣitaḥ | 

dvijaḥ kaniṣṭhaḥ sa jyeṣṭha iti śāstrasya nirṇayaḥ | 

jyeṣṭhatvena ca te pūjyā nāvamānyāḥ kathañcana || 

iti dīkṣājyeṣṭhatvādikathanam || 

kulārṇave'pi || 

dīkṣāṃ vinā na mokṣaḥ syāt prāṇināṃ śivaśāsanāt | 

sā ca na syādvinācāryyamityācāryyaparamparā | 

upāsanāśatenāpi yāṃ vinā naiva sidhyati | 

tāṃ dīkṣāmāśrayed yatnāt śrīgurormantrasiddhaye || 

mahākapilapañcarātranārāyaṇīyayoḥ || 

mantro yaḥ sādhayedekaṃ japahomārcanādibhiḥ | 

kriyābhirbhūribhiryasya sidhyantyanye'lpasādhanāt | 

samyak siddhaikamantrasya nāsādhyamiha kiñcana | 

bahumantravataḥ puṃsaḥ kā kathā hānireva saḥ | 

ityekamantrasādhanaphalaṃ rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtam || 

pustake likhito mantro yena sundari (!) japyate | 

na tasya jāyate siddhirhānireva pade pade || 

ṣaṭkarmadīpikādhṛtamapi || 

pustake likhitā vidyā yena sundari (!) japyate | 

siddhirna jāyate tasya kalpakoṭiśatairapi | 

rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtam || 

dvijānāmanupetānāṃ ṣaṭkarmādhyayanādiṣu | 

yathādhikāro nāstīhasyāccopanayanādanu | 

tathācādīkṣitānāñca mantradevārcanādiṣu | 

nādhikāro'styataḥ kuryyādātmānaṃ śivasaṃskṛtamiti || 

dīkṣāyā akaraṇe doṣakathanam || 

nārāyaṇīye || 

yadṛcchayā śrutaṃ mantraṃ channenāpi chalena vā | 

patrekṣitaṃ vā yathāvattajjaghanyamanarthakṛt || 

iti nindyadīkṣākathanam || 

rāghavabhaṭṭe'pi || 

viprasya vidhivaddīkṣāmabhiṣekāvrasānikām | 

śrutvā tantraṃ gurorlabdhaṃ sādhayeddīkṣitaṃ manum | 

gurumukhyāḥ kriyāḥ sarvā bhuktimuktiphalapradāḥ | 

tasmāt sevyo gururnityaṃ muktyarthaṃ susamāhitaiḥ | 

gurvanuktāḥ kriyāḥ sarvā niṣphalāḥ syuryato dhruvam | 

<p. 219> japo homārcanavidhiḥ kāryyo dīkṣānvitairnaraiḥ || 

iti dīkṣāvidhikathanam ||

mūlasūtram || 

dīkṣāstisraḥ śāktī śāmbhavī māntrī ceti | 

tatra śāktī śaktipraveśanāt śāmbhavī caraṇavinyāsāt māntrī mantropadiṣṭā sarvāśca 
kuryyādekaikamityeke | 

etadvyācakṣate śrīśaṅkarācāryyāḥ | 

śāktīti śaktiḥ kuṇḍalinī paracidrūpā tasyāḥ kriyāsamabhivyāhāreṇa 
kulākulabhedādbrahmanāḍyāṃ paraśambhumelanaṃ śaktipraveśaḥ | 

śāmbhavī caraṇavinyāsyāditi te caraṇāścatvāraḥ śuklaraktamiśraparasvarūpāḥ | 

māntrī mantropadiṣṭeti | 

taduktaṃ rudrajāmale || 

śāktī śaktibhavā dīkṣā śaktiḥ śrīparakuṇḍalī | 

tasyāḥ prāṇavilomena praveśaḥ paraśāmbhave | 

caraṇadvayasambhūtā śāmbhavī śīghrasiddhidā | 

māntrī mantrodbhavā dīksā tacchaktiḥ svātmasambhavā | 

mantrayantrārccanādukta kriyābhirbhogamokṣadā | 

caraṇadvayaṃ śivaśaktirūpam | 

tatra sattvapradhānaḥ śuklaḥ rajaḥpradhāno raktaḥ tamaḥpradhāno miśraḥ | 

guṇātīto nirvāṇaḥ | 

sākṣāt paramānandanirbharaḥ sadāśivasvarūpaḥ | 

sahasradalakamalakarṇikā(-)madhyavartti(-)
candramaṇḍalagatapīyūṣadhārāplāvyamānāścintanīyāḥ | 

uktañca bhagavatā dattātreyeṇa ||

bhrūmadhyagau vidhiharau tava śuklaraktau pādau rajomalaguṇau khalu sevyamānau | 

sṛṣṭisthitī vitanuto hṛdaye tṛtīyamādyaṃ bhajan harati viśvamudagramugraḥ | 

tūryaṃ tavāryyacaraṇaṃ nirupādhibodhyam sāndrāmṛtaṃ śivapade satataṃ nato'smi || 

rudrajāmale || 

trividhā sā bhaveddīkṣā prathamā āṇavī parā | 

śāktī ca śāmbhavī cānyā sadyo muktividhāyinī | 

mantrārcanāsananyāsadhyānopacārakādibhiḥ || 

dīkṣā sā āṇavī proktā yathāśāstroktarūpiṇī | 

siddhyai ca śaktimālokya tayā kevalayā śiśoḥ | 

nirupāyaṃ kṛtā dīkṣā śākteyī parikīrttitā | 

<p. 220> abhidandhiṃ vinācāryyaśiṣyayorubhayorapi | 

deśikānugraheṇaiva śiṣyatā vyāptikāriṇī || 

ṣaḍanvayamahāratne'pi || 

trividhā sā bhaveddīkṣā prathamā āṇavī parā | 

śākteyī śāmbhavī cānyā sadyomuktividhāyinī | 

mantrārcanāsanadhyānasthāpanopāsanādibhiḥ | 

kṛtā dīkṣā sā tvāṇavī proktā yathāśāstroktarūpiṇī | 

siddhyai svaśaktimālokya tayā kevalayā śiśoḥ | 

nirupāyaṃ kṛtā dīkṣā śākteyī parikīrttitā | 

abhisandhiṃ vinācāryyaśiṣyayorubhayorapi | 

deśikānugraheṇaiva śivatāvyaktikāriṇī | 

seyantu śāmbhavī dīkṣā śivādeśanakāriṇī | 

vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmapi | 

śāmbhavī caiva śaktī ca māntrī caiva śivāgame | 

dīkṣopadiśyate tredhā śivena paramātmanā | 

gurorālokamātreṇa sparśāt sambhāṣaṇādapi | 

sadyaḥ saṃjñā bhavejjantordīkṣā sā śāmbhavī matā | 

śāktī jñānavati dīkṣā śiṣyadehaṃ praviśya tu | 

guruṇā jñānamārgeṇa kriyate jñānacakṣuṣā | 

māntrī kriyāvatī dīkṣā kumbhamaṇḍalapūrvikā || 

iti nāmakriyābhedāddīkṣābhedakathanam || 

dīkṣābhedāntarantu śāradāyāṃ caturthapaṭale || 

caturvidhā sā sandiṣṭā kriyāvatyādibhedataḥ | 

kriyāvatī varṇamayī kalātmā vedhamayyapi || 

prayogasāre || 

mantramārgānusāreṇa sākṣātkṛtveṣṭadevatām | 

guruścodbodhayecchiṣyaṃ mantradīkṣeti socyate || 

sūtrāntaramāha sarvāśca kuryyādekaikāṃ vetyeke || 

rudrajāmale || 

āṇavī bahudhā dīkṣā śāmbhavī ca tathā punaḥ | 

ekaikā vāpi vidvadbhiḥ paṭhyate śāstrakovidaiḥ | 

śivaśaktisamāyogājjanmāntarakṛtāt śubhāt | 

śivapūjānusandhānāt karmasāmyaṃ yadā bhavet | 

śiva eva tadā sākṣādāṇavyā dīkṣayā bhavet | 

iti dīkṣāphalakathanam || 

viśvasāratantre || 

caturvidhā tu sā dīkṣā brahmaṇā bhāṣitā purā | 

kriyāvatī kalāvatī varṇavedhamayī gunaḥ | 

<p. 221> tāḥ krameṇāpi kathyante sarvasampatpradāḥ śubhāḥ || 

evaṃ sarvāsāṃ dīkṣāṇāṃ mānasādikaivalyāntaṃ samānameva phalam || 

taduktaṃ rudrajāmale || 

sarvāsāseva dīkṣāṇāṃ muktiḥ phalamakhaṇḍitam | 

avirodhādbhavatyeṣā prāsaṅgikyastu bhaktayaḥ ||

athādhivāsavidhiḥ || 

tatrādau gṛhapraveśānantaraṃ vighnanivāraṇapūrvakaṃ sūryyaḥ somādi paṭhitvā 
bhūrasītyādi mantrairghaṭasthāpanaṃ kuryyāt | 

taduktaṃ viśvasāratantre tṛtīyapaṭale || 

guruṇā vātmanā śiṣyaḥ sarvaṃ kuryyādvidhānataḥ | 

guruṇā kārayet karma śiṣyaḥ sarvaṃ haret sudhīḥ | 

candanāgurukarpūrairdhūpayed yāgamaṇḍapam | 

gṛhaṃ praviśya vidhinā tatra vighnān vināśayet | 

kṛtāñjalipuṭo bhūtvā mantramenaṃ samuccaret | 

oṃ sūryyaḥ somo yamaḥ kālo mahābhūtāni pañca ca | 

ete śubhāśubhasyeha karmaṇo nava sākṣiṇaḥ | 

ghaṭasyāropaṇaṃ kuryyānmantreṇānena deśikaḥ | 

ghaṭāropaṇamantro'yaṃ sarvatantreṣu sammataḥ | 

oṃ bhūrasi bhūmirasyaditirasi viśvadhā yā viśvasya bhuvanasya dhartrī pṛthivīṃ yaccha 
pṛthivīṃ dṛṃha pṛthivīṃ mā hiṃsīḥ bhūmiśodhanamityuktaṃ sarvatantrasamanvitam | 

mantreṇānena deveśi | 

śukladhānyaṃ vinikṣipet | 

oṃ dhānyamasi dhinuhi devān dhinuhi yajñaṃ dhinuhi yajñapatiṃ dhinuhi māṃ 
yajñanam | 

oṃ prāṇo mantro dā nāma tvā kāmāya matvā dīrghā nanu pramitidaśānumeṣāṃ 
devo vaḥ savitā hiraṇyapāṇiḥ | 

pratigṛhṇātyacchidreṇa śilācakṣuṣe tvā mahī māṃ payo'si | 

hastamānaṃ ghaṭaṃ kṛtvā navaratnodareṇa ca | 

ghaṭaṃ saṃsthāpayettatra mantreṇānena deśikaḥ | 

oṃ ājighra kalasaṃ mahyātmā viśantundavam | 

punarūrjāni varttasva sā naḥ || 

sahasraṃ dhukṣorudhārā payasvatī punarmāviśatā drayiḥ | 

mantreṇānena deveśi (!) ghaṭaṃ saṃsthāpayettataḥ | 

muktāmāṇikyavaidūryyagomedavajravidrumān | 

padmarāgaṃ marakataṃ nīlañcaiva kramānnava | 

navaratnāni tatraiva nikṣiped vibhāvāvadhi |

<p. 222> merutantre'pi | 

puṣpaṃ nīlañca vaidūryyaṃ vidrumaṃ mauktikaṃ tathā | 

tato marakataṃ vajraṃ gomedaṃ padmarāgakam | 

proktāni navaratnāni deśikairmantravittamaiḥ | 

oṃ āpyāyasva sametu te viśvataḥ somavṛṣṭyaṃ bhavāvājasya saṅgaye | 

mantreṇānena deveśi (!) jalaṃ dadyādghaṭopari | 

oṃ dhannanā gā dhannanā jiñjayema dhannanā tīvrāḥ samado jayema | 

dhanuḥ śatrorapakāmaṃ kuṇotu dhannanāḥ sarvāḥ pradiśo jayema | 

mantreṇānena deveśi (!) śākhāṃ dadyādghaṭopari | 

tāmbūlaṃ śrīphalaṃ dadyāt kadalīṃ vā viśeṣataḥ || 

oṃ śrīśca te lakṣmīśca patnyā ahorātre pārśve nakṣatrāṇi rūpamaśvinau | 

vyāptamiṣṭumīśānamuṃ ma īśānaḥ sarvalokamaheśānaḥ || 

mantreṇānena deveśi (!) ghaṭe puṣpaṃ vinikṣipet | 

saphalaṃ śālicūrṇañca śarāvañca ghaṭopari | 

vinikṣipyātha vidhinā kṣīraśākhisamanvitam | 

suvastrācchādanaṃ kuryyānmantreṇānena sādhakaḥ | 

oṃ vastrañca pāvanaṃ śvetaṃ bahutantuvinirmitam | 

mayā niveditaṃ bhaktyā vastrañca pratigṛhyatām | 

kvāthito jambucūrṇena indraballīdalena ca | 

ghaṭaṃ saṃveṣṭya vidhinā sindūrādivibhūṣitam | 

sthāpayettaṃ ghaṭaṃ mantramuccārya yatnataḥ sudhīḥ || 

kvāthito jambu cūrṇeneti pradarśanārthamuktamanyenāpi tadviśeṣo merutantre 
tṛtīyaprakāśe || 

pañcāśadoṣadhīḥ kvāthaiḥ pūrayettadanantaram | 

ekena sahitāstāśca varṇauṣadhyaḥ kramādimāḥ | 

candanañcāpi raktākhyacandanañcāgurustathā | 

karpūrośaurakuṣṭhāni bālakañcāpi keśaram | 

kakkolakaṃ jātiphalaṃ jaṭāmāṃsīṃ murāṃ tathā | 

granthikaṃ rocanā jātīpatraṃ pippalavilvakau | 

pṛśniparṇī citrakañca karaṅgaṃ kattṛṇaṃ tathā | 

kaṭphalañcāpi guñjā ca dadruhaṃ vakakāśmarī | 

padmakoṣaṃ śaṅkhapuṣpī mayūrasya śikhā tathā |

plakṣo'gnimanthaśca tathā mudgaparṇī kuśāḥ punaḥ | 

pāṣāṇabhedakaḥ kṛṣṇaśaṅkhapuṣpī ca rohiṇī | 

śyoṇākovṛhatī caiva pāṭalī mukhakarṇikā | 

<p. 223> tulasī śikharā indravallī bhṛṅgābhidhā punaḥ | 

tato'parājitā tālamūlī cāpi kṛtāñjalī | 

dūrvā śrīdevakanyākhyā bhārgyākhyā bhadramustakam | 

lavaṅgarahitāścaitāḥ pañcāśatsaṃkhyakā iti || 

yadyapi prathamakāṇḍe pañcāśadoṣadhayo 
nirūpitāstathāpyekapañcāśattvaprāptyarthamuktasya durūhatvanivṛttaye 
kramabhedajñāpanāya ca punardarśitāḥ | 

dugdhavṛkṣatvacaḥ kvāthairbrahmavṛkṣodbhavaistathā | 

gandhapaṅkaprasūnādivāsitairvāśṛtairjalairiti | 

tato ghaṭaṃ sthirīkuryyāt yāvaddīkṣāvidhirbhavet | 

oṃ sthiro bhava viḍaṅga āśurbhava vāyvarvan pṛthurbhava suṣadasthamagne 
purīśarāhanaḥ | 

mantreṇānena deveśi (!) sthirīkaraṇamuttamam | 

tataḥ kṛtāñjalirbhūtvā mantramenaṃ paṭhettataḥ | 

oṃ gurubhyo gaṇapatibhyaśca vo namo virūpebhyo viśvarūpebhyaśca vo namaḥ | 

vrātebhyo vrātapatibhyaśca namaḥ | 

namo gṛhyebhyo gṛhyapatibhyaśca vo namaḥ | 

puṇyāhaṃ vācayedādau svastivācanapūrvakam || 

oṃ svasti na indro vṛddhaśravāḥ svasti naḥ pūṣā viśvavedāḥ svasti 
nastārkṣo'riṣṭanemiḥ | 

svasti no vṛhaspatirdadhātu || 

astrāya phaḍiti mantreṇa bhaumān vighnān vidārayet | 

divyadṛṣṭyā divyavighnān dūrayecca caturdiśi divyadṛṣṭilakṣaṇaṃ tatraiva | 

animeṣākṣidṛṣṭiśca divyadṛṣṭiḥ prakīrttitā | 

māṣabhaktena cānnena yāvakena ca saṃyutam | 

ajākarṇasya raktena dugdhena madhureṇa ca | 

māṣabhaktabaliṃ dadyādbhūtapretapiśācake | 

oṃ bhūtāḥ pretāḥ piśācāśca pātālabhūtale sthitāḥ | 

parvatāgre tathā vṛkṣe gṛhakṣetrādiṣu sthitāḥ | 

tuṣṭā bhavantu te sarve mama gṛhṇantvimaṃ balim | 

yajecca raktakusumairaktamāhiṃṣaśoṇitaiḥ | 

matsyamāṃsādibhirbhṛṣṭairdugdhaiśca vidhināmunā | 

baliṃ dadyācca tatrāpi tadā maṅgalamārabhet | 

dīkṣāyā dharmmakarmmarūpatvena tatra vighnanirāsa ukto yoginītantre 
uttarakhaṇḍe'ṣṭamapaṭale | 

tīrthe prāsādakaraṇe dharmārambhe viśeṣataḥ | 

<p. 224> vratayajñasamārambhe vighnāni nivasanti vai | 

tāni sampūjayedādau balibhirmodakādibhiḥ | 

anyathā jāyate vighnamiti jānīhi me priye (!) | 

athāparāṇi vighnāni śarīre nivasanti vai | 

mānasājñānanirhārāstāni śṛṇu mama priye (!) | 

kaścinnivarttako devi (!) kaścit pravarttakastathā | 

sannikarṣaṃ vidūraṃ vā sahasraṃ phalameva vā | 

pāpānusaraṇañcaiva ālasyenāpi dūṣaṇam | 

śokamohajvarāvyādhitāruṇyaṃ dhanalālasā | 

kalahaṃ bhāryyayā sārddhaṃ durbhikṣaṃ gṛhasaṅgaṭam | 

nānāvratasamākīrṇaṃ dhārmiko'smītimānasaḥ | 

prāptaśokastu dharmmasya kāraṇe hīnapatnikam | 

vṛkṣapatrañca tulasī dhātrī vṛkṣaphalaṃ tathā | 

śālagrāmaṃ śilākhaṇḍaṃ pratimāṃ dārujāṃ tathā | 

mānuṣaṃ brāhmaṇañcaiva svayambhūvarttulāṃ śilām | 

śaṅkhaṃ śambūkabhedañca khaḍgasya māṃsasambhavam | 

dṛṣṭyādāvābhavedevaṃ tīrthajātaṃ jalaṃ tathā | 

gaṅgāyāṃ vā nadīrūpaṃ puṇyakṣetrañca bhūmikām | 

ityetāni ca vighnāni saṃyānti ca punaḥ punaḥ | 

pūjayitvā vidhānena dvāradeśe na sādhayet | 

kavacenaiva vījena saptakṛtvo japena ca | 

niḥsāryya vighnān tān sarvān tadā rakṣāṃ diśet punaḥ || 

viśvasāratantre || 

ātmānaṃ rakṣayet tatra vahniprācīrayogataḥ | 

vahnivīje caturdikṣu prācīraṃ bhāvayeddhiyā | 

tadā tālatrayaṃ dadyādvahnivījena deśikaḥ | 

ūrddhvorddhva krama yogena gurūṃśca praṇamettataḥ | 

oṃ gurubhyo namo nityaṃ tadā ca gurupādukām | 

paraṃ parāparaṃ devi (!) parātparamataḥ param | 

krameṇa praṇameddhīmān oṃkārādi udīritān | 

gaṇeśaṃ dakṣiṇe natvā sammukhe ceṣṭavatām | 

gaṇeśaṃ sthāpayettasya pūrvokte tu ghaṭe sudhīḥ | 

āvāhamyahaṃ devaṃ gaṇeśaṃ śivanandanam | 

vyāghracarmāmbaradharaṃ vyālayajñopavītinam | 

gaṇakoṭyā parivṛtaṃ devaṃ modakabhojanam | 

avighneśaṃ mahākāyaṃ sarvalokaikapūjitam | 

balinaṃ sarvaśāntyarthaṃ raudramāvāhayāmyaham | 

<p. 225> ehyehi bhagavan (!) deva (!) sarvavighnavināśaka (!) | 

imāṃ pūjāṃ gṛhāṇa tvaṃ sarvaṃ me saphalaṃ kuru | 

sindūraraktapuṣpādyaiḥ pūjayecchivanandanam | 

añjalyāvāhayedādau sarvatrāyaṃ vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ | 

svāgatādi tataḥ praśnaṃ pratyuttarasamanvitam | 

āsanantu tato dattvā pādyamarghyaṃ nivedayet | 

oṃ pādyaṃ suvāsitaṃ deva (!) sarvabhūtasukhāvaham | 

dūrayātrāśramaharaṃ pādyaṃ tatpratigṛhyatām | 

pādyaṃ namaḥ pādayuge svāhārghyaṃ śirasi sthitam | 

madhuparkaṃ svadhānte tu punarācamanīyakam | 

snānīyañca namo brūyāt punargandhādikaṃ tataḥ | 

vastraṃ yajñopavītañca namo'nte parikalpayet | 

annaṃ bhakṣyaṃ svadhānte tu dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ vidhānataḥ | 

krameṇa nikṣipettatra devāya vidhināmunā | 

arghyapātre pradāyārghyaṃ tataḥ śṛṇu varānane (!) | 

arghyodakaṃ bhavecchaṅkhe syāmākayavadūrvake | 

nānāgandhasamāyuktaṃ kuśāṃśca tilasarṣapam | 

viṣṇukrāntāñca yavakaṃ ṣaḍarghyo'yaṃ prakīrttitaḥ | 

oṃ kāṇḍāccaiva mantreṇa arghyaṃ dadyācca sādhakaḥ | 

pādyamantreṇa kurvīta tathā cācamanīyakam | 

oṃ namaḥ svāhāsamāyuktaṃ brahmatvaphalahetave | 

mayā niveditaṃ bhaktyā madhuparkaṃ gṛhāṇa me | 

mūlamantreṇa sarvatra oṃkārādinamo'nvite || 

chāndasatvānna varṇanāśaḥ | 

oṃkārādinamo'nvitena mūlamantreṇetyarthaḥ || 

dadhnā ca madhusarpirbhyāṃ madhuparkaṃ prakīrttitaḥ | 

yajñopavītamantreṇa dadyādyajñopavītakam | 

yajñopavītamantrastu || 

oṃ yajñopavītaṃ paramaṃ pavitraṃ vṛhaspateryat sahajaṃ purastāt | 

āyuṣyamagraṃ pratimuñca śubhraṃ yajñopavītaṃ balamastu tejaḥ || 

oṃ nānāratnaiśca ghaṭitaṃ viśvakarmādinirmitam | 

sarvāṅgaśobhābharaṇaṃ gṛhāṇa phalahetave | 

oṃ vanaspatirasotpanno gandhāḍhyo gandha uttamaḥ | 

āghreyaḥ sarvadevānāṃ dhūpo'yaṃ pratigṛhyatām | 

oṃ suprakāśo mahādīpaḥ sarvatastimirāpahaḥ | 

savāhyābhyāntaraṃ jyotirdīpo'yaṃ pratigṛhyatām | 

<p. 226> pradīpāvalibhiḥ kuryyānnikaṭe dhūpadīpakau | 

prakṣālanāya hastasya paścātpātraṃ kṣipet sudhīḥ | 

śātakumbhaprapūrṇena maṇḍapaṃ racayet sudhīḥ | 

gaṇeśaṃ pūjayitvā ca arkaṃ viṣṇuṃ śivaṃ śivām | 

pūjayedvidhinā tatra ghaṭe sarvasamṛddhaye | 

kūrccamuṣṭiṃ kṣipedādau phaḍmantreṇa tu sādhakaḥ | 

tilān kuśān sarṣapāṃśca kūrccamuṣṭiḥ prakīrttitā | 

vikirān prakṣipettatra sapta sapta śarāṇunā || 

vikirā dīkṣāyāṃ bakṣyante || 

śarāṇunā astramantreṇa | 

tato guruḥ sa vedyāntu gatvā svasyāsane viśet || 

merutantre tṛtīyaprakāśe || 

sañcintyamantreṇa mūrtimevaṃ tasmin gurūttamaḥ | 

tasyāṃ mūrttau samāvāhya pūjayediṣṭadevatām | 

smṛtvā mūlamanuṃ mantrī samyaṅnāḍīmukhāttataḥ | 

nirgamasya ca caitanyaṃ nāsikāgravinirgatam | 

añjalau mātṛkāpadme mahaḥ puṣpānvite guruḥ | 

mastakāstaṃ samutthāpya tasyāmāvāhayettataḥ | 

saṃsthāpanaṃ sannidhānaṃ sannirodhaṃ tataścaret | 

sammukhīkaraṇañcāpi sakalīkaraṇaṃ tataḥ | 

tato'vaguṇṭhanaṃ kāryyamamṛtīkaraṇaṃ tataḥ | 

paramīkaraṇaṃ kuryyāt kramaśaḥ svasvamudrayā | 

kramādvidadhyācca tata upacārān prakalpayet | 

āsanādi prabhūtānāṃ upacārān prakalpya ca | 

tato mantravidhāneṣu proktāṅgāvaraṇānyapi || 

punaḥ pūrvādikāṣṭhāsu kramādastrādikān yajet | 

lokapālān maṇḍaleṣu kumbhopari ca tatra vā | 

airāvataṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ vāmanaṃ kumudāñjanau | 

puṣpadantaṃ sārvabhaumaṃ supratīkaṃ tadagrataḥ | 

sarvānte lokapālānāṃ tadastrāṇāṃ prapūjanam | 

indrādipūjanāt pūrvaṃ brāhmyādipūjanamapi | 

tathā ca viśvasāre || 

aṣṭadikṣu cāṣṭa kumbhān kvāthatoyaprapūritān | 

brāhmyādimātṛkāḥ pūjyāḥ sarvatantrasamanvitāḥ | 

jñātvā jñānavidhānena gandhapuṣpānnavāribhiḥ | 

daṇḍaṃ kamaṇḍalukaramakṣasūtrābhayaṃ tathā | 

bibhratī kanakacchāyāṃ brāhmī kṛṣṇājinojjvalā || 1 || 

śūlaṃ kapālaṃ paraśuṃ nṛṇāṃ potaṃ manoharam | 

<p. 227> vahantī hemasaṅkāśā dhyeyā māheśvarī śubhā || 2 || 

aṅkuśaṃ daṇḍakhaṭvāṅgaṃ pāśañca dadhatī karaiḥ | 

dhyeyā bandhūkasaṅkāśā kaumārī śarmmadāyinī || 3 || 

cakraṃ ghaṇṭāṃ gadāṃ khaḍgaṃ bibhratī sumanoharā | 

tamālaśyāmalā dhyeyā vaiṣṇavī śarmmadāyinī || 4 || 

muṣalaṃ karabālañca kheṭakaṃ dadhatī halam | 

karaiścaturbhirvārāhī dhyeyā kālaghanacchaviḥ || 5 || 

aṅkuśaṃ tomaraṃ vidyut kuliśaṃ bibhratī karaiḥ | 

indranīlanimendrāṇī dhyeyā sarvasamṛddhidā || 6 || 

śūlaṃ kapālaṃ nṛśiraḥ kapālaṃ dadhatī karaiḥ | 

muṇḍasraṅmaṇḍitā dhyeyā cāmuṇḍā raktavigrahā || 7 || 

akṣasrajaṃ vījapūraṃ kapālaṃ paṅkajaṃ karaiḥ | 

vahantī hemasaṅkāśā mahālakṣmīharipriyā || 8 || 

merutantre | 

vidhāya pūjāmevaṃ hi dhūpadīpanivedyakaiḥ | 

kṛte nivedye ca tato maṇḍalaṃ paritaḥ kramāt | 

prabālāṅkurapatrāṇi sthāpanīyāni mantriṇā | 

vedikāyā dakṣabhāge kṛtvā sthaṇḍilamuttamam | 

saṃsthāpya vahniṃ saṃskṛtya vaiśvadevaṃ samārabhet | 

yathāvidhi tatastatra gandhādyairdevatāṃ yajet | 

tataḥ praṇavapūrvābhirhutvā vyāhṛtibhiḥ purā | 

juhuyānmūlamantreṇa pāyasena ghṛtena ca | 

pañcaviṃśatibhiḥ paścāddhutvā vyāhṛtibhiḥ purā | 

sampūjya tāṃ candanādyaiḥ pīṭhamūrtau niyojayet | 

agniṃ visṛjya śiṣṭena pāyasena baliṃ haret | 

pārśvadebhyaścandanādipuṣpākṣatasamanvitam | 

tata utthāpya naivedyaṃ tatsthalaṃ śodhayet sudhīḥ | 

punaryajeccopacāraiḥ pañcabhirdarśayettataḥ | 

darpaṇaṃ vyajanaṃ chatraṃ cāmaraṃ mukhavāsakam | 

sahasrañca japenmantrī mūlamantraṃ samāhitaḥ || 

viśvasāre || 

jalaṃ samarpya vidhinā tadā kurvīta maṅgalam | 

dvijastrīṇāṃ brāhmaṇānāmāśīrvādaṃ pragṛhya ca | 

jayaśabdairbrahmaśabdairbrāhmaṇān paritoṣya ca | 

bandhuvargaṃ pratoṣyātha adhivāsaṃ tato diśet | 

mantreṇānena dravyeṇa krameṇa deśikottamaḥ | 

adhivāsaṃ akurvīta śiṣyasya sāvadhānataḥ | 

<p. 228> oṃkāraṃ pūrvamuccāryya namo'ntena samarpayet | 

oṃ bhūrasīti ca mantreṇa mṛttikāṃ prakṣipettataḥ | 

oṃ gandhadvārāṃ durādharṣāṃ nityapuṣṭāṃ karīṣiṇīm | 

īśvarīṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ tvāmihopahvaye śriyam | 

gandhaṃ dadyācca vidhinā mantreṇānena sādhakaḥ |

oṃ praparvatasya vṛṣabhasya puṣṭāṃ vāvaścarantī śaśi ca iyānātā 
āvṛttamadadhāgudaktā | 

ahiṃ vradhnamanurīṣamaṇāviṣṇorvikramaṇamasi viṣṇorvikrāntamasi viṣṇoḥ krāntamasi 
vaiṣṇave tvā || 

mantreṇānena vidhinā śilāṃ dadyādvicakṣaṇaḥ | 

dhānyamantreṇa dhānyasya dānaṃ tantreṣu sammatam | 

oṃ kāṇḍāt kāṇḍāt prarohantī puruṣaṃ puruṣaḥ pavi | 

etān dūrve pratanu sahasreṇa śatena ca | 

mantreṇānena deveśi (!) dūrvāṃ dadyādvidhānataḥ | 

oṃ śrīśca teti ca mantreṇa puṣpaṃ dadyādvidhānataḥ | 

oṃ yāḥ phalinīti ca mantreṇa phalaṃ dadyādvicakṣaṇaḥ | 

oṃ dadhikrāvro'tyakārṣaṃ jiṣṇoraśvasya vāji vaḥ | 

surabhīno mukhākarat praṇatānaṃsitāyat | 

mantreṇānena taṃ dhyātvā adhivāsaṃ samādiśet | 

oṃ ghṛtavatī bhuvanānāmabhitriyorvī pṛthvīmadhudughe supeśasā | 

dyāvāpṛthivī varuṇasya dharmaṇā viṣkabhīte ajare bhūriretasā | 

mantreṇānena deveśi (!) dadyācchiṣyasya mastake | 

oṃ svasti na iti mantreṇa svastikaṃ vinivedayet |
