oṃ sindhoriva prādhvane sughanāso vātapramīyapatantī jihvā ghṛtasya dhārā aruṣo na 
vājīkāṣṭhābhindannūrmibhiḥ pinnamānaḥ | 

iti mantreṇa sundūraṃ dadyācca śiṣyamastake | 

oṃ śaṅkha (!) tvaṃ śaṅkhapuṇyānāṃ maṅgalānāñca maṅgalam | 

viṣṇutā vidhṛtā nityaṃ mama śāntiprado bhava | 

śaṅkhasparśanamantro'yaṃ sarvatantrasya sammataḥ | 

oṃ samidho'ṅganaṃ kṛdaraṃ matīnāṃ ghṛtamagne madhumat pinnamānaḥ | 

vājīvahan vājinaṃ jātavedā devānāṃ vākṣipriyamādadhanta | 

mantreṇānena deveśi (!) kajjalaṃ vinisajjayet | 

<p. 229> oṃ yuñjanti vradhnamaruṣaṃ carantaṃ paritasthuṣaḥ | 

rocante rocanā divi | 

rocanāṃ parito dadyānmantreṇānena sundari (!) | 

oṃ annaṣate annasya no dhehyannamīrasya śuṣmiṇaḥ | 

pradātāraṃ tārṣa ūrjjaṃ no dhehi dvipadeśaṃ catuṣpade | 

viśvakarmmaṇe svāhā | 

mantreṇānena deveśi (!) sparśanaṃ parikīrttitam | 

oṃ hiraṇyagarbhaḥ samavarttatāgre bhūtasya jātaḥ pratireka āsīt | 

sa dādhāra pṛthivīṃ dyāmutemāṃ kasmaidevāya haviṣā lidhema vā | 

mantreṇānena deveśi (!) kāñcanaṃ parito nyaset | 

oṃ rūpeṇa vai rūpamartyānāṃ puthomā viśvavedā vibhajatu muttamasya yathā 
vedaścandradakṣiṇāpaśyavyantarīkṣaṃ yat suṣaduccaiḥ | 

mantreṇānena deveśi (!) rūpyaṃ dadyādvidhānataḥ |

oṃ asau yastāmro'ruṇa uta babhruḥ sumaṅgalaḥ |

ye cainaṃ rudrā abhito dikṣu śritāḥ | 

sahasraśo vai yāṃ helaṣīmahe | 

mantreṇānena deveśi (!) tāmraṃ dadyādvidhānataḥ | 

oṃ rakṣohano balgahanaḥ prokṣayāmi vaiṣṇavān rakṣohano balgahano balayāmi 
vaiṣṇavān rakṣohano balgahano vastṛṇāmi vaiṣṇavān rakṣohano balgahana upadadhāmi 
vaiṣṇavaṃ rakṣohano balgahanaḥ payyamāmi vaiṣṇavīn vaiṣṇavamasi vaiṣṇavāḥ stha | 

mantreṇānena siddhārthaṃ dattvā bālasya mūrddhani | 

oṃ ākṛṣṇena rajasā varttamānau niveśayanna mṛtaṃ martyañca | 

hiraṇmayena savitā rathenā devo yāti bhuvanāni paśyan | 

mantreṇānena deveśi (!) darpaṇaṃ pratipadayet | 

oṃ manojyotirjuṣatāmājyasya vṛhaspatiryajñamimaṃ tanotu | 

ariṣṭaṃ yajñamimaṃ dadhātu viśve devāḥ sa iha mādayantā mompratiṣṭha | 

mantreṇānena deveśi (!) dīpaṃ dadyādvidhānataḥ || 

oṃ pratipadasi pratipadetvā anupadasi anupade tvā sampadasi sampade tvā tejo'si 
tejasetvā tejomayi dhehi | 

oṃ praśastamātramasi bhūtapātre śirasārthaṃ mama kuru sarvaṃ devīcarayugalaṃ 
vandanīyam || 

śāradātilake caturthapaṭale || 

<p. 230> astreṇa kṣālite pātre nave tāmramayādike (!) | 

taṇḍulān śālisambhūtān mūlamantrābhimantritān | 

prasṛtīnāñca daśabhiḥ kṣiptvā cāntramauṃ japet | 

prakṣālya prātravadanaṃ vidhāya kavacānunā | 

prāṅmukho mūlamantreṇa deśikendraścaruṃ pacet | 

śruveṇājyena saṃsvinne dadyāttaptābhimāraṇam | 

mūlena paścāttatpātraṃ kavacenāvatārayet | 

astrajapte kuśāstīrṇe maṇḍale vidhivadguruḥ | 

taṃ vibhajya tridhā bhāgamekaṃ devāya kalpayet | 

anyadagnau ca juhuyādaparaṃ deśikaḥ svayam | 

śiṣyeṇa sārddhaṃ bhuñjīta vihitācamanaṃ tataḥ | 

ācāntaṃ śiṣyamādāya sakalīkṛtya deśikaḥ | 

tālapramāṇaṃ hṛjjaptaṃ kṣīrivṛkṣādisambhavam | 

dantakāṣṭhaṃ tadā dadyācchiṣyāya niyatātmane | 

dantān viśodhya sa punastat prakṣālya visajjane | 

yathāvidhi tamācāntaṃ śikhābandhābhirakṣitam | 

vidhāya sārddhamamunā vedyāṃ darbhāntare guruḥ | 

śayīta tasyāṃ tāṃ rātrimadhivāsaḥ samīritaḥ || 

viśeṣastu merutantre tṛtīyaprakāśe anusandheyaḥ || 

iti adhivāsavidhiḥ ||
	
athāṅgurāropaṇaprayogaḥ | 

tatra dīkṣādivaśāt prāk navame saptame pañcame śubhe tasmin vahnimaṇḍapaprāntare 
pañcahastamitāni prañcasūtrāṇi pūrvapaścimakrameṇa pātayitvā dakṣiṇottarakrameṇa 
dvādaśāṅgulāntaritānyekādaśasūtrāṇi pātayet tena catvāriṃśatkoṣṭhā bhavanti | 

teṣāṃ vahiścatusro vīthīrmārjjayet | 

mārjjanaprakāraśca prathamaṃ pūrvasyāṃ catuṣkoṣṭhātmikāmekāṃ vīthīṃ tato 
dakṣiṇasyāmaṣṭakoṣṭhātmikāmaparāṃ paścimāyāṃ 
catuṣkoṣṭhātmikāmanyāmuttarasyāmaṣṭakoṣṭhātmikāmita(-)rāñca mārjjayet | 

tato'ntaścatuṣkasya pūrvapaścimapārśvayordve vīthyau dvikoṣṭhātmike punarmārjayet | 

tena maṇḍale catuṣkoṣṭhātmakaṃ koṣṭhatrayamavaśiṣyate | 

tataścatuṣkoṣṭhatrayasthāni dvādaśapadāni pratyekaṃ catuṣkoṣṭhaṃ 
śvetādibhīrajobhīrañjayet | 

<p. 231> tatra vāyupade śvetamāgneye pītaṃ nair(-)ṛte aruṇamīśāne kṛṣṇam | 

tataḥ śyāmalena mārjitavīthīrāpūrayet | 

teṣu padeṣu pālikāpañcamukhīśarāvāṇāṃ suprakṣālitaṃ triguṇasūtrasūtraveṣṭitaṃ 
śuṣkagomayacūrṇabālukāmṛdbhiruttarottaraparipūritam | 

haribrahmarudrasvarūpaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇavraṇādirahitaṃ 
paścimādikrameṇāgnikoṇamārabhyeśānakoṇaparyyantaṃ pratyekaṃ catuṣkoṣṭheṣu 
sthāpayet | 

tataḥ paścimadiśi pālikācatuṣke viṣṇuṃ madhye pālikācatuṣke brahmāṇaṃ 
pūrvasyāṃ śarāvacatuṣke rudraṃ dhyātvāvāhya pūjayet | 

tato vamiti mantreṇa viṣṇuviṣaye oṃ namo bhagavate vāsudevāyeti mantreṇa 
dugdhenāmbhasā ca vījāni prakṣālya vījopari dātavyamantramaṣṭottaraśataṃ japet | 

tataḥ kaṅkau skandaṃ niṣpāve vāyuṃ kulatthe vahnimāḍhakyāṃ nir(-)ṝtiṃ mudge 
somaṃ tile vaivasvataṃ śālau prajāpatiṃ sarṣape'nantaṃ śyāmāke sūryyaṃ māṣe 
ca varuṇaṃ sampūjya vījāni miśrayitvā tatra somaṃ pūjayitvā prāṅmukha udaṅmukho 
vā | 

oṃ tripañcakāya sarvāya śaṅkarāya śivāya ca | 

sarvalokapradhānāya śāśvatāya namo namaḥ | 

iti mantreṇa teṣu pātreṣu vījāni nirvapet | 

tato haridrācūrṇamiśritajalena gandhamiśritajalena ca tāni siñcet | 

uktaprakārajalasecanaṃ somapūjanañcādhivāsadinaparyyantaṃ pratyahaṃ kuryyāt | 

tato nūtanavastratrayeṇa tāni pātrāṇyācchādya pratyekarātriṣu devatābhyo baliṃ 
dadyāt | 

tatra prathamarātrau oṃ bhūtebhyo nama iti mantreṇa gandhapuṣpe dattvā 
lājatiladadhiśaktubhirbaliṃ dadyāt | 

tataḥ sthānamārjjanādi kuryyāt | 

dvitīyarātrau oṃ pitṛbhyo nama iti gandhapuṣpe dattvā satilānnataṇḍulairbaliṃ dadyāt | 

tṛtauyarātrau oṃ yakṣebhyo nama iti gandhapuṣpe dattvā dadhiśaktulājairbaliṃ dadyāt | 

caturtharātrau oṃ nāgebhyo nama iti gandhapuṣpe dattvā nārikelodakaśaktupiṣṭakairbaliṃ 
dadyāt | 

pañcamarātrau oṃ brahmaṇe nama iti gandhapuṣpe dattvā sapaṅkajākṣatataṇḍulena 
baliṃ dadyāt | 

<p. 232> ṣaṣṭharātrau oṃ śivāya nama iti gandhapuṣpe dattvā sapūpānnabaliṃ dadyāt 
| 

saptamarātrau oṃ viṣṇave nama iti gandhapuṣpe dattvā guḍaudanena baliṃ dadyāt | 

tataḥ pāyasādinā lokapālebhyo balidānam | 

tatra īśānapūrvayormadhye brahmaṇe | 

nai(-)ṛtavaruṇayormadhye anantāya balirdeyaḥ | 

prarūḍhānaṅkurānanyo na paśyet | 

aśubheṣu aṅkureṣu śāntyādikaṃ kuryyādityaṅkurāropaṇam ||

atha adhivāsaprayogaḥ || 

tatrādau gururgṛhaṃ praviśya candanāgurudhūpādyairgṛhaṃ suvāsitaṃ kṛtvā vighnān 
vināśya oṃ sūryyaḥ soma ityādi paṭhitvā ghaṭasthāpanaṃ kuryyāt | 

tatkramastu oṃ bhūrasīti bhūmiśodhanam || 1 || 

oṃ dhānyamasīti mantreṇa tatra śukladhānyanikṣepaḥ || 2 || 

oṃ prāṇa iti mantreṇa hastapramāṇasya ghaṭasyodare navaratnadānam || 3 || 

oṃ ājighra ityādimantreṇa tatra tasya sthāpanam || 4 || 

oṃ āpyāyasveti mantreṇa ghaṭopari jaladānam || 5 || 

oṃ dhanna neti tadupari śākhādānam || 6 || 

tadupari tāmbūlasya śrīphalasya ca dānaṃ kadalī phalasya vā || 7 || 

oṃ śrīśca teti tadupari puṣpadānam || 8 || 

tataḥ saphalaṃ śālicūrṇayuktaṃ śarāvasya ghaṭoparinidhānam || 9 || 

oṃ vastrañceti kṣīraśākhiśākhāsahitavastrārpaṇam || 10 || 

jambūpañcāśadoṣadhikvāthena ghaṭapūraṇam || 11 || 

indravallyā ghaṭaveṣṭanam || 12 || 

ghaṭe sindūradānam || 13 || 

oṃ sthiro bhaveti dīkṣāparyyantaṃ gaṭasya sthirīkaraṇam || 14 || 

evaṃ ghaṭaṃ saṃsthāpya oṃ gurubhyo nama ityādi paṭhitvā oṃ yajamānasya 
puṇyāhamityādinā puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā oṃ svasti na iti svastivācanaṃ kṛtvā oṃ 
astrāya phaḍiti bhaumān vighnānanimeṣākṣidṛṣṭirūpadivyadṛṣṭyā caturdiśidivyān 
vighnānapasāryya oṃ bhūtāḥ pretā iti mantreṇa māṃṣabhaktenānnena 
yāvakenājākarṇaraktena dugdhena madhuradravyeṇa vā baliṃ dattvā raktapuṣpeṇa tān 
pūjayet | 

<p. 233> tato humiti saptakṛtvo japtena vikireṇa sarvān vighnān niḥsāryya ramiti 
jaladhārayā caturdikṣu vahniprākāraṃ kalpayet | 

tato vamityūrddhvorddhakrameṇa tālatrayaṃ dattvā oṃ gurubhyo nama evaṃ 
gurupādukābhyaḥ paramagurubhyaḥ parāparagurubhyaśca ūrddhvorddhakrameṇa vāme 
natvā dakṣiṇe oṃ gaṇeśāya namaḥ madhye iṣṭadevyai namaḥ iti praṇamet | 

oṃ āvāhayāmyahamityādinā gaṇeśaṃ dhyātvā ehyehītyādinā ghaṭe āvāhya 
sindūraraktakusumādinā taṃ pūjayet | 

tatkramastu svāgatapraśnottarānantaramāsanadānam | 

oṃ pādyamiti pādayornama iti pādyadānam | 

śaṅkhodakaśyāmākadūrvākuśatilasarṣapāparājitāyavarūpa(-)ṣaḍaṅgārghyaṃ 
gandhayuktaṃ śaṅkhe nidhāya |

oṃ kāṇḍādibhiḥ svāheti śirasyarghyadānam | 

pādyamantreṇācamanīyadānam | 

oṃ nama iti dadhimadhughṛtarūpa madhuparkasya svadheti dānam | 

mūlamantreṇa svadheti punarācamanīyadānam | 

mūlamantreṇa nama iti snānīyadānam | 

oṃ yajñopavītamiti yajñasūtradānam | 

oṃ nānāratnaiścetyādyābharaṇadānam | 

mūlena nama iti gandhadānam | 

mūlena vauṣaḍiti puṣpadānam | 

vanaspatīti dhūpadānam | 

oṃ suprakāśa iti dīpadānam | 

mūlena dīpābalibhirnama iti nīrājanam | 

mūlena nivedayāmīti naivedyadānam | 

tataḥ prāguktanamo gurubhya iti vandanam | 

iti gaṇeśaṃ sampūjya | 

evaṃ krameṇārkaviṣṇuśivadurgā ghaṭepūjayet | 

tata oṃ phaḍiti mantreṇa tilakuśasarṣaparūpakūrcamuṣṭiṃ prakṣipet | 

tata oṃ astrāya phaḍiti saptakṛtvaḥ sañjapya lājacandanaśvetasarṣapabhasmadūrvārūpa(-)
vighnavināśakavikiraprakṣepaṃ kuryyāt | 

tato gururvedikopari svīyāsane upaviśya mūlena mūrtīṃ kalpayitvāvāhya 
āvāhanyādimudrāḥ pradarśyāsanādipuṣpānteneṣṭadevatāṃ sampūjayet | 

<p. 234> tato'ṅgāvaraṇādiṃ sampūjayet | 

tatkramastu tantrasāre'nusandheyaḥ | 

tato'ṣṭasu dikṣu aṣṭa kumbhān kvāthatoya prapūritān pūrvoktamantraiḥ saṃsthāpya 
teṣvaṣṭamātṛkādhyānāvāhanapūrvakaṃ pūjayet | 

tato daśadikṣu indrādilokapālānairāvatādīnaṣṭau gajān vajrādyastrāṇi sampūjya 
dhūpadīpau naivedyañceṣṭadevatāyai nivedya vedikāyā dakṣabhāge maṇḍalaṃ 
kṛtvāṅkurapātrāṇi sthāpayet | 

tato dakṣiṇe sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvāṅkuraṃ saṃskṛtya tatra devatāṃ sampūjya oṃ bhūḥ 
svāhā oṃ bhuvaḥ svāhā oṃ svaḥ svāhā iti vyāhṛtibhirghṛtena hutvā pāyasena 
mūlamantreṇa pañcaviṃśativāraṃ juhuyāt | 

punarvyāhṛtibhirhutvā gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ sampūjyāgniṃ visṛjet | 

tato'vaśiṣṭapāyasena pārśvadevebhyo baliṃ dadyāt | 

tato naivedyamutthāpya sthānamārjjanaṃ kṛtvā punardaśopacārairiṣṭadevatāṃ ghaṭe 
sampūjya darpanaṃ cāmaraṃ vyajanaṃ chatraṃ mukhavāsakatāmbūlañca darśayet tato 
mūlamantraṃ sahasradhā japtvā japaṃ samāpya praṇamya brāhmaṇān paritoṣya 
maṅgaladhvaninṛtyagītapuraḥsaramadhivāsaṃ kuryyāt || 

yathā oṃ bhūrasīti śiṣyamastake mṛttikāsparśanam || 1 || 

oṃ gandhadvārāmiti gandha || 2 || 

oṃ praparvatasyeti śilā || 3 || 

oṃ dhānyamasīti dhānyam || 4 || 

oṃ kāṇḍāditi dūrvā || 5 || 

oṃ śrīścateti puṣpam || 6 || 

oṃ yāḥ kalinīti phalam || 7 || 

oṃ dadhīti dadhi || 8 || 

oṃ dhṛtavatīti ghṛtam || 9 || 

oṃ svasti na iti svastikam || 10 || 

oṃ sindhoriveti sindūram || 11 || 

oṃ śaṅkha tvamiti śaṅkhaḥ || 12 || 

oṃ samidha iti kajjalam || 13 || 

oṃ yuñjantīti rocanā || 14 || 

oṃ anneti siddhānnam || 15 || 

oṃ hiraṇyeti kāñcanam || 16 || 

oṃ rūpeṇeti rūpyam || 17 || 

oṃ asau ya iti tāmram || 18 || 

oṃ rakṣohana iti siddhārthaḥ || 19 || 

oṃ ākṛṣṇeneti darpaṇam || 20 || 

oṃ manojyotiriti dīpaḥ || 21 || 

<p. 235> oṃ pratipadeti praśastapātram || 22 || 

sarvāṇyetāni śiṣyamastake dadyāt || 

tataḥ phaḍiti mantreṇa prakṣālite nave pātre 
mūlamantrābhimantritadaśaprasṛtimitaśālitaṇḍulān kṛtvā humiti mantreṇa pātravadanaṃ 
prakṣālya caruṃ pacet | 

susvinne carau mūlenābhighāraṇaṃ kuryyāt | 

tato hūmiti tatpātramavatāryya kuśāstīrṇe maṇḍale phaḍiti mantreṇa sthāpayet | 

taṃ caruṃ tridhā vibhajya ekaṃ devāya dadyāt aparaṃ vahnau juhuyāt | 

aparaṃ bhāgaṃ śiṣyeṇa sārddhaṃ gururbhuñjīta | 

tata ācāntāya śiṣyāyāṅganyāsaṃ kṛtvā sthitāya dvādaśāṅgulaṃ dantakāṣṭhaṃ 
gururdadyāt | 

śiṣyastu tena dantakāṣṭhena dantān saṃśodhya tat kāṣṭhaṃ prakṣālya visṛjet | 

tata ācāntaśiṣyena sārddhaṃ vedyāṃ darbhasaṃstare tāṃ rātriṃ guruḥ 
śayītetyadhivāsavidhiḥ ||

[iti śrīprāṇatoṣiṇyāṃ dvitīye dharmakāṇḍe adhivāsarūpapuṣpakathanaṃ nāma caturthaḥ 
paricchedaḥ] |



atha dīkṣā || 

viśvasāratantre || 

gurornimantraṇaṃ kṛtvā paredyuḥ sādhakastadā | 

vastrālaṅkāravarṇādyairgobhiścaiva vidhānataḥ |

tataḥ snātvā vidhānena nadyādau sādhakottamaḥ || 

vidhānena saṅkalpādinā tāntrikakarmaṇi saṅkalpādau sauramāsollekha uktaḥ | 

picchilātantre pañcamapaṭale || 

saure māsi śubhā dīkṣā na cāndre na ca tārake | 

prayogādīni sarvāṇi saureṇaiva samācaret || 

viśvasāre dvitīyapaṭale | 

aṅkurāropaṇānantaram || 

mātṛkāḥ pūjayettatra gauryyādyāḥ sarvasammatāḥ | 

gaṇeśādyāḥ pūjayettāḥ kṣamasveti kṣamāṃ vadet || 

mātṛkāḥ pūrvamuktāḥ || 

vasordhārā tadā deyā sarvavighnavināśinī | 

pūjayet parayā bhaktyā rājoparicaraṃ budhaḥ | 

tathā nāndīmukhaśrāddhaṃ kuryyācca vidhināmunā | 

pitaraṃ pitāmahañcaiva tathaiva prapitāmaham | 

jīve pitari tat śrāddhaṃ kuryyāttat pitṛpūrvakam | 

<p. 236> mātāmahaṃ pramātāmahaṃ vṛddhapramātāmahaṃ tataḥ | 

ṣaṭpiṇḍān prakṣipeddevi (!) tānupāsya susādhakaḥ | 

nāmagotrānurūpeṇaṃ śrāddhaṃ kuryyādvidhānataḥ | 

devatāpūrvakaṃ śrāddhaṃ kuryyānnāndīmukhaṃ tataḥ | 

puroravā mādravāśca sarvakāryeṣu devatā | 

nāndaumukhavasuḥ satyā devatā parikīrttitā | 

sarvaṃ daivakrameṇaiva śrāddhe dadyājjalādi ca | 

yattu mahāṃ nirvāṇatantre daśamollāse || 

nāndīmukhāśca pitaro nāndīmukhyaśca mātaraḥ | 

pitāmahādayo'pyevaṃ mātāmahādayo'pi ca | 

śrāddhe nāndyābhyudayike samullekhyā varānane (!) || 

tathā | 

tatrādau devapakṣe tu vākyaṃ śṛṇu śucismite (!) | 

kālādīni nimittāni samullikhya tataḥ param | 

tattatkarmābhyudayārthamuktvā sādhakasattamaḥ | 

pitrādīnāṃ trayāṇāntu mātrādīnāṃ tathaiva ca | 

mātrāmahānāñca mātāmahyādīnāmapi priye (!) | 

ṣaṣṭhyantaṃ kīrttayennāma gotroccāraṇapūrvakam | 

viśveṣāñcaiva devānāṃ śrāddhaṃ padamudīrayet | 

kuśanirmitayoḥ paścādviprayorahamityapi | 

kariṣye parameśānītyanujñāvākyamīritam | 

viśvān devān parityajya pitṛpakṣe tu pārvati (!) | 

tathā mātāmahasyāpi pakṣe'nujñā prakīrttitā || 

iti mātrādīnāṃ mātāmahyādīnāñca śrāddhamuktaṃ taddvādaśadaivikaśrāddhaparam 
| 

viśvasāroktavacanantu ābhyudayikaśrāddhaparam | 

tadapi sāmagānāmeva | 

ṛgvedayajurvedayostu navadaivataśrāddhamiti | 

evañca dvādaśadaivatābhyudayikaśrāddhayoruktapramāṇa(-)
darśanādicchāvikalpayati || 

viśvasāre tathā || 

puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā tu sarvakāryyaṃ samuddiśet || 

iti vṛddhiśrāddham ||

pañcadevapūjāpi tatraiva || 

sarvatra sarvakāryyeṣu gaṇeśaghaṭapūrvakam | 

gaṇeśaṃ savitārañca śivāṃ śivamataḥ param | 

viṣṇuñca pūjayed yatnāt sarvakāryyārthasiddhaye | 

lakṣmīṃ sarasvatīṃ gaṅgāṃ yamunāñca tathaiva ca | 

dikpālān diggajān devānṛṣīn vighnavāsinīṃ grahāṃstataḥ param | 

<p. 237> māsarāśitithīryogān karaṇāni ca pārvati (!) | 

pūjayet parayā bhaktyā sarvatantrasamanvitān || 

dikpālādipūjā pūrvamuktā tadviśeṣastvagrato'nusandheyaḥ | 

samprati grahapūjārthamādau grahamantrā ucyante || 

grahajāmale caturthapaṭale || 

praṇavo vahnimārūḍhaḥ śiva āsyendusaṃyutaḥ | 

māyāyāḥ saptamaḥ sargaścaturvarṇo ravermanuḥ | 

mantrastu oṃ hrāṃ hrīṃ saḥ || 1 || 

praṇavaḥ kacaturthastu sa manuḥ svarabindukaḥ | 

hādivahnisamārūḍhaścaturdaśasvarānvitaḥ | 

bindū raṣaṣṭhaḥ sargaśca indorvedārṇako manuḥ | 

mantrastu oṃ ghauṃ srauṃ saḥ || 2 || 

tāraḥ śikhisamārūḍhaḥ śivo mukhasamanvitaḥ | 

vindvāḍhyaḥ puṭitānena māṣāhādiḥ sasargakaḥ | 

pañcavarṇātmako devi (!) bhaumasya kathito mauḥ | 

mantrastu oṃ hrāṃ hrīṃ saḥ || 3 || 

tāro nādasamārūḍhaḥ saturyyaḥ samanusvaraḥ | 

binduretaddvayaṃ bhānordvicaturthārṇakastathā | 

pañcavarṇātmako devi (!) budhamantraḥ samīritaḥ | 

mantrastu oṃ hrauṃ hrauṃ hrāṃ saḥ || 4 || 

tāraḥ kadaśamārṇaśca samanusvarabindukaḥ | 

etatrayaṃ sūryyaturyyo guroḥ pañcārṇako manuḥ || 

mantrastu oṃ ñauṃ ñauṃ ñauṃ saḥ || 5 || 

candrajādidvitīyaśca dinanāthatrituryyakaḥ | 

caturvarṇātmako mantraḥ śukrasya kathitaḥ priye (!) | 

mantrastu oṃ hrauṃ hrīṃ saḥ || 6 || 

praṇavo racaturthastu samanusvarabindukaḥ | 

etaddvayaṃ sūryyaturyyaścaturvarṇaḥ śanermanuḥ | 

mantrastu oṃ śauṃ śauṃ saḥ || 7 || 

manusvarāḍhyo ghaturyyaḥ sabindustena sampuṭaḥ | 

khaṣaṣṭho mukhavindvāḍhyo bhānuturyyāstakaḥ priye (!) | 

caturvarṇātmako mantraḥ śrīrāhoḥ samudīritaḥ | 

mantrastu oṃ chīṃ chāṃ chauṃ saḥ || 8 || 

tāro dapañcamo varṇo manusvaranabho'nvitaḥ | 

sāmyabinduḥ khaṣaṣṭhārṇaḥ soṣṭhāvo bindunāṣṭamaḥ | 

sūryyaturyyo maheśāni (!) ketoḥ pañcārṇako manuḥ | 

manuḥ | 

mantrastu oṃ phauṃ phāṃ phauṃ saḥ || 9 || 

pūjākramastu ādau mantraṃ samuccāryya paścāt dravyamudīrayet | 

sampradānapadaṃ paścāttyāgātmakapadaṃ tataḥ | 

iti rudrajāmalīyavacanānusāreṇa mūlamuccāryya etatpādyaṃ oṃ sūryyāya grahāya 
nama ityādinā pūjayet | 

<p. 238> sarveṣāmeṣa kramo jñeyaḥ | 

prasaṅgātteṣāṃ puraścaraṇādikaṃ likhyate || 

yathā brahmajāmale śivavākyam || 

viguṇe tu grahe devi (!) vyādhiśatrunṛpārdane | 

rāṣṭropaplvane ghore āyuṣo nāśa āgate | 

jātidhvaṃse kulocchede sarvanāśa upasthite | 

dhanaputrādikāme ca kuryyādgrahapuraṣkriyām | 

daśoktavarṣasaṃkhyena sahasreṇa japena tu | 

puraścaraṇamuddiṣṭaṃ sarveṣāṃ vyomacāriṇām | 

daśāṃśaṃ juhuyādvahnau trimadhvāktasamidvaraiḥ |

kalpoktairgandhakarpūramiśritenodakena ca | 

tarpayeddevatāṃ devi (!) taddaśāṃśakrameṇa tu |

abhiṣiñcettaddaśāṃśaṃ karpūravāsitairjalaiḥ |

taddaśāṃśakrameṇaiva kuryyādbrāhmaṇabhojanam | 

ādyante mahatī pūjā dakṣiṇā vibhavāvidhi || 

iti grahapuraścaraṇam | 

varadātantre tṛtīyapaṭale || 

atastat sādhakenāpi karttavyaṃ grahapūjanam | 

anyathā grahadoṣeṇa na kadācit phalaṃ bhavet | 

sarvāṇi sādhanānīha kṛtvā bhaktyā vidhānataḥ aihike phalaphalabhāṅ na syādvinā hi 
grahapūjanam | 

kathayāmi samāsena grahasādhanamuttamam | 

candratārānukūlaṃ hi dinaṃ grāhyaṃ vicakṣaṇaiḥ | 

ye grahāścābhavan duṣṭāstān prayatnena pūjayet | 

vastrapuṣpāṇi sarvāṇi tattadvarṇāni dāpayet | 

juhuyāttu yathāśakti graheṣviṣṭaprasiddhaye | 

tattadgrahoktadravyāṇi tebhyo dattvā dvije diśet || 

grahadānantu grahajāmale ṣaṣṭhapaṭale || 

śrīdevyuvāca || 

dīnabandho (!) dayāsindho (!) yadi tuṣṭo'si māṃ prati | 

āpadvināśanaṃ karmma kṛpayā kathaya prabho (!) || 

īśvarovāca || 

tailavarttiprapannasya dīpasya pavanāt priye (!) | 

vināśaḥ syādyathā tadvadakāle prāṇasaṃkṣayaḥ || 

ata āpadvināśāya dīrghāyuṣkatvahetave | 

sāramekaṃ pravakṣyāmi yatnāddānaṃ samācaret | 

tulādānañca yo dadyādgrahaviprāya sundari (!) | 

āpanmukto bhavet so'pi bhuvi sammodate sukham || 1 || 

<p. 239> suvarṇasya tulāṃ dadyād grahaviprāya yaḥ priye (!) | 

tasya tuṣṭaḥ suragururāyurvṛddhiṃ karoti saḥ | 

yastu raupyatulādānaṃ dadyād grahadvijāya ca | 

santuṣṭastasya śukraḥ san saṃsṛjeddīrghajīvitam || 2 || 

grahadvijāya yo davyāttulāṃ tāmrasya pārvati (!) | 

santuṣṭo maṅgalastasya dīrghāyuṣṭvaṃ karoti hi || 3 || 

yo yadyād grahaviprāya pittalasya tulāṃ priye (!) | 

tasya tuṣṭo budhaḥ kuryyādāyuḥpuṣṭiṃ sugauravam || 4 || 

grahaviprāya lauhasya tulāṃ yastu prayacchati | 

prītaḥ śanaiścarastasya vipulāyuḥ prayacchati || 5 || 

sīsakasya tulāṃ dadyād grahaviprāya yaḥ priye (!) | 

tasyāśu rāhuḥ suprītaḥ saubhāgyāyuḥ prayacchati || 6 || 

yaḥ kharparatulāṃ dadyād grahaviprāya sundari (!) | 

santuṣṭo bhagavān ketustasyāyurvarddhayet sukham || 7 || 

tulāṃ tāmrasya yo dadyād grahaviprāya sundari (!) | 

ādityastasya suprīto dīghāyuḥ pariyacchati || 8 || 

grahaviprāya yo dadyāttulāṃ kāṃsyasya sundari (!) | 

santuṣṭastasya rātrīśo vibhūtyāyuḥ prayacchati || 9 || 

vaidūryyaṃ nīlamāṇikye padmarāgañca mauktikam | 

vajraṃ pareśaṃ gomedaṃ tathā marakataṃ priye (!) | 

dadyādutsṛjya viprāya svayañca bibhṛyātkare || 

iti grahadānam ||

atha grahakavacam || 

aṣṭādaśapaṭale || 

pārvatyuvāca | 

śrīśān (!) sarvaśāstrajña (!) devatādhīśvara (!) prabho (!) | 

akṣayaṃ kavacaṃ divyaṃ grahādidaivataṃ vibho (!) | 

purā saṃsūcitaṃ guhyaṃ subhaktākṣayakārakam || 

kṛpā mayi tavāste cet kathaya śrīmaheśvara (!) || 

śrīśiva uvācaḥ || 

śṛṇu devi (!) priyatame (!) kavacaṃ devadurlabham |

yaddhṛtvā devatāḥ sarve amarāḥ syurvarānane (!) | 

tava prītivaśādvakṣmi na deyaṃ yasya kasyacit | 

oṃ hrāṃ hrīṃ so me śiraḥ pātu śrīsūryyagrahabhūpatiḥ | 

oṃ ghauṃ sauṃ oṃ me mukhaṃ pātu śrīcandro graharājakaḥ | 

oṃ hrāṃ hrīṃ hrāṃ saḥ karau pātu grahasenāpatiḥ kujaḥ | 

pāyādarthau hrauṃ hrāṃ saḥ pādau jño nṛpabālakaḥ | 

<p. 240> oṃ ñauṃ ñauṃ ñauṃ saḥ kaṭiṃ pātu pāyādamarapūjitaḥ | 

oṃ hrauṃ hrīṃ so daityapūjyo hṛdayaṃ parirakṣatu | 

oṃ śauṃ śauṃ saḥ pātu nābhiṃ me grahapreṣyaṃ śanaiścaraḥ | 

oṃ chauṃ chāṃ chauṃ saḥ kāṇṭhadeśaṃ śrīrāhurdevamardakaḥ | 

oṃ phauṃ phāṃ phauṃ saḥ śikhī pātu sarvāṅgamabhito'vatu | 

grahāścaite bhogadehā nityāstu sphuṭitagrahāḥ | 

etadaṃśāṃśasambhūtāḥ pāntu nityantu durjanāt | 

akṣayaṃ kavacaṃ puṇyaṃ sūryyādigrahadaivatam | 

paṭhedvā pāṭhayedvāpi dhārayedyo janaḥ śuciḥ | 

sa siddhiṃ prāpnuyādiṣṭāṃ durlabhāṃ tridaśaistu thām | 

tava snehavaśāduktaṃ jaganmaṅgalakārakam | 

ṛṣiḥ prokto bhairavo'sya chando'nuṣṭupprakrīrttitam | 

śrīsūryādigrahāstatra devatāḥ parikīrttitāḥ | 

sarvakāmārthasaṃsiddhyai viniyogo mayoditaḥ | 

grahayantrānvitaṃ kṛtvābhīṣṭamakṣayamāpnuyāt ||

iti grajāmale pārvatīśvarasaṃvāde jagaddurlabhākṣaya nāma kavacaṃ samāptam ||


atha tithipūjā ||

gāyatrītantre | 

śrīmahādeva uvāca || 

titherdhyānaṃ vinā devi (!) tithermantraṃ tathaiva ca | 

ajñātvā parameśāni (!) dinakṛtyaṃ karoti yaḥ | 

tasya sarvaṃ bhavedyarthaṃ dinakṛtyaṃ varānane (!) | 

yā tithiḥ sā mahāmāyā ādyāmūrttijaganmayī | 

kṛṣṇapakṣe kṛṣṇavarṇā śukle candra samaprabhā | 

dvibhujāṃ śuklarūpāñca khelatkhañjanagāminīm | 

dvilocanāṃ śaśikalāṃ sindūratilakojjvalām | 

taptahāṭakanirmāṇanānālaṅkārabhūṣitām | 

dāḍimīvījasadṛśadaśanadyutiśobhanām | 

dhyāyet pratipadaṃ devīṃ japapūjāviśuddhaye || 

mantro yathā | 

grīṃ pratipadbhyaḥ svāhā || 1 || 

evaṃ dvilocanāṃ śaśikalāṃ śuddhaspahṭikaśobhanām | 

śuddhābharaṇaśobhāḍhyāṃ śuklavastraparicchadām | 

nānākaṭākṣasaṃyuktabhrūlatāpariśobhitām || 

sindūratilakoddīptāṃ khañjanāñcitalocanām | 

dvibhujāṃ sundarāṅgīñca kiśorīṃ navayauvanām | 

<p. 241> dhyātvā dvitīyāṃ cārvaṅgi (!) japenmantraṃ yathā śṛṇu | 

aiṃ dvitīyāyai svāhā || 2 || 

śuklapadmapratikāśāṃ śuklavastraparicchadām | 

śuklābharaṇaśobhāḍhyāṃ puṇḍarīkoparisthitām | 

dvilocanāṃ śaśikalāṃ sindūratilakojjvalām | 

kaṭākṣaviśikhopetāṃ dvibhujāṃ tṛtīyāṃ bhajet | 

oṃ aiṃ tṛtīyāyai svāhā || 3 || 

caturthīṃ śuklacārvaṅgīṃ yajjñātvā siddhitāṃ vrajet | 

kundapuṣpasamābhāsāṃ dvibhujāṃ lolalocanām | 

śuklavastraparīdhānāṃ śuklābharaṇabhūṣitām | 

sindūratilakoddīptāṃ khañjanāñcitalocanām || 

aiṃ huṃ caturthyai svāhā || 4 || 

pañcamīṃ śṛṇu cārvaṅgi (!) sarvasiddhipradāyinīm | 

yajjñātvā parameśāni (!) titheḥ phalamavāpnuyāt || 

śuddhasphaṭikasaṅkāśāṃ śvetapadmoparisthitām | 

hāsyayuktāṃ prasannāsyāṃ kaṭākṣaviśikhojjvalām | 

kandarpadhanurākārabhrūlatāpariśobhitām | 

dvibhujāṃ śvetavarṇāñca śvetālaṅkārabhūṣitām || 

locanadvayasaṃyuktāṃ sindūratilakojjvalām | 

mṛṇālasadṛśākārabāhuvallīvirājitām | 

khelatkhañjanagāmīñca cārucūḍāvirājitām || 

hrīṃ aiṃ hrīṃ pañcamyai svāhā | 

hrīṃ aiṃ hrīṃ viśeṣaḥ | 

śrīpañcamīdine karttavyaṃ pūjanaṃ mahat | 

nirīkṣya dakṣiṇanāthe yaddhyānapūjādikañcaret | 

dhyāyet paramayatnena pañcamītithirūpiṇīm | 

dhyātvāṃ pādyādikaṃ dattvā prajapeddaśadhā manum | 

tatastu pūjayeddevīṃ śāradā vāgvilāsinīm || 7 || 

kundapuṣpasamābhāsāṃ dvibhujāṃ lolalocanām | 

kaṭākṣaviśikhoddīptāṃ sindūratilakojjvalām | 

dāḍimīvījasadṛśadaśanadyutimujjvalām | 

śuklālaṅkārasubhagāṃ śuklāsananivāsinīm |

 śuklavastraparicchinnāṃ śuklahāravinodinīm | 

dvibhujāṃ candravadanāṃ dhyāyedātmavibhūtaye | 

oṃ hrīṃ ṣaṣṭhyai svāhā || 6 || 

śaraccandrapratīkāśāṃ dvibhujāṃ śaśiśekharām | 

locanadvayasaṃyuktāṃ khelatkhañjanagāminīm | 

sindūratilakoddīptāmañjanāñcitalocanām | 

<p. 242> kaṭākṣaviśikhopetāṃ bhrūlatāpariśobhitām | 

śuklāsanasamāsīnāṃ śuklābharaṇabhūṣitām |

krīṃ huṃ aiṃ aṣṭamyai svāhā aiṃ huṃ krīṃ || 8 ||

aiṃ oṃ daśamyai svāhā oṃ aiṃ || 10 ||

<p. 243> raktacandrapratīkāśāṃ kiśorīṃ navayauvanām | 

raktavastraparīdhānāṃ kuṭilālakamaṇḍitām |

saptamapaṭale ||

sarvāsāṃ parameśāni (!) ekena bhavati priye (!) |
