amāvāsyāmanuṃ devi (!) śṛṇuṣva varavarṇini (!) || 

oṃ hrīṃ hrīṃ oṃ amāvāsyāyai svāhā oṃ hrīṃ hrīṃ oṃ || 

atha vakṣyāmi saphalaṃ pakṣadhyānaṃ varānane (!) || 

śaṅkhakundasamābhāsāṃ navayauvanasaṃyutām | 

caturbhujāṃ trinayanāṃ mattadviradagāminīm | 

lalāṭapaṭṭikāmadhye sindūratilakasthitim | 

bhramadbhramaranīlābhāmañjanāñcitalocanām | 

pītāṃśukaparīdhānāṃ kṛṣṇavastrottarīyiṇīm | 

nānālaṅkārasubhagāṃ nīlapadmoparisthitām | 

ghūrṇāyamānanayanāṃ nīlapadmavidhāriṇīm | 

sadā ṣoḍaśavarṣīyāṃ kadambakorakastanīm | 

hīrakadyutisaṅkāśadaśanajyotirujjvalām | 

nānāpuṣpamayairhārairnānāgandhamayīṃ parām | 

pratyahaṃ bhāvayeddevīṃ śuklapakṣasvarūpiṇīm || 

aiṃ aiṃ krīṃ krīṃ śuklapakṣāya svāhā aiṃ aiṃ krīṃ krīṃ || 

kṛṣṇapakṣe maheśāni (!) adhunā nigadāmi te | 

mahāmarakataśyāmāṃ caturbāhusamanvitām | 

pīnottuṅgakucāṃ ramyāṃ citravastravilāsinīm | 

nānāśṛṅgāraveśāḍhyāṃ sphuraccakitalocanām | 

sindūratilakoddīptāmañjanāñcitalocanām | 

kandarpadhanurākārabhrūlatāpariśobhitām | 

ratnahāreṇa sahitanāgahāravirājitām | 

pītapadmasamāsīnāṃ citracūḍāvirājitām | 

pītāvidyutsamākāntyuttarīyavasanacchavim | 

pūrṇacandramukhīṃ devīṃ kṛṣṇapakṣasvarūpiṇīm || 

oṃ oṃ kṛṣṇapakṣāya svāhā oṃ oṃ || 

śrīśiva uvāca || 

titherdhyānaṃ tithermantra pakṣadhyānaṃ tathaiva ca | 

pakṣamantra tathā devi (!) divase divase sudhīḥ | 

pratyahaṃ kurute yastu tasya siddhiradūrataḥ | 

etāstu tithayo devi (!) sūryyamaṇḍalasaṃsthitāḥ | 

pakṣadhyānaṃ prathamatastithidhyānaṃ tataḥ priye (!) | 

pakṣamantraṃ tato japtvā tithimantra tato japet | 

<p. 245> tataḥ siddhimavāpnoti japapūjādikā ca yā | 

dinakṛtyaṃ maheśāni (!) yaḥ karoti dine dine | 

pakṣapūjāṃ prathamataḥ kṛtvā siddhimavāpnuyāt | 

tithipūjāṃ vinā devi (!) japayajñādikā kriyā | 

sarvaṃ niṣphala | 

yāti āmapātramivāmbhasi | 

divase divase devi (!) tithimāsādya yatnataḥ | 

prātaḥkāle tato dhīmān tithidhyānaṃ samācarat | 

daśadhā prajapenmantraṃ sūryyaṃ dṛṣṭvā prayatnataḥ | 

tadā tithiphalaṃ prāpya sarvaṃ pūjāphala labhat |

etattattvamavijñāya āyuryāti dine dine | 

tithirūpā mahādevī mahāmāyā jaganmayī | 

dhyānamantraṃ vinā devi (!) sarveṣāṃ siddhināśinī | 

pratyahaṃ kurute yastu japadhyānaṃ titheḥ priye (!) | 

gaṅgāsnānaphalaṃ so'pi labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ | 

aśvamedhamahāyajñe vyāso hotā yadi priye (!) | 

viphalamaśvamedhaṃ hi tithidhyānaṃ vinā bhavet | 

amṛtenāmṛtaṃ kṛṣṇe prīyante devatāḥ kramāt | 

prathamāṃ pibate vahnirdvitīyāṃ tapanaḥ kalām | 

viśvadevāstṛtīyāntu caturthīntu prajāpatiḥ | 

pañcamīṃ varuṇaścāpi ṣaṣṭhīṃ pibati vāsavaḥ | 

saptamīmṛṣayo divyā vasavo'ṣṭau tathāṣṭamīm | 

navamīṃ kṛṣṇapakṣasya pibatīndraḥ kalāmapi | 

daśamīṃ marutaścāpi rudra ekādaśīṃ kalām | 

dvādaśīntu kalāṃ viṣṇurdhanadaśca trayodaśīm | 

caturdaśīṃ paśupatiḥ kalāṃ pibati nityaśaḥ | 

tataḥ pañcadaśīñcāpi pibanti pitaraḥ kalām ||

atha tithikavacam | 

asya tithīnāṃ kavacasya aśvatthāmabalivyāsahanūmadvibhīṣaṇakṛpaparaśurāmā ṛṣayo 
gāyatrīcchandaḥ śrīmṛtyuñjayaśivo devatā dharmārthakāmamokṣesu viniyogaḥ | 

pūrve pātu sadā nityā krīṅkārī pratipattithiḥ | 

klīṅkārī pātu me nityaṃ dvitīyā brahmapūjitā | 

aiṅkārī pātu me nityaṃ tṛtīyā paścime parā vluṅkārī pātu me nityā caturthī 
viṣṇupūjitā | 

<p. 246> vlūṅkārī pātu me nityamūrddhadehamaharniśam | 

hrīṅkārī pātu me nityaṃ pañcamī mukhamaṇḍalam | 

śrīṅkārī pātu me nityaṃ ṣaṣṭhīrūpā ca pārvatī | 

strīṅkārī saptamī pātu nityaṃ me nābhimaṇḍalam | 

huṃkārī cāṣṭamī nityaṃ kaṇṭhaṃ me pātu sarvadā | 

śrīṅkārī navamī nityaṃ svādhiṣṭhānaṃ sadāvatu | 

daśamī pātu me nityaṃ hastayugmaṃ yathā tathā | 

ekādaśī sadā pātu cākṣiyugmamaharniśam | 

dvādaśī pātu me nityaṃ hastayugmaṃ yathā tathā | 

trayodaśī mahālakṣmīḥ kṣetrasthānanivāsinī | 

trayodaśī pātu nityaṃ haṃsa ityakṣarātmikā | 

haṅkāraḥ pātu me nityaṃ pādayugmamaharniśam | 

saḥkāraḥ pātu me nityaṃ pādāgrāt keśamaṇḍalam | 

caturdaśī mahāmāyā sarvatra parirakṣatu | 

svākāraścaturdaśī nityā nityaṃ me dhanasampadam | 

hākāraḥ pūrṇimā nityaṃ devi me āyuvarddhanam | 

śrīṃ huṃ hrīṃ svāhā sarvarūpā devī tithirūpā ca māṃ parirakṣatu sarvadā | 

huṃ hrīṃ amāvāsyā sadā pātu dārān puttraparicchadam |

hrīṃ huṃ hrīṃ svāhā kathitaṃ kavacaṃ devi (!) trailokyeṣu ca durlabham | 

idaṃ kavacamajñātvā kavacānyaṃ paṭhettu yaḥ | 

anyeṣāṃ kavacānāñca tejāṃsi harate tithiḥ | 

tasmādyatnena he devi (!) tithīnāṃ kavacaṃ paṭhet | 

dhyānaṃ mantraṃ tathā stotraṃ kavacañca tathā priye (!) | 

kalikālamataṃ devi (!) paratattvaṃ vinirgatam | 

iti te kathitaṃ devi (!) sārātsāraṃ parātparam ||

iti śrīvarṇavilāsatantre gaṇeśajatukarṇasubhagāsaṃvāde tithikavacaṃ samāptam ||

māsādīnāṃ dhyānantu kāmadhenutantrādāvanusandheyamatra granthagauravabhiyā na 
likhitam || 

atha varṇānāṃ dhyānādikaṃ varṇoddhāratantroktam | 

praṇāmamantrastu sarvatra kāmadhenutantrokta iti | 

tadyathā || 

ketakīpuṣpagarbhābhāṃ dvibhujāṃ haṃsalocanām | 

śuklapaṭṭāmbaradharāṃ padmamālyavibhūṣitām | 

<p. 247> caturvargapradāṃ nityaṃ nityānandamayīṃ parām | 

varābhayakarāṃ devīṃ nāgapāśasamanvitām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā akārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || a || 

dhyānamasyāḥ pravakṣyāmi ṣaḍbhujāṃ raktalocanām | 

ratnakaṅkaṇakeyūrahārojjvalakalevarām | 

siddhāṃ siddhipradāṃ saumyāṃ siddhagandharvasevitām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā suraśreṣṭhāṃ tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || ā || 

dhūmravarṇāṃ mahāraudrīṃ pītāmbarayutāṃ parām | 

kāmadāṃ siddhidāṃ saumyāṃ nityotsāhavivarddhinīm | 

caturbhujāntu varadāṃ haricandanabhūṣitām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā brahmarūpāṃ tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || i || 

caturbhujāṃ raktavarṇāṃ raktapuṣpopaśobhitām | 

cārucandanadigdhāṅgīṃ raktapaṅkajalocanām | 

raktacīraparīdhānāṃ dharmakāmārthamokṣadām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā suraśreṣṭhāṃ tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || ī || 

pītavarṇāṃ trinayanāṃ pītāmbaradharāṃ parām | 

dvibhujāṃ jaṭilāṃ bhīmāṃ sarvasiddhipradāyinīm | 

evaṃ dhyātvā suraśreṣṭhāṃ tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || u || 

dvibhujāṃ śuklavarṇāñca jaṭāmukuṭaśobhitām | 

śuklamālyāmbaradharāṃ cārucandanabhūṣitām | 

caturvargapradāṃ nityāṃ raktapaṅkajalocanām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā tu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japamācaret || ū || 

ṣaḍbhujāṃ nīlavarṇāñca nīlāmbaradharāṃ parām | 

nānālaṅkārabhūṣāḍhyāṃ sarvālaṅkṛtamastakām | 

bhaktipradāṃ bhagavatīṃ bhogamokṣapradāyinīm | 

evaṃ dhyātvā suraśreṣṭhāṃ tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet | 

pañcaprāṇamayaṃ varṇaṃ caturjñānamayaṃ tathā | 

raktavidyullatākāraṃ ṛkāraṃ praṇamāmyaham || ṛ || 

dhyānamasya pravakṣyāmi dvibhujāṃ padmalocanām | 

santaptasvarṇavarṇābhāṃ sarvālaṅkārabhūṣitām | 

raktapadmekṣaṇāṃ devīṃ ratnahāravibhūṣitām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā suraśreṣṭhāṃ tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet | 

caturjñānamayaṃ varṇaṃ pañcaprāṇamayaṃ sadā | 

triśaktisahitaṃ varṇaṃ praṇamāmi sadā priye (!) || ṝ || 

svarṇacampakavarṇāñca svarṇālaṅkāra vigrahām | 

<p. 248> caturbhujāṃ trinayanāṃ raktacandanacarccitām | 

praṇamāmi sadā devīṃ dharmakāmārthamokṣadām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā brahmarūpāṃ tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || ḹ ||

dhyānamasyāḥ pravakṣyāmi pītavarṇāṃ caturbhujām |

pītāmbaraparīdhānāṃ nānālaṅkāramastakām |

vicitramālyābharaṇāṃ devadānavasevitām |

caturvargapradāṃ nityāṃ nityotsāhavivarddhinīm |

evaṃ dhyātvā brahmarūpāṃ tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || ḹ ||

raktāmbaraparīdhānāṃ ṣaḍbhujāṃ raktalocanām | 

vicitrābharaṇāṃ nityāṃ caturvargapradāyinīm | 

īṣaddhāsyamukhīṃ saumyāṃ devagandharvasevitām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā brahmarūpāṃ tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || e || 

vicitrarūpiṇīṃ devīṃ vicitrāmbaradhāriṇīm | 

vicitramālyābharaṇāṃ caturbāhusamanvitām || 

nānālaṅkārasaṃyuktāṃ catuvargaphalapradām | 

devadānavagandharvaiḥ sevitāṃ mokṣakāṅkṣibhiḥ | 

evaṃ dhyātvā brahmarūpāṃ tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || ai || 

ratnālaṅkārasaṃyuktāṃ padmarāgaprabhāṃ śubhām | 

śaratpūrṇenduvadanāṃ vicitravasanānvitām | 

caturbhujāṃ trinayanāṃ smerāsyāṃ nīlakuntalām | 

vidyuddāmasamānāṅgīṃ muktāpaṃktisrajaṃ bhaje | 

evaṃ dhyātvā brahmarūpāṃ tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || o || 

caturbhujāṃ trinayanāṃ jaṭāmukuṭamaṇḍitām | 

śvetarohitapītādipuṣpahāropaśobhitām | 

sadā smeramukhīṃ saumyāṃ caturvargapradāyinīm | 

evaṃ dhyātvā brahmarūpāṃ tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || au || 

javādāḍimapuṣpābhāṃ dvibhujāṃ raktalocanām | 

raktāmbaraparīdhānāṃ raktālaṅkārabhūṣitām | 

caturvargapradāṃ saumyāṃ varadāṃ nāgaśekharām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā brahmarūpāṃ tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || aṃ || 

santaptahemavarṇābhāṃ sarvālaṅkārabhūṣitām | 

ratnayajñopavītāñca ratnākaṅkaṇarājitām | 

pūrṇendu vadanāṃ saumyāṃ turīyakarasaṃyutām | 

candrasūryyāgnirūpeṇa nayanatritayānvitām | 

sādhakābhīṣṭadāṃ nityāṃ dharmakāmārthamokṣadām | 

<p. 249> evaṃ dhyātvā brahmarūpāṃ tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || aḥ || 

javāyāvakasindūrasadṛśīṃ kāminīṃ parām | 

caturbhujāṃ trinetrāñca bāhuvallīvirājitām | 

kadambakorakākārastanayugmavirājitām | 

ratnakaṅkaṇakeyūrahāranūpurabhūṣitām | 

evaṃ kakāraṃ dhyātvā tu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || ka || 

bandhūkapuṣpasaṅkāśāṃ ratnālaṅkārabhūṣitām | 

varābhayakarāṃ nityāmīṣaddhāsyamukhīṃ parām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā khakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet | 

guṇatrayayutaṃ devi (!) pañcadevamayaṃ sadā | 

triśaktisaṃyutaṃ varṇaṃ khakāraṃ praṇamāmyaham || kha || 

dāḍimīpuṣpasaṅkāśāṃ caturbāhusamanvitām | 

raktāmbaradharāṃ nityāṃ raktālaṅkārabhūṣitām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā gakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet | 

pañcaprāṇamayaṃ varṇaṃ sarvaśaktyātmakaṃ priye (!) | 

taruṇādityasaṅkāśāṃ kuṇḍalīṃ praṇamāmyaham || ga || 

mālatīpuṣpavarṇābhāṃ ṣaḍbhujāṃ raktalocanām | 

śuklāmbaraparīdhānāṃ śuklamālyavibhūṣitām | 

tadā smeramukhīṃ ramyāṃ locanatrayarājitām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā ghakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet | 

nirguṇaṃ triguṇopetaṃ sadā trigolasaṃyutam | 

sarvagaṃ sarvadā śāntaṃ ghakāraṃ praṇamāmyaham || gha || 

dhūmravarṇāṃ mahāghorāṃ lalajjihvāṃ caturbhujām | 

pītāmbaraparīdhānāṃ sādhakābhīṣṭasiddhidām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā brahma rūpāṃ tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet | 

sarvadevamayaṃ varṇaṃ triguṇaṃ lolalocane (!) | 

pañcaprāṇamayaṃ varṇaṃ ṅakāraṃ praṇamāmyaham || ṅa || 

tuṣārakundapuṣpābhāṃ nānālaṅkārabhūṣitām | 

sadā ṣoḍaśavarṣīyāṃ varābhayakarāṃ parām | 

śuklavastrāvṛtakaṭiṃ śuklavastrottarīyiṇīm | 

varadāṃ śobhanāṃ ramyāmaṣṭabāhusamanvitām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā cakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || ca || 

dhyānamasyāḥ pravakṣyāmi dvibhujāntu trilocanām | 

pītāmbaradharāṃ nityāṃ varadāṃ bhaktavatsalām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā chakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet | 

<p. 250> tribindusahitaṃ varṇaṃ sadaiveśvarasaṃyutam | 

pītavidyullatākāraṃ chakāraṃ praṇamāmyaham || cha || 

nānālaṅkārasaṃyuktairbhujairdvāśabhiryutām | 

raktacandanadigdhāṅgīṃ citrāmbaravidhāriṇīm | 

trilocanāṃ jagaddhātrīṃ varadāṃ bhaktavatsalām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā jakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || ja || 

santaptahemavarṇābhāṃ raktāmbaravibhūṣitām | 

raktacandanaliptāṅgīṃ raktamālyavibhūṣitām | 

caturdaśabhujāṃ devīṃ ratnahārojjvalāṃ parām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā jhakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || jha || 

caturbhujā dhūmravarṇāṃ kṛṣṇāmbaravibhūṣitām | 

nānālaṅkārasaṃyuktāṃ jaṭāmukuṭarājitām | 

īṣaddhāsyamukhīṃ nityāṃ varadāṃ bhaktavatsalām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā ñakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || ña || 

mālatīkundapuṣpābhāṃ pūrṇacandranibhekṣaṇām | 

daśabāhusamāyuktāṃ sarvālaṅkārabhūṣitām | 

paramokṣapradāṃ nityāṃ sadā smeramukhīṃ parām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā ṭakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || ṭaṃ || 

pūrṇacandrānibhāṃ devīṃ vikasatpaṅkajekṣaṇām | 

sundarīṃ ṣoḍaśabhujāṃ dharmakāmārthamokṣadām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā ṭhakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || ṭha || 

javāsindūrasaṅkāśāṃ varābhayakarāṃ parām | 

trinetrāṃ varadāṃ nityāṃ paramokṣapradāyinīm | 

evaṃ dhyātvā ḍakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet | 

caturjñānamayaṃ varṇamātmāditattvasaṃyutām | 

pītāvidyullatākārāṃ ḍakāraṃ praṇamāmyaham || ḍa || 

raktotpalanibhāṃ ramyāṃ raktapaṅkajalocanām | 

aṣṭādaśabhujāṃ bhīmāṃ mahāmokṣapradāyinīm | 

evaṃ dhyātvā ḍhakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet | 

sadā triguṇasaṃyuktamāditattvamayaṃ sadā | 

raktavidyullatākāraṃ ḍhakāraṃ praṇamāmyaham || ḍha || 

dvibhujāṃ varadāṃ vandyāṃ bhaktābhīṣṭapradāyinīm | 

rājīvalocanāṃ nityāṃ dharmmakāmārthamokṣadām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā ṇakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || ṇa || 

caturbhujāṃ mahāśāntāṃ mahāmokṣapradāyinīm | 

sadā ṣoḍaśavarṣīyāṃ raktāmbaradharāṃ parām | 

<p. 251> nānālaṅkārabhūṣāṃ vā sarvasiddhipradāyinīm | 

evaṃ dhyātvā takārastu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || ta || 

nīlavarṇāṃ trinayanāṃ ṣaḍbhujāṃ varadāṃ parām | 

pītavastraparīdhānāṃ sadā siddhipradāyinīm | 

evaṃ dhyātvā thakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet | 

pañcadevamayaṃ varṇaṃ pañcaprāṇamayaṃ sadā | 

taruṇādityasaṅkāśaṃ thakāraṃ praṇamāmyaham || tha || 

caturbhujāṃ pītavastrāṃ navayauvanasaṃsthitām | 

anekaratnaghaṭitahāranūpuraśobhitām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā dakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet | 

triśaktisahitaṃ devi (!) tribindusahitaṃ sadā | 

ātmāditattvasaṃyuktaṃ dakāraṃ praṇamāmyaham || da || 

ṣaḍbhujāṃ meghavarṇāñca raktāmbaradharāṃ parām | 

varadāṃ śubhadāṃ ramyāṃ caturvargapradāyinīm | 

evaṃ dhyātvā dhakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || dha || 

dalitāñjanavarṇābhāṃ lalajjihvāṃ sulojanām | 

caturbhujāṃ cakorākṣīṃ cārucandanacarccitām | 

kṛṣṇāmbaraparīdhānāmiṣaddhāsyamukhīṃ sadā | 

evaṃ dhyātvā nakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || na || 

vicitravasanāṃ devīṃ dvibhujāṃ paṅkajekṣaṇām | 

raktacandanaliptāṅgīṃ padmamālāvibhūṣitām | 

maṇiratnādikeyūrahārakeyūravigrahām | 

caturvargapradāṃ nityāṃ nityānandamayīṃ parām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā pakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || pa || 

pralayāmbudavarṇābhāṃ lalajjihvāṃ caturbhujām | 

bhaktābhayapradāṃ nityāṃ nānālaṅkārabhūṣitām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā phakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || pha || 

nīlavarṇāṃ trinayanāṃ nīlāmbaradharāṃ parām || 

nāgahārojjvalāṃ devīṃ dvibhujāṃ padmalocanām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā bakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet | 

triśaktisahitaṃ varṇaṃ vividhāmṛtalepitām | 

svayaṃ kuṇḍalinīṃ devīṃ satataṃ praṇamāmyaham || ba || 

taḍitprabhāṃ mahādevīṃ nāgakaṅkaṇaśaubhitām | 

caturvargapradāṃ devīṃ sādhakābhīṣṭasiddhidām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā bhakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet | 

triśaktisahitaṃ varṇaṃ tribindusahitaṃ priye (!) | 

<p. 252> ātmāditattvasaṃyuktaṃ bhakāraṃ praṇamāmyaham || bha || 

kṛṣṇāṃ daśabhujāṃ bhīmāṃ pītalohitalocanām | 

kṛṣṇāmbaradharāṃ nityāṃ dharmmakāmārthamokṣadām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā makārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet | 

triśaktisahitaṃ varṇaṃ tribindusahitaṃ sadā | 

ātmāditattvasaṃyuktaṃ hṛdisthaṃ praṇamāmyaham || ma || 

dhūmravarṇāṃ mahāraudrīṃ ṣaḍbhujāṃ raktalocanām | 

raktāmbaraparīdhānāṃ nānālaṅkārabhūṣitām | 

mahāmokṣapradāṃ nityāmaṣṭasiddhipradāyinīm | 

evaṃ dhyātvā yakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet | 

triśaktisahitaṃ varṇaṃ tribindusahitaṃ sadā | 

praṇamāmi sadā varṇaṃ śaktimāmokṣamavyayam || ya || 

lalajjihvāṃ mahāraudrīṃ raktāsyāṃ raktalocanām | 

raktavarṇāmaṣṭabhujāṃ raktapuṣpopaśobhitām | 

raktamālyāmbaradharāṃ raktālaṅkārabhūṣitām | 

mahāmokṣapradāṃ nityāmaṣṭasiddhipradāyikām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā brahmarūpāṃ tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet | 

triśaktisahitaṃ devi (!) ātmāditattvasaṃyutam | 

sarvatejomayaṃ varṇaṃ satataṃ praṇamāmyaham || ra || 

caturbhujāṃ pītavastrāṃ raktapaṅkajalocanām | 

sarvadā varadāṃ bhīmāṃ sarvālaṅkārabhūṣitām | 

yogīndrasevitāṃ nityāṃ yoginīṃ yogarūpiṇīm | 

caturvargapradāṃ devīṃ nāgahāropaśobhitām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā lakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || la || 

kundapuṣpaprabhāṃ devīṃ dvibhujāṃ paṅkajekṣaṇām | 

śuklamālyāmbaradharāṃ ratnahārojjvalāṃ parām | 

sādhakābhīṣṭadāṃ siddhāṃ siddhidāṃ siddhasevitām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā vakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || va || 

caturbhujāṃ cakorākṣīṃ cārucandanacarccitām | 

śuklavarṇāṃ trinayanāṃ varadāñca śucismitām | 

ratnālaṅkārabhūṣāḍhyāṃ śvetamālyopaśobhitām | 

devavṛndairabhivandyāṃ sevitāṃ mokṣakāṅkṣibhiḥ | 

evaṃ dhyātvā śakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || śa || 

śuklāmbarāṃ śukla varṇāṃ dvibhujāṃ raktalocanām | 

śvetacandanaliptāṅgīṃ muktāhāropaśobhitām | 

<p. 253> gandharvagīyamānāñca sadānandamayīṃ parām | 

aṣṭasiddhipradāṃ nityāṃ bhaktānandavivarddhinīm | 

evaṃ dhyātvā ṣakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet | 

triśaktisahitaṃ varṇamātmāditattvasaṃyutam | 

praṇamya satataṃ devi (!) hṛdi bhāvaya sundari (!) || ṣa || 

karauṣabhūṣitāṅgīñca sāṭṭahāsāṃ digambarīm | 

asthimālyāmaṣṭabhujāṃ varadāmambujekṣaṇām | 

nāgendrahārabhūṣāḍhyāṃ jaṭāmukuṭamaṇḍitām | 

sarvasiddhipradāṃ nityāṃ dharmakāmārthamokṣadām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā sakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || sa || 

caturbhujāṃ raktavarṇāṃ śuklāmbaravibhūṣitām | 

raktālaṅkārasaṃyuktāṃ varadāṃ padmalocanām | 

īṣaddhāsyamukhīṃ lolāṃ raktacandanacarccitām | 

syāddātrīñca caturvargapradāṃ saumyāṃ manoharām | 

gandharvasiddhadevādyairdhyātāmādyāṃ sureśvarīm | 

evaṃ dhyātvā hakārantu tanmantraṃ daśadhā japet || ha || 

tanmantrapuṭitaṃ kṛtvā mūlamantraṃ tato japet || 

iti sarvavarṇaṣu jñeyam | 

anyathā niṣphalaṃ jñeyaṃ japapūjādisādhanam | 

dīkṣā ca niṣphalā jñeyā tasmāt kuryyāt prayatnataḥ | 

kṛtvā japaṃ prakurvīta yathoktaphalamāpnuyāt | 

śaive śākte vaiṣṇave ca sarvatantreṣvayaṃ vidhiḥ | 

tanmantrapuṭitaṃ mantraṃ daśadhā daśadhā japet | 

dīkṣādau guruṇā sārddhaṃ karttavyaṃ mantraśodhanam | 

anyathā niṣphalā dīkṣā japapūjādikañca yat | 

sadguruḥ saṃyato nityaṃ dhyātvābhyarcya japādikam | 

gurucaitanyamātreṇa caitanyaṃ janayettataḥ | 

saṃskārañca śodhanañca sāgamena viśeṣataḥ | 

tanmantrapuṭitaṃ kṛtvā śatamaṣṭottaraṃ japet | 

dīkṣādau guruṇā pūrvaṃ karttavyo'yaṃ kramaḥ sadā | 

pañcāśadvarṇamanunā puṭitā manavaḥ priye (!) | 

caturvargapradāḥ śāntā yathoktaphaladāḥ sadā | 

anyathā niṣphalā dīkṣā japapūjādisādhanam | 

vidhinānena hīnā yā sā dīkṣā niṣphalā bhavet | 

iha dāridryamatulamante ca nirayaṃ vrajet | 

dīkṣādau gurukarttavyakrama eva prayatnataḥ | 

<p. 254> ādyāvasāne varṇena puṭitaṃ prajapenmanum | 

śiṣyaḥ prayatnato nityaṃ kuryyādetaṃ kramaṃ priye (!) | 

pūjādau vā japādau vā anyathā viphalaṃ bhavet || 

iti varṇānāṃ dhyānam ||

eteṣāṃ mantrastu oṃ akārāya nama ityādi draṣṭavyam || 

pañcadevādivarṇāntapūjānāṃ sarvatropayogitvenādau likhitaṃ 
sāmānyārghyasthāpanamantarameva karttavyam | 

śāradāyāṃ caturthapaṭale || 

ācamya vidhinā tatra sāmānyārghyaṃ vidhāya ca | 

dvāramantrāmbubhiḥ prokṣya dvārapūjāṃ samācaret | 

sāmānyārghyasthāpanantu maṇḍapadvāryyeva | 

dvārapūjā tu arthakāṇḍe'nusandheyā ||

rudrajāmale pūrvakhaṇḍe dvitīya paṭale || 

merupṛṣṭha ṛṣiḥ proktaḥ sutalaṃ chanda ucyate | 

kūrmo devatāsanayoge viniyogaḥ prakīrttitaḥ | 

māyābījaṃ samuccāryya kūrmmāsanāya te namaḥ | 

pārṣṇighātatrayeṇaiva vārayedbhūmivighnakān | 

tālatrayeṇāntarīkṣān tathā vighnānnivārayet || 

picchilātantre pañcamapaṭale || 

saure māsītyādi prāguktam || 

viśvasāre || 

kṣetrādhipaṃ kṣetrapālaṃ gṛhadevīṃ tataḥ param | 

gandhapuṣpau dhūpadīpau naivedyañca nivedayet | 

eṣāṃ pūjāṃ vilaṅghyātha nāccayeddevatāntaram || 

śāradāyām || 

pañcagavyārghyatoyābhyāṃ prokṣayedyāgamaṇḍapam | 

catuṣpathāntāṃ tacchuddhiṃ vidadhyādvīkṣaṇādibhiḥ | 

vīkṣaṇaṃ mūlamantreṇa śareṇa prokṣaṇaṃ matam | 

tenaiva tāḍanaṃ darbhairvarmmaṇābhyukṣaṇaṃ matam | 

candanāgurukarpūrairdhūpayedantaraṃ sudhīḥ | 

vikirān vikirettatra saptajaptāccharāṇunā | 

lājacandanasiddhārthabhasmadūrvākuśākṣatāḥ | 

vikirā iti sandiṣṭāḥ sarve vighnaughanāśanāḥ | 

somaśambhostu viśeṣo rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛto yathā | 

vikirān śuddhalājān vā saptamantrābhimantritān | 

astrādbhiḥ prokṣitānetān kavacenāvaguṇṭhitān | 

nānāpraharaṇākārān vighnaughavinivārakān | 

astrajaptena darbhāṇāṃ muṣṭimāmārjjayecca tat | 

īśasya diśi bandhanyā āsanāya prakalpayet | 

<p. 255> puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā tu brāhmaṇānupaveśya ca | 

ukteṣu maṇḍaleṣvekaṃ vedikāyāṃ samālikhet | 

viśenmṛdvāsane mantrī prāṅmukho vāpyudaṅmukhaḥ | 

baddhapadmāsano maunī samāhito jitendriyaḥ | 

ṛjukāyo jitendriya iti merutantre pāṭhaḥ || 

maṅgalāṅkurapātrāṇi sthāpayeddikṣu deśikaḥ | 

maṅgalarūpāṇi yānyaṅkura pātrāṇi uktarītyā uktāni tāni dikṣu sthāpayedityarthaḥ | 

kṛtāñjalipuṭo bhūtvā vāmadakṣiṇapārśvayoḥ | 

natvā guruṃ gaṇeśānaṃ bhūtaśuddhiṃ samācaret | 

karaśuddhiṃ samāpādya kuryyāttālatrayaṃ tataḥ | 

ūrddhvorddhvamantramantreṇa digbandhamapi deśikaḥ | 

tena so'ñjalitastejo rakṣāṃ kuryyāt samantataḥ | 

ṛṣiśchando daivatāni nyasenmantrasya mantravit | 

ātmano mūrddhnivadane hṛdaye ca yathākramam || 

prapañcasāre'ṣṭamapaṭale || 

ṛṣirguratvācchirasaiva dhāryyaśchando'kṣaratvādrasanāgataṃ syāt | 

dhiyāvagantavyatayā sadaiva hṛdi praviṣṭā manudevatā ca | 

vidhāya mūlamantreṇa prāṇāyāmaṃ yathāvidhi | 

vidadhyānmātṛkānyāsaṃ mantranyāsamataḥ param | 

aṅguṣṭhādiṣvaṅgulīṣu nyasedaṅgaiḥ sajātibhiḥ | 

astraṃ tattālayornyasya kuryyāttālatrayādikam | 

diśastenaiva badhnīyācchoṭikābhiḥ samāhitaḥ | 

hṛdādiṣu ca vinyasyedaṅgamantraistataḥ sudhīḥ | 

hṛdayāya namaḥ pūrvaṃ śirase vahnivallabhā | 

śikhāyai vaṣaḍityuktvā kavacāya humīritam | 

netratrayāya vauṣaṭ syādastrāya phaḍiti kramāt | 

ṣaḍaṅgamantrānityuktān ṣaḍaṅgeṣu niyojayet | 

aṃsoruyugmayorvidvān prādakṣiṇyena deśikaḥ | 

dharmaṃ jñānañca vairāgyamaiśvaryyaṃ nyasya tu krasāt | 

mukhapārśvanābhipārśveṣvadharmādīn prakalpayet | 

dharmādayaḥ smṛtāḥ pādāḥ pīṭhagātrāṇi cāpare | 

anantaṃ hṛdaye padmamasmin sūryyendupāvakān | 

eṣu svasvakalā nyasyennāmādyākṣarapūrvakān | 

sattvādīṃstrīn guṇān nyasyettathaivātra gurūttamaḥ | 

ātmānamantarātmānaṃ paramātmānamatra tu | 

<p. 256> jñānātmānaṃ pravinyasya nyaset pīṭhamanuṃ tataḥ |

evaṃ dehamaye pīṭhe cintayediṣṭadevatām | 

mudrāṃ pradarśya vidhivadarghya sthāpanamācaret | 

śaṅkhamantrāmbunā prokṣya vāmato vahnimaṇḍale | 

sādhāraṃ sthāpayedvidvān bindusrutasudhāmayaiḥ | 

toyaiḥ sugandhipuṣpādyaiḥ pūjayettaṃ yathāvidhi | 

ādhāraṃ pāvākaṃ śaṅkhaṃ sūryyaṃ toyaṃ niśākaram |

smaredvahnyarkacandrāṇāṃ kalāstāsteṣvanukramāt | 

kalāstu prathamakāṇḍe nirūpitāḥ |

mūlamantraṃ japet sṛṣṭvā nyāse tasyāṅgamantravit | 

hṛnmantreṇābhisampūryya hastābhyāṃ chādayannapaḥ | 

japedvidvān yathānyāyaṃ deśiko devatādhiyā | 

astramantreṇa saṃrakṣya kavacenāvaguṇṭhya ca | 

dhenumudrāṃ samāpādya bodhayet svasvamudrayā | 

dakṣiṇe prokṣaṇīpātramādhāyādbhiḥ prapūrayet | 

kiñcidarghyāmbu saṃgṛhya prokṣaṇyambhasi yojayet | 

arghyasyottarataḥ kāryyaṃ pādyamācamanīyakam | 

ātmānaṃ yāgavastūni maṇḍalaṃ prokṣayedguruḥ | 

prokṣaṇīpātratoyena manunānyadapi kramāt | 

nyāsakrameṇa dehe sve dharmādīn pūjayettataḥ | 

puṣpādyaiḥ pīṭhamanvantaṃ tasmiṃśca paradevatām | 

pañcakṛtvaḥ punaḥ kuryyāt puṣpāñjalimananyadhīḥ | 

uttamāṅgahṛdādhārapādasarvāṅgake kramāt | 

vinā naivedyaṃ gandhādyairupacāraiḥ samarccayet | 

gurūpadiṣṭavidhinā śeṣamanyat samāpayet | 

sarvametat prayuñjīta prokṣaṇīsthena vāriṇā | 

visṛjya toyaṃ prokṣaṇyāḥ pūrayettat yathāpurā | 

tatastanmaṇḍalaṃ mantrī gandhādyaiḥ sādhu pūjayet | 

śālibhiḥ karṇikāmadhyamāpūryyopari taṇḍulaiḥ | 

alaṅkṛtya punasteṣu darbhānāstīryya mantravit | 

kūrccamakṣatasaṃyuktaṃ nyasetteṣāmathopari || 

merutantre caturthaprakāśe'pi || 

pūrayet karṇikāṃ pūrvaṃ śālibhistaṇḍulaistataḥ | 

darbhānnyaseduparyyeṣāṃ sākṣataṃ kūrccakaṃ tataḥ | 

chinnamūlāḥ kuśāḥ dīrghāḥ saptaviṃśatisaṃkhyakāḥ | 

brahmagranthiyutāstvagredeśe kūrcca ihocyate | 

ādhāraśaktimārabhya pīṭhamantramayaṃ yajet | 

<p. 257> adhaḥ kūrccaśilārūḍhāṃ śaraccandranibhaprabhām | 

ādhāraśaktiṃ prayajet paṅkajadvayadhāriṇīm | 

mūrdhni tasyāḥ samāsīnaṃ kūrmaṃ nīlābhamarccayet | 

ūrddhvabrahma śilāsīnamanantaṃ kundasannibham | 

yajeccakradharaṃ murdhni dhārayantaṃ vasundharām | 

tamālaśyāmalāṃ tatra nīlendīvaradhāriṇīm | 

samarccayedvasumatīṃ sphuratsāgaramekhalām | 

tasyāṃ ratnamayaṃ dvīpaṃ tasmiṃśca maṇimaṇḍapam | 

yajet kalpataruṃ tasmin sādhakābhīṣṭasiddhidam | 

adhastāt pūjayettasya vedikāṃ maṇḍalojjvalām | 

paścādabhyarccayettasyāṃ pīṭhaṃ dharmādibhiḥ punaḥ | 

raktaśyāmaharidrendranīlābhān pādarūpiṇaḥ | 

vṛṣakeśavibhūtendrarūpān dharmādikān yajet | 

gātreṣu pūjayettāṃstu nañpūrvānuktalakṣaṇān | 

āgneyādiṣu koṇeṣu dikṣu madhye'mbujaṃ yajet | 

ānandakandaṃ prathamaṃ saṃvinnālamanantaram | 

sarvatattvātmakaṃ padmamabhyarcya tadanantaram | 

mantrī prakṛtipatrāṇi vikāramayakeśarān | 

pañcāśadvarṇavījāḍhyakarṇikāṃ pūjayettataḥ | 

kalābhiḥ pūjayet sārddhaṃ tasyāṃ sūryyendupāvakān | 

praṇavasya tribhirbhāgairatha sattvādikān guṇān | 

ātmānamantarātmānaṃ paramātmānamarccayet | 

jñānātmānañca vidhivat pīṭhamantrāvasānakam | 

pīṭhaśaktīḥ keśareṣu madhye ca savarābhayāḥ | 

hemādiracitaṃ kumbhaṃ mantrādbhiḥ kṣālitāntaram || 

candanāgurukarpūradhūpitaṃ śobhanākṛtim | 

āveṣṭitāṅgaṃ nīrandhraṃ tantunā triguṇātmanā | 

arccitaṃ gandhapuṣpādyaiḥ kurccākṣatasamanvitat | 

navaratnodaraṃ mantrī sthāpayettāramuccaran | 

aikyaṃ saṅkalpya kumbhasya pīṭhasya ca vidhānavit | 

kṣīradrumakaṣāyeṇa palāśatvagbhavena vā | 

tīrthodakairvā karpūragandhapuṣpasuvāsitaiḥ | 

ātmabhedena vidhivat mātṛkāṃ pratilomataḥ | 

japenmūlamanuṃ tadvat pūjayeddevatādhiyā | 

śaṅkhekvāthāmbusampūrṇe gandhāṣṭakamabhīṣṭadam | 

viloḍya pūrayettasminnāvāhya sakalāḥ kalāḥ | 

<p. 258> daśavahneḥ kalāḥ pūrvaṃ dvādaśa dvādaśātmanaḥ | 

ṣoḍaśāpi ca somasya paścātpañcaśataṃ kalāḥ | 

japitvā pratilomena mūlamantrañca mantravit | 

samāhitena manasā dhyāyan mantrasya devatām | 

prāṇapratiṣṭhāṃ kurvīta tatra tatra vicakṣaṇaḥ | 

kalātmakaṃ śaṅkhasaṃsthaṃ kvāthaṃ kumbhe vinikṣipet | 

pāśāditryakṣarātmānte syādamuṣya padaṃ punaḥ | 

kramāt prāṇā iha prāṇāstathā jīva iha sthitaḥ | 

amuṣya sarvendriyāṇi bhūyo'muṣya padaṃ vadet | 

vāṅmanonayanaśrotraghrāṇaprāṇapadānyatha | 

paścādihāgatya sukhaṃ ciraṃ tiṣṭhantu ṭhadvayam | 

ayaṃ prāṇamanuḥ proktaḥ sarvajīvapradāyakaḥ | 

paścādaśvatthapanasacūtakomalapallavaiḥ | 

indravallīsamābaddhaiḥ suradrumadhiyā guruḥ | 

kumbhavaktraṃ pidhāyāsmiṃścaṣakaṃ sakalīkṛtam | 

saṃsthāpayet phaladhiyā vidhivat kalpaśākhinām | 

tataḥ kumbhaṃ nirmalena kṣaumayugmena veṣṭayet | 

mūlamantraṃ samuccāryya suṣumnāvartmanā sudhīḥ | 

saṃsthāpanaṃ sannidhānaṃ sannibodhamanantaram | 

sakalīkaraṇaṃ paścādvidadhyādavaguṇṭhanam | 

amṛtīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā kurvīta paramīkṛtim | 

kramādetāni kurvīta svamudrābhiḥ samāhitaḥ | 

athopacārān kurvīta mantravit svāgatādikān | 

svāgataṃ kuśalapraśnaṃ nigadedagrato guruḥ | 

pādyaṃ pādāmbuje dadyāddevasya hṛdayātmanā | 

hṛdayātmanā nama iti mantreṇa | 

arghyaṃ diśettato mūrddhni śiromantreṇa deśikaḥ | 

svadhāmantreṇa vadane dadyādācamanīyakam | 

svadhātmanā tataḥ kuryyānmadhuparkaṃ mukhāmbuje | 

tenaiva manunā dadyādadbhirācamanīyakam | 

gandhādbhiḥ kārayet snānaṃ vāsasī paridhāpayet | 

dadyādyajñopavītañca hārādyābharaṇaiḥ saha | 

nyāsakrameṇa manunā puṭitairmātṛkākṣaraiḥ | 

abhyarcya devaṃ gandhādyairaṅgādīt pūjayettataḥ | 

tatra tatra jalaṃ dayādupacārāntarāntarā | 

aṅgāni lokapālāntaṃ yajedāvaraṇānyapi | 

keśareṣvagnikoṇādi hṛdayādīni pūjayet | 

<p. 259> ṣaḍaṅgapūjādikramastu gandharvatantre caturthapaṭale ||

agnīśāsuravāyavyamadhye dikṣu ca pārvati (!) | 

ṣaḍaṅgayuvatīḥ paścāt pūjayitvā vidhānataḥ | 

netramagre diśāṃ vastraṃ dhyātavyānyaṅgadevatāḥ | 

tuṣārasphaṭikaśyāmanīlakṛṣṇāruṇārcciṣaḥ | 

varadābhayadhāriṇyaḥ pradhānato varastriyaḥ | 

paścādabhyarccanīyāḥ syuḥ kalpoktāvṛṣayaḥ kramāt | 

ante yajellokapālān mūlapāriṣadānvitān | 

hetijātyadhipopetān dikṣu pūrvāditaḥ kramāt | 

evaṃ sampūjya vidhivannaivedyāntaṃ tato guruḥ | 

dakṣiṇe sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā tatrādhāya hutāśanam | 

saṃskṛtya vidhivadvidvān vaiśvadevamakhaṃ caret | 

tatra sampūjya gandhādyairdevatāmuktavigrahām | 

tārasya vyāhṛtiṃ hatvā mūlamantreṇa mantravit | 

sarpiṣmatā pāyasena pañcaviṃśatisaṃkhyayā | 

hutvā vyāhṛtibhirbhūyo gandhādyaiḥ punararccayet | 

saṃyojayitvā pīṭhasthamūrttau vahniṃ visarjjayet | 

avaśiṣṭena haviṣā vikiret parito balim | 

devatāyāḥ pārṣadebhyo gandhapuṣpākṣatānvitam |

tato nivedyamuddhṛtya śodhayitvā sthalaṃ punaḥ |

pañcopacāraiḥ sampūjya darśayecchatracāmare | 

karpūraśakalonmiśraṃ tāmbūlañca nivedayet | 

sahasrāvṛtya sañjapya mūlamantramananyadhīḥ | 

tajjaptaṃ sarvasampattyai devatāyai nivedayet | 

tataḥ śambhordiśi gururvikiret pūrvasañcite | 

hemavastrādisaṃyuktāṃ ghargharīṃ toyapūritām | 

saṃsthāpya tasyāṃ siṃhasthāṃ khaḍgakheṭakadhāriṇīm | 

ghorarūpāṃ paścimāsyāṃ pūjayedastradevatām | 

calāsanena sampūjya tāmādāya guruḥ punaḥ | 

rakṣeti lokapālānāṃ nālamuktena vāriṇā | 

devājñāṃ śrāvayannantaḥ parivṛtte pradakṣiṇam | 

astramantraṃ samuccāryya yathāpūrvaṃ niveśayet | 

vilokya divyadṛṣṭyā taṃ taccaitanyaṃ hṛdambujāt | 

gururātmani saṃyojya kuryyādadhvaviśodhanam | 

taṃ śiṣyam | 

uktaṃ kalādhvā tattvādhvā bhuvanādhveti ca trayam | 

varṇādhvā ca padādhvā ca mantrādhvetyaparatrayam | 

nivṛttyādyāḥ kalāḥ pañca kalādhveti prakīrttitam | 

<p. 260> tattvādhvā bahudhā bhinnaḥ śaivādyāgamabhedataḥ | 

ṣaṭtriṃśacchaivatattvāni dvātriśadvaiṣṇavāni tu | 

caturviṃśatitattvāni maitrāṇi prakṛteḥ punaḥ | 

uktāni daśa tattvāni sapta ca tripadātmanaḥ | 

tattvāni śaivānyucyante śivaḥ śaktiḥ sadāśivaḥ | 

īśvaro vidyayā sārddhaṃ pañca śuddhānyamūni tu | 

māyā kālaśca niyatiḥ kālavidyā punaḥ smṛtā | 

rāgaḥ puruṣa etāni śuddhāśuddhāni sapta ca | 

prakṛtirbuddhyahaṅkārau mano jñānendriyāṇyatha | 

karmendriyāṇi tanmātrāḥ pañca bhūtāni deśikāḥ | 

etānyāhuraśuddhāni caturviṃśatimāgame | 

śaivānāmiti tattvānāṃ vibhāgo'tra pradarśitaḥ | 

jīvaḥ prāṇo dhiyaścittaṃ jñānakarmendriyāṇyatha | 

tanmātrāḥ pañca bhūtāni hṛtpadmaṃ tejasāṃ trayam | 

vāsudevādayaśceti tattvānyetāni śārṅgiṇaḥ | 

pañca bhūtāni tanmātrā indriyāṇi manastathā | 

garvo buddhiḥ pradhānañca maitrāṇīti vidurbudhāḥ | 

nivṛttādyāḥ kalāḥ pañca tato binduḥ kalāḥ punaḥ | 

nādaḥ śaktiḥ sadāpūrvaḥ śivaśca prakṛtirviduḥ | 

ātmavidyā śivaḥ paścācchivo vidyātmanā punaḥ | 

sarvatattvañca tattvāni proktāni tripadātmanaḥ | 

tattvādhvā kathito hyevaṃ tattadāgamavedibhiḥ | 

īrito bhuvanādhveti bhuvanāni manīṣibhiḥ | 

varṇādhveti vadantyarṇānādīkṣāntānmanīṣiṇaḥ | 

varṇasaṅghaḥ padādhvā syānmantrādhvā mantrarāśayaḥ | 

kramādetānadhvanaḥ ṣaṭ śodhayedgurusattamaḥ | 

padāndhunāndhuhṛdbhālamūrddhvasvapi śiśoḥ smaret || 

andhurgudasthānam || 

tatra kṛcchreṇa vidhivattat spṛśan juhuyādguruḥ | 

ācāryyakuṇḍe saṃśuddhaistilairājyapariplutaiḥ | 

śodhayāmyamumadhvānaṃ svāheti pṛthagadhvanām | 

tārādyā māhutīraṣṭau kramāttān vilayaṃ nayet | 

śive śivāttān saṃlīnān janayet sṛṣṭimārgataḥ | 

vilokayan divyadṛṣṭyā | 

tat sthitaṃ tacca caitanyaṃ punaḥ śiṣye niyojayet | 

srucā pūrṇāhutiṃ dattvā mūlamantreṇa deśikaḥ | 

<p. 261> netre śiṣyasya badhnīyānnetramantreṇa vāsasā | 

kare gṛhītvā tat śiṣyaṃ kuṇḍato maṇḍalaṃ nayet | 

tasyāñjaliṃ punaḥ puṣpaiḥ pūrayitvā yathāvidhi | 

kalase devatāprītyai kṣepayenmūlamūccaran | 

vyapohya taṃ netrabandhamāsīnaṃ darbhasaṃstare | 

ātmayāgakramādbhūyaḥ saṃhṛtyotpādya deśikaḥ | 

tattanmantroditānnyāsān kuryyāddehe śiśostadā | 

pañcopacāraiḥ kumbhasthāṃ pūjayitveṣṭadevatām | 

tasya tantroktamārgeṇa vidadhyāt sakalīkṛtim | 

maṇḍale'laṅkṛte śiṣyamanyasminnupaveśayet | 

nadatsu pañcavādyeṣu sārddhaṃ viprāśiṣā guruḥ | 

vidhivat kumbhamuddhṛtya tanmukhasthān suradrumān | 

śiśoḥ śirasi vinyasya mātṛkāṃ manasā japan | 

mūlena sādhitaistoyaiḥ siñcedrakṣārthamañjasā ||

athābhiṣekavidhiḥ || 

vāmakeśvaratantre pañcāśatpaṭale || 

abhiṣekastu dvividhaḥ śāktaśca pūrṇa eva ca | 

avadhūtena guruṇā śāktābhiṣekamācaret | 

kiñcijjalañca kumbhānāṃ brahmakumbhe niveśayet | 

ānīya brahmakalasaṃ sammukhe sthāpayedbudhaḥ | 

niruttaratantre saptamapaṭale || 

śrīśiva uvāca || 

abhiṣekaśca dvividho rājño vā jñānināmapi | 

rājābhiṣeke deveśi (!) vaidikīñca kriyāñcaret | 

jñānināmabhiṣekantu sarvatantreṣu gopitam | 

sarvaśāntikaraṃ puṇyaṃ sarvaroganivāraṇam | 

dhanadaṃ kīrttidañcaiva āyurvṛddhikaraṃ nṛṇām | 

sarvasaubhāgyajananaṃ mahāpātakanāśanam | 

sarvāśāpūrakaṃ sarvamantradoṣanivāraṇam | 

sarvārthasādhakaṃ devi (!) sarvatīrthaphalapradam | 

abhicāraharaṃ sarvagrahadoṣavināśakam | 

bhūtāveśādiśamanaṃ ḍākinīnāṃ bhayāpaham | 

tejovṛddhikaraṃ devi (!) balavṛddhikaraṃ param | 

takṣakeṇāpi daṣṭasya viṣapīḍāvināśakam | 

tejohrāse balahrāse buddhihrāse dhanakṣaye | 

strīkṛteṣvapi doṣeṣu śārīre mānase tathā | 

vikāre deśikaḥ kuryyādabhiṣekaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ | 

asaubhāgye ca nārīṇāmabhiṣekaḥ pravarttate | 

<p. 262> gurutvañca labheddevi (!) karmmaṇābhiṣekavartmanā | 

vaiṣṇavo jñānasampannaḥ śaivaścaiva kuleśvari (!) | 

abhiṣekaṃ prakurvīta śāktaśca kulabhūṣaṇaḥ | 

mantratantrañca sarveṣāmabhiṣekeṇa sidhyati | 

abhiṣekeṇa sarveṣāmadhikāro bhaveddhruvam | 

abhiṣekakṛto vipro brahmatvaṃ labhate dhruvam | 

abhiṣekakṛtaḥ kṣetrī vipradharmmatvamāgataḥ | 

vaiśyaḥ kṣatriyatāṃ yāti śūdro vaiśyatvamāgataḥ | 

abhiṣekeṇa sarveṣāṃ caturṇāṃ varṇatāṃ tyajet | 

brāhmaṇasya surāpāne brāhmaṇyaṃ tyajate kṣaṇāt | 

abhiṣekakṛte vipre surāpānaṃ vidhīyate | 

āgamaḥ pañcamo vedaḥ kaulaśca pañcamāśramaḥ | 

śivo'pi pañcamo varṇo mahāvidyāṃ japedyataḥ | 

svasvavarṇaṃ parityajya śivatvañca prajāyate | 

abhiṣekaṃ vinā naiva brāhmaṇaḥ prapibet surām | 

pragṛhya siddhavidyāñca saṅketajñastato bhavet | 

saṅketajñaḥ kulācāre tvabhiṣekaṃ samācaret | 

abhiṣekakṛto mantrī kulapūjāṃ samācaret | 

kulapūjākṛto mantrī pitṛbhūmiṃ tato vrajet | 

pitṛbhūmikṛtaṃ sthānamekākī viharettadā | 

ekākī viharedvīraḥ prāntare rātripāntare | 

tatra siddhiṃ labheddevi (!) devānāmapi durlabhām |

kulācāraṃ vinā devi (!) mantratantraṃ na sidhyati |

siddhavidyā kulācāre dravyaṃ sidhyati niścitam | 

abhiṣekasurāṃ dadyād kulakarma yuge yuge | 

surāṃ pītvā japedvidyāṃ kālādhipaviśeṣataḥ | 

vijayāñcānukalpañca surābhāve nivedayet | 

ānandena vinā bhraṃśo na ca tṛpyanti devatāḥ | 

pañcamenārcayet kālīṃ kāmākhyāyāṃ viśeṣataḥ | 

kāmākhyāyāṃ viśeṣeṇa kālikā siddhidā bhavet | 

kulācāraṃ vinā naiva kālimantraśca sidhyati | 

abhiṣekaṃ vinā devi (!) kulakarmma karoti yaḥ | 

tasya pūjādikaṃ karma abhicārāya kalpyate | 

abhiṣekaṃ vinā devi (!) siddhavidyāṃ dadāti yaḥ | 

tāvatkālaṃ vasedghore yāvaccandradivākarau | 

brahmatvañca haritvañca śivatvañca kuleśvari (!) | 

<p. 263> sarvasiddhīśvaratvañca abhiṣekeṇa jāyate | 

divyo vīraśca deveśi (!) kulabhaktiparāyaṇaḥ | 

abhiṣekañcareddevi (!) adhivāsapuraḥsaram | 

vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā śivaśaktīḥ prapūjayet | 

guruṃ sampūjya vidhivat svarṇālaṅkārabhūṣaṇaiḥ | 

tataḥ saṅkalpya vidhinā gurūṇāṃ varaṇaṃ caret | 

tataḥ pūjāṃ careddevyāḥ pañcamaiśca pṛthak pṛthak | 

praṇamya sadguruṃ devaṃ devīñca sādhakeśvaraḥ | 

gurupūjāṃ vidhāyātha devyā dhyānaparāyaṇaḥ | 

abhiṣekaṃ vidhāyātha śucau deśe ca sādhakaḥ | 

śūnyāgāre nadītīre vilvamūle tripāntare | 

mahātripāntare vāpi nirjjane pitṛkānane | 

grāme palātake vāpi parvate taṭinītaṭe | 

devatāyatane vāpi sthānañca paricintayet || 

aṣṭamapaṭale || 

śivaśaktiṃ pūjayitvā sthāpayedghaṭamuttamam | 

nātihrasvaṃ nātidīrghaṃ svarṇaraupyavinirmitam | 

viśeṣārghyaṃ svayantre vā trikoṇe vāpi vinyaset | 

gaṅgādyāḥ saritaḥ sarvāḥ samudrāśca sarāṃsi ca | 

sarve samudrāḥ saritaḥ sarāṃsi jaladā nadāḥ | 

hradāḥ prasravaṇāḥ puṇyāḥ svaḥpātālamahīgatāḥ | 

sarvatīrthāni puṇyāni ghaṭe kurvanti sannidhim | 

śrīvījena prajaptena pallavaṃ pratipādayet | 

kūrccena phaladānaṃ syāt strīvījena sthirākṛtiḥ | 

sindūraṃ vahnivījena puṣpaṃ pretena vinyaset | 

mūlena dūrvāṃ praṇavaiḥ kuryyādabhyukṣaṇaṃ tataḥ | 

huṃ phaṭ svāheti mantreṇa kuryyāddarmeṇa tāḍanam | 

vicintya devi (!) pīṭhantu tatrāvāhya prapūjayet | 

anenaiva vidhānena sarvakarmmasu sundarī (!) | 

ghaṭaṃ saṃsthāpayeddevi (!) ṣaṭkarmmasu viśeṣataḥ | 

mahāpūjāñcareddevi (!) ṣoḍaśairupacārakaiḥ | 

gurūṇāñca mahāpūjāṃ śāktīnāñca tataḥ param | 

tatpaścāt sādhakānāñca kumārīṇāñca pūjanam | 

kumārībhyo baliṃ dattvā kulajābhyo viśeṣataḥ | 

abhiṣekaṃ tato devi (!) kuryyāt svagurumārgataḥ | 

svatantroktavidhānena mantraproccāraṇena ca | 

cālayettaṃ ghaṭaṃ mantrī mantreṇānena deśikaḥ | 

<p. 264> oṃ uttiṣṭha brahmakalasa (!) devatābhīṣṭasiddhidaḥ | 

sarvatīrthāmbupūrṇena pūrayāsya manoratham | 

īśānendusmarakṣauṇī tadante bhuvaneśvarī | 

mantreṇānena vāditranirghoṣaiścālayedghaṭam | 

abhiṣiñcedguruḥ śiṣyaṃ yajamānaṃ purohitaḥ | 

siñcedaśvatthavṛkṣotthaiḥ pallavairbhūtasaṅgame | 

siñcedauḍumbarairmantradoṣe ca karavīrajaiḥ | 

yaśodhanāyustejaḥ śrīphalakāmena cūtajaiḥ | 

tulasīmamañjarībhiśca sarvapāpakṣayārthibhiḥ | 

sarvatīrthaphalāvāptyai śāktānāṃ vilvapallavaiḥ | 

abhicāre nārasiṃhairabhiṣekaḥ pravarttate || 

nārasiṃha cuniyāhāgaḍā iti yasya prasiddhiḥ || 

kuryyāddarbhairagarbhaiśca doṣeṣu svīkṛteṣvapi | 

asaubhāgye ca nārīṇāṃ dūrvābhirabhiṣecanam | 

sarvakāryyeṣu mantrāṇāṃ siddhyarthaṃ cūtapallavaiḥ | 

tasyābhiṣekamantrasya dakṣiṇāmūrttir(-)ṛṣiranuṣṭupchandaḥ śaktirdevatā 
saṅkalpasiddhiprāptaye viniyogaḥ || 

oṃ rājarājeśvarī devī bhairavī kālabhairavī | 

śmaśānabhairavī devī tripurānandabhairavī | 

trikūṭātripurā devī tathā tripurasundarī | 

tripureśī mahādevī tathā tripurakārikā | 

tripurānandinī devī tathaiva tripurātanī | 

etāstvāmabhiṣiñcantu mantrapūtena vāriṇā || 1 || 

chinnamastā mahādevī tathā caikajaṭeśvarī | 

tārā ca jayadurgā ca śūlinī bhuvaneśvarī | 

tvaritākhyā mahādevī tathaiva ca trikhaṇḍikā | 

nityā ca nityarūpā ca vajraprastariṇī tathā | 

etāstvāmabhiṣiñcantu mantrapūtena vāriṇā || 2 || 

aśvārūḍhā maheśānī tathā mahiṣamardinī | 

durgā ca vanadurgā ca śrīdurgā bhagamālinī | 

tathā bhagandarī devī bhagaklinnā tathāparā | 

sarvacakreśvarī devī tathā nīlasarasvatī | 

sarvasiddhikarī devī sarvagandharvasevitā | 

ugratārā mahādevī tathā dakṣiṇakālikā || 

etāstvāmityādi || 3 || 

kṣemaṅkarī mahākālī cāniruddhasarasvatī | 

mātaṅginī cānnaparṇā rājarājeśvarī tathā || 

<p. 265> etāstvāmityādi || 4 || 

ugracaṇḍā pracaṇḍā ca caṇḍogrā caṇḍanāyikā | 

caṇḍā caṇḍavatī caiva caṇḍarūpāticaṇḍikā | 

etāstvāmityādi || 5 || 

ugradaṃṣṭrā mahādaṃṣṭrā śubhadaṃṣṭrā kapālinī | 

bhīmanetrā viśālākṣī maṅgalā vijayā jayā || 

etāstvāmityādi || 6 || 

maṅgalā nandinī bhadrā kīrttirlakṣmīryaśasvinī | 

puṣṭirmedhā śivā sādhvī yaśaḥ śobhā jayādhṛtiḥ | 

śrīnandā ca sunandā ca nandinyānandapūjitā || 

etāstvāmityādi || 7 || 

vijayā nandinī bhadrā smṛtiḥ śāntirdhṛtiḥ kṣamā | 

siddhistuṣṭīramā puṣṭiḥ śrīrvṛddhiśca ratistathā | 

dīptiḥ kāntiryaśo lakṣmīrīśvarī buddhireva ca | 

śākrī māyāvatī brāhmī jayantī cāparājitā | 

ajitā mānavī śvetā ditiścāditireva ca | 

māyā caiva mahāmāyā mohinī kṣobhiṇī tathā | 

kamalā vimalā gaurī śaraṇyāmbudhisundarī | 

durgā kriyārundhatī ca ghaṇṭākarṇā kapālinī | 

raudrī kālī ca māyūrī trinetrā cāparājitā | 

svarūpā bahurūpā ca tathaiva vigrahātmikā | 

carcikā cāparā jyeṣṭhā tathaiva surapūjitā | 

vaivasvatī ca kaumārī tārā māheśvarī parā | 

vaiṣṇavī ca mahālakṣmīḥ kārttikī kauṣikī tathā | 

śivadūtī ca cāmuṇḍā muṇḍamālāvibhūṣitā || 

etāstvāmityādi || 8 || 

indro vahniryamaścaiva nair(-)ṛto varuṇastathā | 

pavano dhanadeśānau brahmānantau digīśvarāḥ | 

ete tvāmityādi || 9 || 

saṃvatsaraścāyanau ca bhāsāḥ pakṣau dināni ca | 

tithayaścābhiṣiñcantu mantrapūtena vāriṇā || 10 || 

raviḥ somaḥ kujaḥ saumyo guruḥ śukraḥ śanaiścaraḥ | 

rāhuḥ ketuśca satatamabhiṣiñcantu te grahāḥ || 11 || 

nakṣatraṃ karaṇaṃ yogaḥ amṛtaṃ siddhireva ca | 

dagdhaṃ pāpaṃ tathā bhadrā yogā vārāḥ kṣaṇāstathā | 

vāravelā kālavelā daṇḍā vṛddhādayastathā | 

abhiṣiñcantu satataṃ mantrapūtena vāriṇā || 12 || 

<p. 266> asitāṅgo ruruścaṇḍaḥ krodha unmattasaṃjñakaḥ | 

kapālī bhīṣaṇaścaiva saṃhāro'ṣṭau ca bhairavāḥ | 

abhiṣiñcantu ityādi || 13 || 

ḍākinīputrakāścaiva rākiṇīputrakāstathā || 

lākinīpurakāścānye kākinīputrakāḥ pare | 

śākinīputrakā bhūyo hākinīputrakāstathā | 

tataśca yakṣiṇīputrā devīputrāstataḥ param | 

mātṝṇāñca tathā putrā ūrddhvamukhyāḥ sutāśca ye | 

adhomukhyāḥ sutā ye ca unmukhyāśca sutāḥ pare || 

ete tvāmityādi || 14 || 

brahmā viṣṇuśca rudraśca īśvaraśca sadāśivaḥ | 

ete tvāmityādi || 15 || 

puruṣaḥ prakṛtiścaiva vikārāścaiva ṣoḍaśa | 

ātmāntarātmaparamajñānātmānaḥ prakīrttitāḥ | 

ātmanaśca guṇā ye tu sthūlāḥ sūkṣmāstathā pare || 

ete tvāmityādi || 16 || 

vedādibījaṃ huṃbījaṃ strībījaṃ mīnaketanam | 

śaktibījaṃ ramābījaṃ māyābījaṃ sudhākaram | 

cintāratnaṃ mahābījaṃ nārasiṃhañca śāṅkaram | 

mārttaṇḍabhairavaṃ daurgaṃ bījaṃ śrīpuruṣottamam | 

gāṇapatyañca vārāhaṃ kālībījaṃ bhayāpaham | 

ete tvāmityādi || 17 || 

gaṅgā godāvarī revā yamunā ca sarasvatī | 

ātreyī bhāratī caiva sarayūrgaṇḍakī tathā | 

karatoyā candrabhāgā śvetagaṅgā ca kauśikī | 

bhogavatī ca pātāle svarge mandākinī tathā || 

etāstvāmityādi || 18 || 

bhairavo bhīmarūpaśca śoṇo gharghara eva ca | 

sindhutoyahradāḥ pāntu tathā pātālasambhavāḥ | 

yāni kāni ca tīrthāni puṇyānyāyatanāni ca | 

tāni tvāmabhiṣiñcantvityādi || 19 || 

jambudvipādayo dvīpāḥ sāgarā lavaṇādayaḥ | 

anantādyāstathā nāgāḥ sarpā ye takṣakādayaḥ | 

ete tvāmityādi || 20 || 

ratiśca vallabhā vahnervaṣaṭkūrccamataḥ param | 

vauṣaṭkārastu phaṭkāramabhiṣiñcantu sarvadā || 21 || 

naśyantu pretakuṣmāṇḍā rākṣasā dānavāśca ye | 

piśācā guhyakā bhūtā abhiṣekeṇa tāḍitāḥ || 22 || 

<p. 267> alakṣmīḥ kālakarṇo ca pāpāni sumahānti ca | 

naśyantu cābhiṣikeṇa tārāvījena tāḍitāḥ || 23 || 

rogāḥ śokāśca daurbalyaṃ dāridryaṃ cittavikriyā | 

naśyantu cābhiṣekeṇa vāgvījenaiva tāḍitāḥ || 24 || 

lokānurāgatyāgaśca daurbhāgyamapi durvaśaḥ | 

naśyantu cābhiṣekeṇa manmathenaiva tāḍitāḥ || 25 || 

tejohrāso balahrāso buddhihrāsastathaiva ca | 

naśyantu cābhiṣekeṇa śaktivījena tāḍitāḥ || 26 || 

viṣāpamṛtyurogaśca ḍākinyādibhayaṃ tathā | 

ghorābhicārāḥ krūrāśca grahā nāgāstathā pare | 

naśyantu cābhiṣekeṇa kālīvījena tāḍitāḥ || 27 || 

naśyantu cāpadaḥ sarvāḥ sampadaḥ santu susthirāḥ | 

abhiṣekeṇa śāktena pūrṇāḥ santu manorathāḥ || 28 || 

śāradāyām || 

avaśiṣṭena toyena śiṣyamācāmayedguruḥ | 

tatastat sakalīkuryyāddevatātmānamātmavit | 

ācamya vāgyato bhūtvā niṣīdet sannidhau guroḥ | 

devatāmātmanaḥ śiṣye saṃkrāntāṃ deśikottamaḥ | 

pūjayedgandhapuṣpādyairaikyaṃ sambhāvayaṃstayoḥ || 

niruttaratantre || 

evaṃ saṃsicya śiṣyantu punaḥ pūjāṃ samācaret | 

merutantre tṛtīyaprakāśe'pi || 

atha śiṣyañcābhiṣiñcedāśusampadavāptaye | 

kumbhāsyasthasuradruśca mūrddhvanyasya śiśorguruḥ | 

atha pañcaninādaiśca viprāśīrvacanaiḥ saha | 

gṛhītvābhyarccitaṃ pūrvaṃ karakaṃ hyasrarūpiṇam | 

tattoyairabhiṣiñcettaṃ rakṣāyai deśikottamaḥ | 

śiṣyo'pi tatra sampūjya gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dadet | 

gobhūmiśiṣyasattvañca raupyaratnāni pārvati (!) | 

sarvasvaṃ vā tadarddhaṃ vā tadarddhvaṃ vāpi dakṣiṇām || 

dakṣiṇaiṣā tu mantragrahaṇānantaraṃ yadi abhiṣekaḥ kriyate tadā karttavyā | 

abhiṣekānantaraṃ cenmantragrahaṇaṃ tadā taddakṣiṇāyai vā 
aṅgībhūtābhiṣekādidakṣiṇā siddheti || 

vāmakeśvaratantre tripañcāśatpaṭale || 

abhiṣiktaḥ sādhakendraḥ kriyādakṣo bhavet priye (!) | 

śarīraśodhanaṃ devi (!) na prasidhyet kriyāṃ vinā || 

śāradāyām || 

<p. 268> dadyādvidyāntaraṃ tasmai vinītāyāmbupūrvakam | 

gurorlabdhvāṃ punarvidyāmaṣṭakṛtvo japet sudhīḥ | 

guruvidyādevatānāmaikyaṃ sambhāvayan dhiyā | 

praṇameddaṇḍavadbhūmau guruṃ taṃ devatātmakam | 

tasya pādāmbujadvandvaṃ nijamūrddhani yojayet | 

śarīramarthaṃ praṇañca sarvaṃ tasmai nivedayet | 

tataḥ prabhṛti kurvīta guroḥ priyamananyadhīḥ | 

ṛtvigbhyo dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt samagrāṃ prītamānasaḥ | 

brāhmaṇāṃstarpayet paścāt bhakṣyabhojyasadakṣiṇān | 

eṣā kriyāvatī dīkṣā proktā sarvasamṛddhidā || 

vṛhannīlatantre tṛtīyapaṭale || 

tasya chāyānusārī syānnikaṭe tridinaṃ vaset | 

nocet sañcāriṇī śaktirgurumeti na saṃśayaḥ || 

iti kriyāvatī dīkṣā ||

āṇavī dīkṣā tu daśavidhā | 

tadyathā || 

āṇavī bahudhā dīkṣā nādeyī śāmbhavī punaḥ | 

ekadhaiveti vidvadbhiḥ paṭhyate śāstrakovidaiḥ || 

rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtaṃ vacanam || 

āṇavī bahudhetyuktā tadbhedamadhunocyate | 

smārtī mānasikī yaugī cākṣuṣī spārśikī tathā | 

vācikī māntrikī hautrī śāstrī cetyabhiṣecikā | 

videśasthaṃ guruṃ smṛtvā śiṣyaṃ pāpatrayaṃ kramāt | 

viśleṣya layabhāgāṅgavidhānena pare śive | 

samyagyojanarūpairyā smārtī dīkṣeti kathyate | 

svasannidhau samāsīnamālokya manasā śuciḥ |

malatrayādupāyairyā vijñeyā sā tu mānasī || 

yogoktakramato yogī śiṣyadehaṃ praviśya tu | 

gṛhītvā tasya cātmānaṃ svadehayojanātmikā | 

yogadīkṣeti sā proktā malatrayavināśinī || 3 || 

śivo'hamiti niścitya vīkṣaṇaṃ karuṇārdrayā | 

dṛśā sā cākṣuṣī dīksā sarvapāpapraṇāśinī || 4 || 

svayaṃ paraśivo bhūtvā niḥsandigdhamanā guruḥ | 

śiṣyahastena śiṣyasya svamantraṃ mūrdhnisaṃspṛśet | 

sparśadīkṣeti sā proktā śivābhivyaktikāriṇī || 5 || 

śivahastalakṣaṇaṃ yathā somaśambhau || 

gandhairmaṇḍalakaṃ svīyairvidadhyāddakṣiṇe kare | 

<p. 269> vidhinā tvarccayeddevamitthaṃ syācchivahastakamiti | 

svīyairgandhaiḥ svasvagandhāṣṭakairityarthaḥ || 

guruvaktraṃ nijaṃ vaktraṃ vibhāvya gururādarāt | 

guruvaktraprayogeṇa divyamantrādikaṃ śiśoḥ | 

mantranyāsādibhiḥ sārddhaṃ dadyāt seyañca vācikī || 6 || 

dīpavāto yathā mantranyāsasaṃyuktavigrahaḥ | 

svayaṃ mantratanurbhūtvā saṃkramaṃ mantramādarāt | 

dadyāt śiṣyāya sā dīkṣā māntrī malavighātinī || 7 || 

kuṇḍe vā sthaiṇḍile vāpi nikṣipyāgniṃ vidhānataḥ | 

layabhāgakrameṇaiva pratyadhvānaṃ yathākramam | 

mantravarṇakalā tatra padaviṣṭapameva ca | 

saṃśodhya homarūpairyā hautrī dīkṣā samīritā || 8 || 

yogyaśiṣyāya bhaktāya śuśrūṣārccāparāya ca | 

sārddhaṃ śāstrapadā dravyā śāstrī dīkṣeti socyate || 9 || 

śivañca śivapatnīñca kumbhe sampūjya sādaram | 

śivakumbhābhiṣekātmā dīkṣā syādabhiṣecikā || 10 || 

śāradāyām || 

atha varṇātmikāṃ vakṣye dīkṣāmāgamacoditāt | 

puṃprakṛtyātmakā varṇāḥ śarīramapi tāṛśam | 

yatastasmāttanau nyasyedvarṇān śiṣyasya deśikaḥ | 

tattatsthānayutān varṇān pratilomena saṃharet | 

ājñayā devatābhāvādvividhā deśikottamaḥ | 

tadā vinīya tattoyaṃ śiṣyo divyatanurbhavet | 

paramātmani saṃyojya taccaitanyaṃ gurūttamaḥ | 

tasmādutpāditān varṇānnyasyecchiṣyatanau punaḥ | 

sṛṣṭikrameṇa vidhivaccaitanyañca niyojayet | 

jāyate devatābhāvaḥ parānandamayaḥ śiśoḥ | 

eṣā varṇamayī dīkṣā proktā jñānapradāyinī || 

kalāvatī dīkṣā tu asmadgoṣṭhīgariṣṭhakṛṣṇānandāgama(-)vāgīśena 
svakṛtatantrasāragranthe likhitā | 

saṃkṣepadīkṣāpītyato naitayoratra prayojanam || 

vedhamayīi dīkṣāpyapracaradrūpatayā na likhitā || 

anveṣakaiḥ śāradāyāṃ pañcamapaṭale anveṣyeti ||

atha kramdīkṣā || 

kāmākhyātantre dvātriṃśatpaṭale || 

devyuvāca || 

kramadīkṣāṃ brūhi nātha (!) yadi te'sti kṛpā mayi | 

kathaṃ vā kurute dīksāṃ krameṇa jagadīśvara (!) || 

<p. 270> śiva uvāca || 

śrutā dīkṣā bahuvidhā devānāṃ prītidāyikā | 

idānīṃ kathayiṣyāmi yadi kasmai na vakṣyasi | 

prakāśādbahavo doṣāḥ sarvatra gopitā mayā | 

kalau pāpasamācāre siddhirna syāt kadācana | 

siddhirna syāt siddhirna syāt kalau nānyavidhānataḥ | 

kramadīkṣāvihīnasya kalau na syāt kathañcana | 

iti jñātvā maheśāni (!) kramadīkṣāṃ samācaret | 

rasairmantrairyathā viddhamayaḥ sauvarṇatāṃ vrajet | 

kamadīkṣāprabhāveṇa tathātmā śivatāṃ labhet | 

japo devārccanavidhiḥ kāryyo dīkṣākrameṇa vai | 

upacārasahasraistu yojitāṃ bhaktisaṃyutām | 

kramadīkṣāṃ vinā devi (!) na gṛhṇanti kadācana | 

ādau kālī tatastārā sundarī tadanantaram | 

kramadīkṣeti vikhyātā sarvadā siddhikāmataḥ | 

rājyaṃ deyaṃ śiro deyaṃ na deyaṃ kramayogataḥ | 

vidhānaṃ kramataḥ siddhirabhiṣekācca pārvati (!) | 

viśuddhamātāpitṛko jitendriyaḥ sarvāgamajñaḥ paraduḥkhakātaraḥ | 

evaṃvidhāno gurureva śāntaḥ kulīnaśiṣyaḥ kramato vidhānam | 

nātra śuddhādyapekṣāsti na vā siddhādicintanam | 

na cādhikāricintā ca grahaṇe kramayogataḥ | 

ekāhe vā vatsare vā vatsarānte'pi pārvati (!) | 

tasmāt śiṣyaḥ prayatnena gṛhṇīyāt kramayogataḥ | 

gṛhītvā guruvaktrācca krameṇa pūjayecca tāḥ | 

krameṇa ca yajeddevi (!) puraścaryyākrameṇa ca | 

prathame divase śiṣyaḥ saṃyatātmā jitendriyaḥ | 

dvitīye copavāsaśca tridine dīkṣayedguruḥ | 

kulīnatvasya svīkāraṃ kārayitvā manūn vadet | 

ekākṣaraṃ tryakṣaraṃ vā dvāviṃśatyakṣaraṃ tathā | 

dvitīyāyāstathā proktaṃ tṛtīyāyāstrikūṭataḥ | 

krameṇa divase vāpi krameṇa vatsareṇa ca | 

vatsarānte tathā devi (!) krameṇa dīkṣayedguruḥ | 

grāmān dāsāṃśca dāsīśca suvarṇañca yathākramam | 

gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dadyād vibhavasyānusārataḥ | 

yadi bhāgyavaśāddevi (!) kramadīkṣā ca jāyate | 

<p. 271> tadā siddhirbhavettasya nātra kāryyā vicāraṇā | 

kramadīkṣāvihīnasya kathaṃ siddhiḥ kalau bhavet | 

sarvāśramepu bhūteṣu sarvadeveṣu suvrate (!) | 

kramaṃ vinā maheśāni (!) sarvaṃ teṣāṃ vṛthā bhavet | 

tasmāt sarvaprayatnena guruṇā dīkṣit bhavet | 

kramamūlaṃ jagat sarvaṃ kramamūlaṃ paraṃ tapaḥ | 

adīkṣitā yadi jñātā kiñcidādāya bhojayet | 

vṛthā dānaṃ bhavettasya tasmāt sarvaṃ vivarjayet | 

sadgurorāhitā dīkṣā sarvakarmāṇi kārayet || 

iti kramadīkṣā ||
	
śāktānandataraṅgiṇīdhṛtaguptadīkṣātantre || 

mṛtamapyanugaccheta vidyāmantro viśeṣataḥ | 

mana eva manuṣyasya pūrvakarmāṇi śaṃsati | 

yadi na syānmaheśāni (!) manuṣyasya kathaṃ bhavet | 

dīkṣāyāñca kathaṃ tasya mano bhavati pārvati (!) | 

tasmāttu yatnato devi (!) pūrvavidyāṃ samuddharet | 

vakulāśvatthavaṭakaṃ patraratnaṃ śṛṇu priye (!) | 

vaṭapatre maheśāni (!) śaktimantraṃ likhet priye (!) | 

aśvatthe viṣṇumantrañca vakule śivamantrakam | 

raktacandanena deveśi (!) kāśmīreṇa maheśvari (!) | 

śaktimantraṃ likheddevi (!) candane viṣṇumantrakam | 

bhasmanā śivamantrañca vilikhet parameśvari (!) | 

saptasaptakapatreṣu tattaddevatāyā mantraṃ likhedityarthaḥ || 

prāṇapratiṣṭhāṃ tanmantre kārayedyatnataḥ sudhīḥ | 

tattaddevatāyāḥ prāṇapratiṣṭhāṃ kārayet | 

yathāśaktyupacāreṇa sampūjya parameśvari (!) | 

tataḥ śiṣyo'rghyapātrañca haste kṛtvā maheśvari (!) | 

anena manunā mantrī bhāskarāya nivedayet || 

arghyadravyamāha || 

āpaḥ kṣīraṃ kuśāgrāṇi ghṛtaṃ dadhi tathā madhu | 

raktāni karavīrāṇi tathaiva raktacandanam | 

aṣṭāṅga eṣo'rghyo vai bhānave parikīrttitaḥ | 

oṃ bho deva (!) pṛthivīpāla (!) sarvaśaktisamanvitaḥ | 

mamārghyañca gṛhāṇa tvaṃ pūrvavidyāṃ prakāśaya | 

arghyaṃ dattvā namaskṛtya kṛtāñjaliḥ paṭhettataḥ || 

gāndharve || 

na dadyādbhāskarāyārghyaṃ śaṅkhatoyairmaheśvari (!) | 

<p. 272> oṃ sūryyaḥ somo yamaḥ kālo mahābhūtāni pañca vai | 

ete śubhāśubhasyeha karmmaṇo nava sākṣiṇaḥ | 

sarvadevāḥ śarīrasthā mama mantrasya sākṣiṇaḥ | 

pūrvajanmārjitāṃ vidyāṃ mama haste pradāpaya | 

paṭhitvedaṃ maheśāni (!) satvaraṃ patramuddharet | 

uddhṛtya patramekantu gurorhaste pradāpayet | 

gurustu akṣaraśreṇīmuddhṛtya parameśvari (!) | 

setuṃ dattvā maheśāni (!) tatrāṣṭaśatakaṃ japet | 

śiṣyasya mastake hastaṃ dattvā cāṣṭaśataṃ japet | 

gurustu prāṅmukho bhūtvā śiṣyaḥ pratyaṅmukhaḥ sthitaḥ || 

anyatra | 

prāṅmukho gururāsīnaḥ śiṣyaḥ pratyaṅmukhaḥ sthitaḥ | 

trivāraṃ dakṣiṇe karṇe vāmakarṇe tathā sakṛt | 

strīśūdraviṣaye kuryyādvaiparītyena cintanam | 

ācamya saṃyato bhūtvā prāṇāyāmaṃ vidhāya ca | 

aṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā ṛṣyādikasamanvitam | 

aṣṭakṛtvo japenmantraṃ vāmakarṇe sureśvari (!) | 

iyaṃ dīkṣā sarvatantre śakteryā parikīrttitā | 

gurorlabdhāṃ mahāvidyāmaṣṭottaraśataṃ japet | 

gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dadyādvittaśāṭhyaṃ na kārayet | 

gurave guruputrāya tatpatnyai vā pradāpayet || 

kulārṇave || 

guruprītisamutpanne devatā prītimāpnuyāt | 

devatāprītimāpanne mantrasiddhirbhaveddhruvam | 

patraratnaṃ pradhānena dīkṣāṃ kuryyāt kalau yuge | 

tataḥ siddho bhavenmantrī nātra kāryyā vicāraṇā | 

etajjñānaṃ vinā devi (!) dīkṣāṃ kuryyācca yo naraḥ | 

dīkṣā tu viphalā tasya ante tu narakaṃ vrajet | 

tataḥ śiṣyo maheśāni (!) praṇameddaṇḍavadbhuvi || 

tato gururvadet || 

uttiṣṭha vatsa (!) mukto'si samyagācāravān bhava | 

kīrttiḥ śrīkāntimedhāyurbalārogyaṃ sadāstu te || 

yoginīhṛdaye || 

mantraṃ dattvā guruścaiva upavāsaṃ samācaret | 

mahāndhakāranarake kṛmirbhavati nānyathā || 

rudrajāmale || 

śyāmābhayā bhairavī tārācchinnamastāsu bhairave (!) | 

mañjughoṣe tathā raudre pañcāṅgaṃ nyasyate budhaiḥ || 

tatrāpi guhyakālīviṣaye pañcāyatanadīkṣā tat puraivoktam || 

<p. 273> kalau tāntrikasaṃskāraḥ patraratnadīkṣādikañcoktam || 

prāptāksareṇedānīṃ prabalakalilakṣaṇamucyate || 

yathā mahānirvāṇatantre caturthollāse || 

yadā tu vaidikī dīkṣā dīkṣā paurāṇikī tathā | 

na sthāsyati varārohe (!) tadaiva prabalaḥ kaliḥ | 

yadā tu puṇyapāpānāṃ parīkṣā vedasambhavā | 

na sthāsyati śive (!) śānte (!) tadaiva prabalaḥ kaliḥ | 

kvacicchinnā kvacidbhinnā yadā surataṅgiṇī | 

bhaviṣyati kuleśāni (!) tadaiva prabalaḥ kaliḥ | 

yadā striyo'tidurdāntāḥ karkaśāḥ kalahe ratāḥ | 

yadā tu mlecchajātīyā rājāno dhanalolupāḥ | 

bhaviṣyanti mahāprājñe (!) tadaiva prabalaḥ kaliḥ | 

yadā tu mānavā bhūmau strījitāḥ kāmakiṅkarāḥ | 

druhyanti gurumitrādīn tadaiva prabalaḥ kaliḥ | 

yadā kṣauṇī svalpaphalā na meghāstoyavarṣiṇaḥ | 

asamyakaphalino vṛkṣāstadaiva prabalaḥ kaliḥ | 

bhrātaraḥ svajanāmātyā yadā dhanaphalecchayā | 

mithaḥ samprahariṣyanti tadaiva prabalaḥ kaliḥ | 

prakaṭe madhyamāṃsādau nindārambhavivarjite | 

gūḍhapānaṃ prakurvanti tadaiva prabalaḥ kaliḥ | 

satyatretādvāpare ca yathā madyādisevanam | 

kalāvapi tathā kuryyāt kulavartmānusārataḥ || 

iti prabalakalilakṣaṇam ||

kalerupaṃśamaprakāro'pi tatraiva || 

ye kurvanti kulācāraṃ satyapūtā jitendriyāḥ | 

vyaktācāradayāśīlā na hi tān bādhate kaliḥ || 

guruśuśrūṣaṇe yuktā bhaktā mātṛpadāmbuje | 

anuraktāḥ svadāreṣu na hi tān bādhate kaliḥ | 

satyavratāḥ satyaniṣṭhāḥ satyadharmmaparāyaṇāḥ | 

kulasādhanasatyā yena hi tān bādhate kaliḥ | 

kulamārgeṇa tattvāni śodhitāni ca yogine |

ye dadyuḥ satyavacase na hi tān bādhate kaliḥ | 

hiṃsāmātsaryyarahitā dambhadveṣavivarjjitāḥ | 

kuladharmeṣu niṣṭhā ye na hi tān bādhate kaliḥ | 

laukikaiḥ saha saṃsargaṃ vasitaṃ kulasādhuṣu | 

kurvanti kaulasevāṃ ye na hi tān bādhate kaliḥ | 

<p. 274> nānāveśadharāḥ kaulāḥ kulācāreṣu niścalāḥ | 

sevante tvāṃ kulācārairna hi tān bādhate kaliḥ | 

vījasekādisaṃskārān pitṛśrāddhādikāḥ kriyāḥ | 

ye kurvanti kulācārairna hi tān bādhate kaliḥ |

kulatattvaṃ kuladravyaṃ kulayoginameva ca | 

namaskurvanti ye bhaktyā na hi tān bādhate kaliḥ |

kauṭilyānṛtahīnānāṃ svacchānāṃ kulamārgiṇām | 

paropakāravratināṃ sādhūnāṃ kiṅkaraḥ kaliḥ ||

atha kalerguṇaḥ | 

kalerdoṣasamūhasya mahāneko guṇaḥ priye (!) | 

satyapratijñā kaulānāṃ śreyaḥ saṅkalpamātrataḥ | 

apare tu yuge devi (!) puṇyapāpañca mānasam | 

nṛṇāmāsīt kalau puṇyaṃ mānasaṃ na tu duṣkṛtam | 

kulācāravihīnā ye satataṃ satyabhāṣiṇaḥ | 

paradrohaparā ye ca te narāḥ kalikiṅkarāḥ | 

kuladharmeṣvabhaktā ye parayovitsu kāmukāḥ | 

dveṣṭāraḥ kulaniṣṭhānāṃ te jñeyāḥ kalikiṅkarāḥ | 

prakaṭe'tra kalau devi (!) sarve dharmāśca durbalāḥ | 

sthāsyatyekaṃ satyamātraṃ tasmāt satyamayo bhavet | 

satyadharmaṃ samāśritya yatkarmma kurute naraḥ | 

tadeva saphalaṃ karmma satyaṃ jānīhi suvrate (!) | 

na hi satyāt paro dharmo na pāpamanṛtātparam | 

tasmāt sarvātmanā martyaḥ satyamekaṃ samāśrayet | 

satyahīnā vṛthā pūjā satyahīno vṛthā japaḥ | 

satyamūlāḥ kriyāḥ sarvāḥ satyāt parataraṃ na hi | 

ata(-)eva mayā proktaṃ duṣkṛte prabale kalau | 

kulācāro'pi satyena karttavyo vyaktabhāvataḥ | 

gopanāddhīyate satyaṃ na guptiranṛtaṃ vinā | 

tasmāt prakāśataḥ kuryyāt kaulikaḥ kulasādhanam | 

kuladharmmasya guptyarthaṃ nānṛtyaṃ syājjugupsitam | 

yaduktaṃ kulatantreṣu na śastaṃ prabale kalau | 

kṛte dharmaścatuṣpādastretāyāṃ pādahīnakaḥ | 

dviṃpādo dvāpare devi (!) pādamātraṃ kalau yuge | 

tarāpi satyaṃ balavattapaḥ khaṇḍaṃ dayāpi ca | 

satyapāde kṛte lope dharmmalopaḥ prajāyate | 

<p. 275> tasmāt satyaṃ samāśritya sarvakarmāṇi sādhayet | 

kulācāraṃ vinā yatra nāstyupāyaḥ kuleśvari (!) | 

tatrānṛtapraveśaḥ syāt kuto niḥśreyasaṃ bhavet | 

sarvathā satyapūtātmā manmukheritavartmanā | 

sarvaṃ karma naraḥ kuryyāt svasvavarṇāśramoditam | 

dīkṣāṃ pūjāṃ japaṃ homaṃ puraścaraṇatarpaṇam | 

vratoddvāhau puṃsavanaṃ sīmantonnayanaṃ tathā | 

jātakarma tathā nāma cūḍākaraṇameva ca | 

yātrāṃ gṛhapraveśañca navavastrādidhāraṇam | 

vāpokūpataḍāgānāṃ saṃskāraṃ tithikarma ca | 

gṛhārambhaṃ pratiṣṭhāñca devatāsthāpanaṃ tathā | 

mayoktena vidhānena tatsarvaṃ sādhayennaraḥ | 

yadi manmatamutsṛjya maheśi (!) prabale kalau | 

tadā yat kriyate karma viparītāya tadbhavet || 

aṣṭamollāse || 

ato'tra kathitaṃ devi (!) dvijānāṃ prabale kalī || 

gāyatryāmadhikāro'sti nānyamantreṣu karhicit | 

dvijātīnāṃ prabhedārthaṃ śūrebhyaḥ parameśvari (!) | 

sandhyeyaṃ vaidikī proktā prāgevāhnikakarmaṇaḥ ||

viśvāso rāmaharyyāhvaya iha sakṛtī puṇyakīrttirvariṣṭhaḥ sākṣāddharmo mahātmā sma 
janayati naraḥ śrīlanārāyaṇabhau | 

śāntau dāntau sadantaḥkṛtimilitajaganmohanaprāṇakṛṣṇau śrīvāṇīśau 
pratāpānalakalitamahārātivaṃśāṭavīkau || 

śrīprāṇakṛṣṇaviśvāsaḥ sutān sapta mahodayān | 

ajījanaddevaviprabhaktānnānāguṇānvitān || 

śrīlānandamayajyeṣṭhaḥ sadānandamayaḥ kṛtī | 

śrīrāmacandrastadanu dayādharmapratāpavān | 

śrīviśvanātho viśvātmā tadanu jñānadānavān | 

śrīśambhunāthastadanu snigdhamūrtirdayāmayaḥ | 

śrīkāśīnāthaviśvāso viśvāsakulapāvanaḥ | 

śrīcandranāthastadanu candradṛkṣo guṇākāraḥ | 

ete'pyudayogino granthakāraṇāyai yataḥ sadā | 

ato nāmānyatra teṣāṃ nibadhnāmyatiyatnataḥ || 

dhīmān śrīmān bhuvanaviditastantrasārasya karttā kṛṣṇānando'jani bhuvi 
navadvīpadeśapradīpaḥ | 

kāśīnātho'bhavadiha sutastasya sārāvalīkṛt vidvān mānyo'jani tadanujo 
viśvanāthāhvayo'taḥ || 

<p. 276> gopālo nirṇayakṛtiyaśasvī madhoḥ sūdanāccābhūtāṃ putrau madhusuta itaḥ 
kālidāsaḥ prasiddhaḥ | 

tatputro'bhūdraghuvaraparo nātha ekāntabuddhistatsūnuścābhavadiha sudhīmaṅgalaḥ 
kṛṣṇapūrvaḥ || 

śrīkṛṣṇamaṅgalasuto navavallikāyāḥ śrīrāmatoṣaṇa idaṃ kṛtavān dvitīyam |

kāṇḍantu saṃskṛtiyutaṃ phalapuṣpaśobhaṃ dharmābhidhaṃ kṛtimatāṃ sakaleṣṭasiddhyai 
||

[iti śrīprāṇakṛṣṇaviśvāsānumatāyāṃ 
śrīrāmatoṣaṇavidyālaṅkārabhaṭṭācāryyacāviracitāyāṃ śrīprāṇatoṣiṇyāṃ 
dīkṣārūpaphalakathanātmakaṣaṣṭha(-)parichedānvitadharmakāṇḍaṃ samāptam] ||
